===== ./src/components/ui/item.tsx =====

import * as React from "react"
import { mergeProps } from "@base-ui/react/merge-props"
import { useRender } from "@base-ui/react/use-render"
import { cva, type VariantProps } from "class-variance-authority"
import { cn } from "#/lib/utils"

import { Separator } from "#/components/ui/separator"

function ItemGroup({ className, ...props }: React.ComponentProps<"div">) {
  return (
    <div
      role="list"
      data-slot="item-group"
      className={cn(
        "group/item-group flex w-full flex-col gap-4 has-data-[size=sm]:gap-2.5 has-data-[size=xs]:gap-2",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function ItemSeparator({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof Separator>) {
  return (
    <Separator
      data-slot="item-separator"
      orientation="horizontal"
      className={cn("my-2", className)}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

const itemVariants = cva(
  "group/item flex w-full flex-wrap items-center rounded-2xl border text-sm transition-colors duration-100 outline-none focus-visible:border-ring focus-visible:ring-[3px] focus-visible:ring-ring/50 [a]:transition-colors [a]:hover:bg-muted",
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        default: "border-transparent",
        outline: "border-border",
        muted: "border-transparent bg-muted/50",
      },
      size: {
        default: "gap-3.5 px-4 py-3.5",
        sm: "gap-3.5 px-3.5 py-3",
        xs: "gap-2.5 px-3 py-2.5 in-data-[slot=dropdown-menu-content]:p-0",
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: {
      variant: "default",
      size: "default",
    },
  }
)

function Item({
  className,
  variant = "default",
  size = "default",
  render,
  ...props
}: useRender.ComponentProps<"div"> & VariantProps<typeof itemVariants>) {
  return useRender({
    defaultTagName: "div",
    props: mergeProps<"div">(
      {
        className: cn(itemVariants({ variant, size, className })),
      },
      props
    ),
    render,
    state: {
      slot: "item",
      variant,
      size,
    },
  })
}

const itemMediaVariants = cva(
  "flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-2 group-has-data-[slot=item-description]/item:translate-y-0.5 group-has-data-[slot=item-description]/item:self-start [&_svg]:pointer-events-none",
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        default: "bg-transparent",
        icon: "[&_svg:not([class*='size-'])]:size-4",
        image:
          "size-10 overflow-hidden rounded-lg group-data-[size=sm]/item:size-8 group-data-[size=xs]/item:size-6 group-data-[size=xs]/item:rounded-md [&_img]:size-full [&_img]:object-cover",
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: {
      variant: "default",
    },
  }
)

function ItemMedia({
  className,
  variant = "default",
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<"div"> & VariantProps<typeof itemMediaVariants>) {
  return (
    <div
      data-slot="item-media"
      data-variant={variant}
      className={cn(itemMediaVariants({ variant, className }))}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function ItemContent({ className, ...props }: React.ComponentProps<"div">) {
  return (
    <div
      data-slot="item-content"
      className={cn(
        "flex flex-1 flex-col gap-1 group-data-[size=xs]/item:gap-0.5 [&+[data-slot=item-content]]:flex-none",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function ItemTitle({ className, ...props }: React.ComponentProps<"div">) {
  return (
    <div
      data-slot="item-title"
      className={cn(
        "line-clamp-1 flex w-fit items-center gap-2 text-sm leading-snug font-medium underline-offset-4",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function ItemDescription({ className, ...props }: React.ComponentProps<"p">) {
  return (
    <p
      data-slot="item-description"
      className={cn(
        "line-clamp-2 text-left text-sm font-normal text-muted-foreground [&>a]:underline [&>a]:underline-offset-4 [&>a:hover]:text-primary",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function ItemActions({ className, ...props }: React.ComponentProps<"div">) {
  return (
    <div
      data-slot="item-actions"
      className={cn("flex items-center gap-2", className)}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

export {
  Item,
  ItemMedia,
  ItemContent,
  ItemActions,
  ItemGroup,
  ItemSeparator,
  ItemTitle,
  ItemDescription,
}

import * as React from "react"

import { Button } from "#/components/ui/button"
import { HugeiconsIcon } from "@hugeicons/react"

function Sheet({ ...props }: SheetPrimitive.Root.Props) {
  return <SheetPrimitive.Root data-slot="sheet" {...props} />
}

function SheetTrigger({ ...props }: SheetPrimitive.Trigger.Props) {
  return <SheetPrimitive.Trigger data-slot="sheet-trigger" {...props} />
}

  return (
    <SheetPrimitive.Backdrop
      data-slot="sheet-overlay"
      className={cn(
        "fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/80 transition-opacity duration-150 data-ending-style:opacity-0 data-starting-style:opacity-0 supports-backdrop-filter:backdrop-blur-xs",

function SheetContent({
  className,

  side = "right",

  ...props
}: SheetPrimitive.Popup.Props & {
  side?: "top" | "right" | "bottom" | "left"
  showCloseButton?: boolean
}) {
  return (
    <SheetPortal>
      <SheetOverlay />
      <SheetPrimitive.Popup
        data-slot="sheet-content"
        data-side={side}
        className={cn(
          "fixed z-50 flex flex-col bg-popover bg-clip-padding text-sm text-popover-foreground shadow-lg transition duration-200 ease-in-out data-ending-style:opacity-0 data-starting-style:opacity-0 data-[side=bottom]:inset-x-0 data-[side=bottom]:bottom-0 data-[side=bottom]:h-auto data-[side=bottom]:border-t data-[side=bottom]:data-ending-style:translate-y-10 data-[side=bottom]:data-starting-style:translate-y-10 data-[side=left]:inset-y-0 data-[side=left]:left-0 data-[side=left]:h-full data-[side=left]:w-3/4 data-[side=left]:border-r data-[side=left]:data-ending-style:-translate-x-10 data-[side=left]:data-starting-style:-translate-x-10 data-[side=right]:inset-y-0 data-[side=right]:right-0 data-[side=right]:h-full data-[side=right]:w-3/4 data-[side=right]:border-l data-[side=right]:data-ending-style:translate-x-10 data-[side=right]:data-starting-style:translate-x-10 data-[side=top]:inset-x-0 data-[side=top]:top-0 data-[side=top]:h-auto data-[side=top]:border-b data-[side=top]:data-ending-style:-translate-y-10 data-[side=top]:data-starting-style:-translate-y-10 data-[side=left]:sm:max-w-sm data-[side=right]:sm:max-w-sm",
          className
        )}
        {...props}

===== ./src/components/ui/slider.tsx =====

import { Slider as SliderPrimitive } from "@base-ui/react/slider"
import { cn } from "#/lib/utils"

function Slider({
  className,
  defaultValue,
  value,
  min = 0,
  max = 100,
  ...props
}: SliderPrimitive.Root.Props) {
  const _values = Array.isArray(value)
    ? value
    : Array.isArray(defaultValue)
      ? defaultValue
      : [min, max]

  return (
    <SliderPrimitive.Root
      className={cn("data-horizontal:w-full data-vertical:h-full", className)}
      data-slot="slider"
      defaultValue={defaultValue}
      value={value}
      min={min}
      max={max}
      thumbAlignment="edge"
      {...props}
    >
      <SliderPrimitive.Control className="relative flex w-full touch-none items-center select-none data-disabled:opacity-50 data-vertical:h-full data-vertical:min-h-40 data-vertical:w-auto data-vertical:flex-col">
        <SliderPrimitive.Track
          data-slot="slider-track"
          className="relative grow overflow-hidden rounded-4xl bg-muted select-none data-horizontal:h-3 data-horizontal:w-full data-vertical:h-full data-vertical:w-3"
        >
          <SliderPrimitive.Indicator
            data-slot="slider-range"
            className="bg-primary select-none data-horizontal:h-full data-vertical:w-full"
          />
        </SliderPrimitive.Track>
        {Array.from({ length: _values.length }, (_, index) => (
          <SliderPrimitive.Thumb
            data-slot="slider-thumb"
            key={index}
            className="block size-4 shrink-0 rounded-4xl border border-primary bg-white shadow-sm ring-ring/50 transition-colors select-none hover:ring-4 focus-visible:ring-4 focus-visible:outline-hidden disabled:pointer-events-none disabled:opacity-50"
          />
        ))}
      </SliderPrimitive.Control>
    </SliderPrimitive.Root>
  )
}

export { Slider }


===== ./src/components/ui/sonner.tsx =====

import { Toaster as Sonner, type ToasterProps } from "sonner"
import { HugeiconsIcon } from "@hugeicons/react"
import { CheckmarkCircle02Icon, InformationCircleIcon, Alert02Icon, MultiplicationSignCircleIcon, Loading03Icon } from "@hugeicons/core-free-icons"

const Toaster = ({ ...props }: ToasterProps) => {
  return (
    <Sonner
      theme="light"
      className="toaster group"
      icons={{
        success: (
          <HugeiconsIcon icon={CheckmarkCircle02Icon} strokeWidth={2} className="size-4" />
        ),
        info: (
          <HugeiconsIcon icon={InformationCircleIcon} strokeWidth={2} className="size-4" />
        ),
        warning: (
          <HugeiconsIcon icon={Alert02Icon} strokeWidth={2} className="size-4" />
        ),
        error: (
          <HugeiconsIcon icon={MultiplicationSignCircleIcon} strokeWidth={2} className="size-4" />
        ),
        loading: (
          <HugeiconsIcon icon={Loading03Icon} strokeWidth={2} className="size-4 animate-spin" />
        ),
      }}
      style={
        {
          "--normal-bg": "var(--popover)",
          "--normal-text": "var(--popover-foreground)",
          "--normal-border": "var(--border)",
          "--border-radius": "var(--radius)",
        } as React.CSSProperties
      }
      toastOptions={{
        classNames: {
          toast: "cn-toast",
        },
      }}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

export { Toaster }

import { Tabs as TabsPrimitive } from "@base-ui/react/tabs"

function Tabs({

}: TabsPrimitive.Root.Props) {

      data-orientation={orientation}

      )}

===== ./src/components/ui/toggle-group.tsx =====

import * as React from "react"
import { Toggle as TogglePrimitive } from "@base-ui/react/toggle"
import { ToggleGroup as ToggleGroupPrimitive } from "@base-ui/react/toggle-group"
import { type VariantProps } from "class-variance-authority"
import { cn } from "#/lib/utils"

import { toggleVariants } from "#/components/ui/toggle"

const ToggleGroupContext = React.createContext<
  VariantProps<typeof toggleVariants> & {
    spacing?: number
    orientation?: "horizontal" | "vertical"
  }
>({
  size: "default",
  variant: "default",
  spacing: 2,
  orientation: "horizontal",
})

function ToggleGroup({
  className,
  variant,
  size,
  spacing = 2,
  orientation = "horizontal",
  children,
  ...props
}: ToggleGroupPrimitive.Props &
  VariantProps<typeof toggleVariants> & {
    spacing?: number
    orientation?: "horizontal" | "vertical"
  }) {
  return (
    <ToggleGroupPrimitive
      data-slot="toggle-group"
      data-variant={variant}
      data-size={size}
      data-spacing={spacing}
      data-orientation={orientation}
      style={{ "--gap": spacing } as React.CSSProperties}
      className={cn(
        "group/toggle-group flex w-fit flex-row items-center gap-[--spacing(var(--gap))] data-[spacing=0]:data-[variant=outline]:rounded-4xl data-vertical:flex-col data-vertical:items-stretch",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    >
      <ToggleGroupContext.Provider
        value={{ variant, size, spacing, orientation }}
      >
        {children}
      </ToggleGroupContext.Provider>
    </ToggleGroupPrimitive>
  )
}

function ToggleGroupItem({
  className,
  children,
  variant = "default",
  size = "default",
  ...props
}: TogglePrimitive.Props & VariantProps<typeof toggleVariants>) {
  const context = React.useContext(ToggleGroupContext)

  return (
    <TogglePrimitive
      data-slot="toggle-group-item"
      data-variant={context.variant || variant}
      data-size={context.size || size}
      data-spacing={context.spacing}
      className={cn(
        "shrink-0 group-data-[spacing=0]/toggle-group:rounded-none group-data-[spacing=0]/toggle-group:px-3 group-data-[spacing=0]/toggle-group:shadow-none focus:z-10 focus-visible:z-10 group-data-[spacing=0]/toggle-group:has-data-[icon=inline-end]:pr-2.5 group-data-[spacing=0]/toggle-group:has-data-[icon=inline-start]:pl-2.5 group-data-horizontal/toggle-group:data-[spacing=0]:first:rounded-l-3xl group-data-vertical/toggle-group:data-[spacing=0]:first:rounded-t-3xl group-data-horizontal/toggle-group:data-[spacing=0]:last:rounded-r-3xl group-data-vertical/toggle-group:data-[spacing=0]:last:rounded-b-3xl data-[state=on]:bg-muted group-data-horizontal/toggle-group:data-[spacing=0]:data-[variant=outline]:border-l-0 group-data-vertical/toggle-group:data-[spacing=0]:data-[variant=outline]:border-t-0 group-data-horizontal/toggle-group:data-[spacing=0]:data-[variant=outline]:first:border-l group-data-vertical/toggle-group:data-[spacing=0]:data-[variant=outline]:first:border-t",
        toggleVariants({
          variant: context.variant || variant,
          size: context.size || size,
        }),
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    >
      {children}
    </TogglePrimitive>
  )
}

export { ToggleGroup, ToggleGroupItem }


===== ./src/components/ui/toggle.tsx =====

import { Toggle as TogglePrimitive } from "@base-ui/react/toggle"
import { cva, type VariantProps } from "class-variance-authority"
import { cn } from "#/lib/utils"

const toggleVariants = cva(
  "group/toggle inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1 rounded-4xl text-sm font-medium whitespace-nowrap transition-colors outline-none hover:bg-muted hover:text-foreground focus-visible:border-ring focus-visible:ring-[3px] focus-visible:ring-ring/50 disabled:pointer-events-none disabled:opacity-50 aria-invalid:border-destructive aria-invalid:ring-destructive/20 aria-pressed:bg-muted dark:aria-invalid:ring-destructive/40 [&_svg]:pointer-events-none [&_svg]:shrink-0 [&_svg:not([class*='size-'])]:size-4",
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        default: "bg-transparent",
        outline: "border border-input bg-transparent hover:bg-muted",
      },
      size: {
        default:
          "h-9 min-w-9 px-3 has-data-[icon=inline-end]:pr-2.5 has-data-[icon=inline-start]:pl-2.5",
        sm: "h-8 min-w-8 px-3 has-data-[icon=inline-end]:pr-2 has-data-[icon=inline-start]:pl-2",
        lg: "h-10 min-w-10 px-4 has-data-[icon=inline-end]:pr-3 has-data-[icon=inline-start]:pl-3",
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: {
      variant: "default",
      size: "default",
    },
  }
)

function Toggle({
  className,
  variant = "default",
  size = "default",
  ...props
}: TogglePrimitive.Props & VariantProps<typeof toggleVariants>) {
  return (
    <TogglePrimitive
      data-slot="toggle"
      className={cn(toggleVariants({ variant, size, className }))}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

export { Toggle, toggleVariants }


===== ./src/data/catalog.ts =====

/**
 * Single source of truth for the storefront taxonomy.
 *
 * The slug tuples feed the Postgres enums, the zod schemas and the routes.
 * Adding a department or category here also needs a migration
 * (`pnpm db:generate && pnpm db:migrate`) and an entry in `tones` in
 * `components/home/department-tiles.tsx` (a new department won't compile
 * without one).
 */
export const departmentSlugs = ["men", "women", "kids"] as const;
export type Department = (typeof departmentSlugs)[number];

export const categorySlugs = ["shoes", "clothing"] as const;
export type Category = (typeof categorySlugs)[number];

export const departments = {
	men: { label: "Men" },
	women: { label: "Women" },
	kids: { label: "Kids" },
} satisfies Record<Department, { label: string }>;

export const categories = {
	shoes: { label: "Shoes" },
	clothing: { label: "Clothing" },
} satisfies Record<Category, { label: string }>;


===== ./src/data/site.ts =====

/** Storefront-wide settings and copy. Everything market- or brand-specific lives here. */
export const site = {
	name: "2o",

	/** ISO 4217 code used when a product or cart has no currency of its own. */
	currency: "GEL",

	/** IANA zone the store operates in; times shown to shoppers use it. */
	timezone: "Asia/Tbilisi",

	/** When the daily price check is scheduled, in `site.timezone`. Copy only; the last real run comes from the stats. */
	priceCheckTime: "08:00",

	delivery: {
		region: "Georgia",
		/** Free-delivery threshold in minor units (tetri), in `site.currency`. */
		freeFrom: 10_000,
	},

	tagline: "Big brands. Small prices.",
	description:
		"Daily deals on shoes and clothing for men, women and kids, checked across wholesale stores.",
	pitch:
		"We check wholesale stores every day and show you the cheapest deals on shoes and clothing for the whole family.",
	heroPitch:
		"Shoes and clothing for the whole family, at the lowest price we found this morning.",
};

===== ./src/db/auth-schema.ts =====

import { defineRelationsPart } from "drizzle-orm";
import { boolean, index, pgTable, text, timestamp } from "drizzle-orm/pg-core";

export const user = pgTable("user", {
	id: text("id").primaryKey(),
	name: text("name").notNull(),
	email: text("email").notNull().unique(),
	emailVerified: boolean("email_verified").default(false).notNull(),
	image: text("image"),
	createdAt: timestamp("created_at").defaultNow().notNull(),
	updatedAt: timestamp("updated_at")
		.defaultNow()
		.$onUpdate(() => /* @__PURE__ */ new Date())
		.notNull(),
});

export const account = pgTable(
	"account",
	{
		id: text("id").primaryKey(),
		accountId: text("account_id").notNull(),
		providerId: text("provider_id").notNull(),
		userId: text("user_id")
			.notNull()
			.references(() => user.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
		accessToken: text("access_token"),
		refreshToken: text("refresh_token"),
		idToken: text("id_token"),
		accessTokenExpiresAt: timestamp("access_token_expires_at"),
		refreshTokenExpiresAt: timestamp("refresh_token_expires_at"),
		scope: text("scope"),
		password: text("password"),
		createdAt: timestamp("created_at").defaultNow().notNull(),
		updatedAt: timestamp("updated_at")
			.$onUpdate(() => /* @__PURE__ */ new Date())
			.notNull(),
	},
	(table) => [index("account_userId_idx").on(table.userId)],
);

export const authRelations = defineRelationsPart({ user, account }, (r) => ({
	user: {
		accounts: r.many.account({
			from: r.user.id,
			to: r.account.userId,
		}),
	},
	account: {
		user: r.one.user({
			from: r.account.userId,
			to: r.user.id,
		}),
	},
}));


===== ./src/db/connection.test.ts =====

import type { PeerCertificate } from "node:tls";
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";

afterEach(() => {
	vi.unstubAllEnvs();
	vi.resetModules();
});

async function connection(ssl: string, servername = "") {
	vi.stubEnv("DB_PASSWORD", "test");
	vi.stubEnv("DB_SSL", ssl);
	vi.stubEnv("DB_SSL_SERVERNAME", servername);
	return (await import("./connection")).dbConnection;
}

describe("database TLS", () => {
	it("respects an explicit opt-out in production", async () => {
		vi.stubEnv("NODE_ENV", "production");
		expect((await connection("false")).ssl).toBe(false);
	});

	it("verifies the configured certificate name when connecting through Docker", async () => {
		vi.stubEnv("NODE_ENV", "development");
		const { ssl } = await connection("true", "db.example.com");
		expect(ssl).toMatchObject({ rejectUnauthorized: true });
		if (!ssl) throw new Error("TLS should be enabled");
		const certificate = {
			subjectaltname: "DNS:db.example.com",
		} as PeerCertificate;
		expect(ssl.checkServerIdentity("db", certificate)).toBeUndefined();
		expect(
			ssl.checkServerIdentity("db", {
				subjectaltname: "DNS:other.example.com",
			} as PeerCertificate),
		).toBeInstanceOf(Error);
	});

	it("verifies the connection hostname when no override is configured", async () => {
		const { ssl } = await connection("true");
		if (!ssl) throw new Error("TLS should be enabled");
		const certificate = { subjectaltname: "DNS:db" } as PeerCertificate;
		expect(ssl.checkServerIdentity("db", certificate)).toBeUndefined();
		expect(ssl.checkServerIdentity("other", certificate)).toBeInstanceOf(Error);
	});
});


===== ./src/db/connection.ts =====

import { checkServerIdentity } from "node:tls";
import type { PoolConfig } from "pg";
import { z } from "zod";

/**
 * Database credentials, parsed on their own so tooling that only needs the
 * database (drizzle-kit, the migrate container) doesn't also require every
 * application secret.
 */
const dbEnv = z
	.object({
		DB_HOST: z.string().min(1).default("localhost"),
		DB_PORT: z.coerce.number().int().positive().default(5432),
		DB_USER: z.string().min(1).default("root"),
		DB_PASSWORD: z.string(),
		DB_NAME: z.string().min(1).default("2o"),
		DB_SSL: z.enum(["true", "false"]).default("false"),
		DB_SSL_SERVERNAME: z.string().optional(),
	})
	.parse(process.env);

export const dbConnection = {
	host: dbEnv.DB_HOST,
	port: dbEnv.DB_PORT,
	user: dbEnv.DB_USER,
	password: dbEnv.DB_PASSWORD,
	database: dbEnv.DB_NAME,
	max: 20,
	idleTimeoutMillis: 30_000,
	connectionTimeoutMillis: 5_000,
	maxLifetimeSeconds: 1_800,
	ssl: dbEnv.DB_SSL === "true" && {
		rejectUnauthorized: true,
		checkServerIdentity: (hostname, cert) =>
			checkServerIdentity(dbEnv.DB_SSL_SERVERNAME || hostname, cert),
	},
} satisfies PoolConfig;

import { dbConnection } from "./connection";
import { relations } from "./schema";

export const db = drizzle({ connection: dbConnection, relations });


===== ./src/db/predicates.ts =====

import { and, eq, gt } from "drizzle-orm";
import { skus } from "./schema";

/**
 * An in-stock SKU with a real discount. Uses the generated `skus.discount`
 * column, so the predicate matches the partial `skus_deals_idx` index.
 */
export const onSale = and(eq(skus.inStock, true), gt(skus.discount, 0));


===== ./src/db/schema.ts =====

import { defineRelations, type SQL, sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import {
	boolean,
	index,
	integer,
	jsonb,
	pgEnum,
	pgTable,
	text,
	timestamp,
	unique,
	uniqueIndex,
	uuid,
} from "drizzle-orm/pg-core";
import { categorySlugs, departmentSlugs } from "#/data/catalog";
import { site } from "#/data/site";
import { account, user } from "#/db/auth-schema";

export * from "./auth-schema";

export const departmentEnum = pgEnum("department", departmentSlugs);
export const categoryEnum = pgEnum("category", categorySlugs);
export const orderStatusEnum = pgEnum("order_status", [
	"pending",
	"paid",
	"shipped",
	"delivered",
	"cancelled",
]);

export type ShippingAddress = {
	fullName: string;
	phone: string;
	line1: string;
	line2?: string;
	city: string;
	postalCode?: string;
	country: string;
};

const timestamps = {
	createdAt: timestamp("created_at").defaultNow().notNull(),
	updatedAt: timestamp("updated_at")
		.defaultNow()
		.$onUpdate(() => new Date())
		.notNull(),
};

export const product = pgTable(
	"product",
	{
		id: uuid("id").primaryKey().defaultRandom(),
		url: text("url").notNull().unique(),
		slug: text("slug").notNull().unique(),
		name: text("name").notNull(),
		brand: text("brand"),
		description: text("description"),
		department: departmentEnum("department").notNull(),
		category: categoryEnum("category").notNull(),
		warehouse: text("warehouse")
			.generatedAlwaysAs(
				(): SQL =>
					sql`regexp_replace(split_part(${product.url}, '/', 3), '^www\\.', '')`,
			)
			.notNull(),
		currency: text("currency").notNull().default(site.currency),
		lastSeenAt: timestamp("last_seen_at").defaultNow().notNull(),
		...timestamps,
	},
	(t) => [index("product_listing_idx").on(t.department, t.category)],
);

export const skus = pgTable(
	"skus",
	{
		id: uuid("id").primaryKey().defaultRandom(),
		productId: uuid("product_id")
			.notNull()
			.references(() => product.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
		color: text("color").notNull(),
		size: text("size").notNull(),
		imageUrl: text("image_url"),
		price: integer("price").notNull(),
		originalPrice: integer("original_price"),
		discount: integer("discount")
			.generatedAlwaysAs(
				(): SQL => sql`
					case when ${skus.originalPrice} > ${skus.price}
					then round((${skus.originalPrice} - ${skus.price}) * 100.0 / ${skus.originalPrice})::int
					else 0 end
				`,
			)
			.notNull(),
		inStock: boolean("in_stock").notNull().default(true),
		...timestamps,
	},
	(t) => [
		unique("skus_product_color_size_uq").on(t.productId, t.color, t.size),
		index("skus_product_price_idx").on(t.productId, t.price),
		index("skus_deals_idx")
			.on(t.productId, t.discount.desc(), t.id)
			.where(sql`${t.inStock} and ${t.discount} > 0`),
	],
);

export const orders = pgTable(
	"orders",
	{
		id: uuid("id").primaryKey().defaultRandom(),
		userId: text("user_id")
			.notNull()
			.references(() => user.id, { onDelete: "restrict" }),
		status: orderStatusEnum("status").notNull().default("pending"),
		total: integer("total").notNull(),
		currency: text("currency").notNull(),
		shippingAddress: jsonb("shipping_address")
			.$type<ShippingAddress>()
			.notNull(),
		...timestamps,
	},
	(t) => [index("orders_user_idx").on(t.userId)],
);

export const orderItem = pgTable(
	"order_item",
	{
		id: uuid("id").primaryKey().defaultRandom(),
		orderId: uuid("order_id")
			.notNull()
			.references(() => orders.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
		skuId: uuid("sku_id").references(() => skus.id, {
			onDelete: "set null",
		}),
		name: text("name").notNull(),
		price: integer("price").notNull(),
		quantity: integer("quantity").notNull().default(1),
		color: text("color"),
		size: text("size"),
		imageUrl: text("image_url"),
	},
	(t) => [index("order_item_order_idx").on(t.orderId)],
);

export const address = pgTable(
	"address",
	{
		id: uuid("id").primaryKey().defaultRandom(),
		userId: text("user_id")
			.notNull()
			.references(() => user.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
		fullName: text("full_name").notNull(),
		phone: text("phone").notNull(),
		line1: text("line1").notNull(),
		line2: text("line2"),
		city: text("city").notNull(),
		postalCode: text("postal_code"),
		country: text("country").notNull().default(site.delivery.region),
		isPrimary: boolean("is_primary").notNull().default(false),
		...timestamps,
	},
	(t) => [
		index("address_user_idx").on(t.userId),
		// At most one primary address per user, enforced by the database.
		uniqueIndex("address_primary_uq").on(t.userId).where(sql`${t.isPrimary}`),
	],
);

export const relations = defineRelations(
	{ user, account, product, skus, orders, orderItem, address },
	(r) => ({
		user: {
			accounts: r.many.account({ from: r.user.id, to: r.account.userId }),
			orders: r.many.orders({ from: r.user.id, to: r.orders.userId }),
			addresses: r.many.address({ from: r.user.id, to: r.address.userId }),
		},
		address: {
			user: r.one.user({ from: r.address.userId, to: r.user.id }),
		},
		account: {
			user: r.one.user({ from: r.account.userId, to: r.user.id }),
		},
		product: {
			skus: r.many.skus({ from: r.product.id, to: r.skus.productId }),
		},
		skus: {
			product: r.one.product({ from: r.skus.productId, to: r.product.id }),
		},
		orders: {
			user: r.one.user({ from: r.orders.userId, to: r.user.id }),
			items: r.many.orderItem({ from: r.orders.id, to: r.orderItem.orderId }),
		},
		orderItem: {
			order: r.one.orders({ from: r.orderItem.orderId, to: r.orders.id }),
			sku: r.one.skus({ from: r.orderItem.skuId, to: r.skus.id }),
		},
	}),
);

===== ./src/features/addresses/address.format.test.ts =====

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { formatAddress } from "./address.format";

const row = {
	fullName: "Nino Beridze",
	phone: "+995 555 12 34 56",
	line1: "12 Rustaveli Ave",
	line2: "Apt 4",
	city: "Tbilisi",
	postalCode: "0108",
	country: "Georgia",
};

describe("formatAddress", () => {
	it("joins the street lines and the locality", () => {
		expect(formatAddress(row)).toEqual({
			street: "12 Rustaveli Ave, Apt 4",
			locality: "0108 Tbilisi, Georgia",
		});
	});

	it("skips what is missing without leaving stray separators", () => {
		expect(formatAddress({ ...row, line2: null, postalCode: null })).toEqual({
			street: "12 Rustaveli Ave",
			locality: "Tbilisi, Georgia",
		});
	});
});


===== ./src/features/addresses/address.format.ts =====

import type { Address } from "./address.functions";

/** The two lines an address is shown on: street and apartment, then postcode, city and country. */
export function formatAddress(
	address: Pick<Address, "line1" | "line2" | "city" | "postalCode" | "country">,
) {
	const street = [address.line1, address.line2].filter(Boolean).join(", ");
	const city = [address.postalCode, address.city].filter(Boolean).join(" ");
	return {
		street,
		locality: [city, address.country].filter(Boolean).join(", "),
	};
}


===== ./src/features/addresses/address.functions.ts =====

import { createServerFn } from "@tanstack/react-start";
import { z } from "zod";
import { requireUserId } from "#/features/auth/session.server";
import { addressSchema } from "./address.schema";
import { AddressError, addresses } from "./address.server";

const idInput = z.object({ id: z.uuid() });

/**
 * Runs a handler as the signed-in user. Messages from `AddressError` reach the
 * shopper; anything else (a database error, say) is logged and replaced, so
 * query text never leaks into the form.
 */
async function run<T>(task: (userId: string) => Promise<T>) {
	const userId = await requireUserId();
	try {
		return await task(userId);
	} catch (error) {
		if (error instanceof AddressError) throw error;
		console.error("Address request failed:", error);
		throw new Error("Something went wrong. Please try again.");
	}
}

export const listAddressesFn = createServerFn({ method: "GET" }).handler(() =>
	run((userId) => addresses.list(userId)),
);

export const createAddressFn = createServerFn({ method: "POST" })
	.validator(addressSchema)
	.handler(({ data }) => run((userId) => addresses.create(userId, data)));

export const updateAddressFn = createServerFn({ method: "POST" })
	.validator(idInput.extend({ address: addressSchema }))
	.handler(({ data }) =>
		run((userId) => addresses.update(userId, data.id, data.address)),
	);

export const setPrimaryAddressFn = createServerFn({ method: "POST" })
	.validator(idInput)
	.handler(({ data }) =>
		run((userId) => addresses.setPrimary(userId, data.id)),
	);

export const deleteAddressFn = createServerFn({ method: "POST" })
	.validator(idInput)
	.handler(({ data }) => run((userId) => addresses.remove(userId, data.id)));

/** One saved address, as the account settings list it. */
export type Address = Awaited<ReturnType<typeof listAddressesFn>>[number];


===== ./src/features/addresses/address.queries.test.ts =====

import { QueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { addressesQuery } from "./address.queries";

vi.mock("./address.functions", () => ({ listAddressesFn: vi.fn() }));

it("keeps cached addresses separate when the signed-in account changes", () => {
	const client = new QueryClient();
	const addresses = [
		{
			id: "private-address",
			fullName: "Nino Beridze",
			phone: "+995555123456",
			line1: "12 Rustaveli Ave",
			line2: null,
			city: "Tbilisi",
			postalCode: null,
			country: "Georgia",
			isPrimary: true,
		},
	];
	client.setQueryData(addressesQuery("first-user").queryKey, addresses);

	expect(client.getQueryData(addressesQuery("first-user").queryKey)).toEqual(
		addresses,
	);
	expect(
		client.getQueryData(addressesQuery("second-user").queryKey),
	).toBeUndefined();
	expect(
		client.getQueryData(addressesQuery(undefined).queryKey),
	).toBeUndefined();
	expect(addressesQuery(undefined).enabled).toBe(false);
	client.clear();
});


===== ./src/features/addresses/address.queries.ts =====

import { queryOptions } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { listAddressesFn } from "./address.functions";

export const addressQueryKey = ["addresses"] as const;

export const addressesQuery = (userId: string | undefined) =>
	queryOptions({
		queryKey: [...addressQueryKey, userId],
		queryFn: ({ signal }) => listAddressesFn({ signal }),
		enabled: Boolean(userId),
	});


===== ./src/features/addresses/address.schema.test.ts =====

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { addressSchema } from "./address.schema";

const valid = {
	fullName: "Nino Beridze",
	phone: "+995 555 12 34 56",
	line1: "12 Rustaveli Ave",
	line2: "",
	city: "Tbilisi",
	postalCode: "",
	isPrimary: false,
};

const messages = (input: unknown) =>
	addressSchema.safeParse(input).error?.issues.map((issue) => issue.message);

describe("addressSchema", () => {
	it("accepts a complete address and leaves the optional fields empty", () => {
		expect(addressSchema.parse(valid)).toEqual(valid);
	});

	it("trims every text field", () => {
		const parsed = addressSchema.parse({
			...valid,
			fullName: "  Nino Beridze ",
			city: " Tbilisi  ",
			line2: "  Apt 4 ",
		});
		expect(parsed).toMatchObject({
			fullName: "Nino Beridze",
			city: "Tbilisi",
			line2: "Apt 4",
		});
	});

	it("requires a name, street and city, with a message that says which", () => {
		expect(messages({ ...valid, fullName: "  " })).toEqual([
			"Enter the recipient's name",
		]);
		expect(messages({ ...valid, line1: "" })).toEqual([
			"Enter a street address",
		]);
		expect(messages({ ...valid, city: "" })).toEqual(["Enter a city"]);
	});

	it("accepts common phone formats and rejects the rest", () => {
		for (const phone of [
			"555123456",
			"+995 (555) 12-34-56",
			"032 2 12 34 56",
		]) {
			expect(addressSchema.safeParse({ ...valid, phone }).success).toBe(true);
		}
		for (const phone of ["", "abc", "12345", "+(((  )))", "5".repeat(31)]) {
			expect(messages({ ...valid, phone })).toEqual([
				"Enter a valid phone number",
			]);
		}
	});

	it("caps lengths", () => {
		expect(
			addressSchema.safeParse({ ...valid, fullName: "a".repeat(101) }).success,
		).toBe(false);
		expect(
			addressSchema.safeParse({ ...valid, postalCode: "1".repeat(21) }).success,
		).toBe(false);
	});

	it("needs an explicit primary flag", () => {
		const { isPrimary: _omitted, ...withoutFlag } = valid;
		expect(addressSchema.safeParse(withoutFlag).success).toBe(false);
	});
});


===== ./src/features/addresses/address.schema.ts =====

import { z } from "zod";

/** Saved addresses one account may hold. */
export const MAX_ADDRESSES = 10;

const text = (max: number, empty: string) =>
	z.string().trim().min(1, empty).max(max, `Use ${max} characters or fewer`);

const optionalText = (max: number) =>
	z.string().trim().max(max, `Use ${max} characters or fewer`);

const PHONE_ERROR = "Enter a valid phone number";

/**
 * One address as the form submits it and the server function accepts it, so
 * both sides run the same rules. The country is not asked for: the store
 * delivers in one region (`site.delivery.region`), which the table defaults to.
 */
export const addressSchema = z.object({
	fullName: text(100, "Enter the recipient's name"),
	phone: z
		.string()
		.trim()
		.refine(
			(value) =>
				value.length <= 30 &&
				/^\+?[\d\s()-]+$/.test(value) &&
				value.replace(/\D/g, "").length >= 6,
			PHONE_ERROR,
		),
	line1: text(150, "Enter a street address"),
	line2: optionalText(150),
	city: text(80, "Enter a city"),
	postalCode: optionalText(20),
	/** Ask for this address to become the primary one. Ignored when it already is. */
	isPrimary: z.boolean(),
});

export type AddressInput = z.infer<typeof addressSchema>;


===== ./src/features/addresses/address.server.ts =====

import { and, asc, desc, eq } from "drizzle-orm";
import { db } from "#/db";
import { address, user } from "#/db/schema";
import { type AddressInput, MAX_ADDRESSES } from "./address.schema";

/** An error whose message is meant for the shopper; anything else is hidden behind a generic one. */
export class AddressError extends Error {}

function ensure(condition: unknown, message: string): asserts condition {
	if (!condition) throw new AddressError(message);
}

type Tx = Parameters<Parameters<typeof db.transaction>[0]>[0];

/** What the browser may see: never the owner or the timestamps. */
const publicColumns = {
	id: address.id,
	fullName: address.fullName,
	phone: address.phone,
	line1: address.line1,
	line2: address.line2,
	city: address.city,
	postalCode: address.postalCode,
	country: address.country,
	isPrimary: address.isPrimary,
};

type AddressFields = Omit<AddressInput, "isPrimary">;

/** Empty optional fields are stored as null. */
const toRow = (fields: AddressFields) => ({
	...fields,
	line2: fields.line2 || null,
	postalCode: fields.postalCode || null,
});

const mine = (userId: string, id: string) =>
	and(eq(address.id, id), eq(address.userId, userId));

/**
 * Takes a row lock on the owner for the rest of the transaction, so one
 * account's address changes run one at a time. That keeps the address limit
 * and the single primary true even when two requests race.
 */
const lockOwner = (tx: Tx, userId: string) =>
	tx
		.select({ id: user.id })
		.from(user)
		.where(eq(user.id, userId))
		.for("update");

/** Frees the primary slot first: the partial unique index allows only one primary per user. */
const demotePrimary = (tx: Tx, userId: string) =>
	tx
		.update(address)
		.set({ isPrimary: false })
		.where(and(eq(address.userId, userId), eq(address.isPrimary, true)));

export const addresses = {
	/** The primary address first, then the rest in the order they were added. */
	async list(userId: string) {
		return db
			.select(publicColumns)
			.from(address)
			.where(eq(address.userId, userId))
			.orderBy(
				desc(address.isPrimary),
				asc(address.createdAt),
				asc(address.id),
			);
	},

	async create(userId: string, { isPrimary, ...fields }: AddressInput) {
		await db.transaction(async (tx) => {
			await lockOwner(tx, userId);

			const existing = await tx
				.select({ id: address.id })
				.from(address)
				.where(eq(address.userId, userId));
			ensure(
				existing.length < MAX_ADDRESSES,
				`You can save up to ${MAX_ADDRESSES} addresses.`,
			);

			// The first address is primary automatically.
			const primary = isPrimary || existing.length === 0;
			if (primary) await demotePrimary(tx, userId);

			await tx
				.insert(address)
				.values({ ...toRow(fields), userId, isPrimary: primary });
		});
	},

	async update(
		userId: string,
		id: string,
		{ isPrimary, ...fields }: AddressInput,
	) {
		await db.transaction(async (tx) => {
			await lockOwner(tx, userId);

			const [current] = await tx
				.select({ isPrimary: address.isPrimary })
				.from(address)
				.where(mine(userId, id));
			ensure(current, "That address no longer exists.");

			// An account always keeps a primary address, so unticking it here does
			// nothing; picking another address as primary is how it moves.
			const promote = isPrimary && !current.isPrimary;
			if (promote) await demotePrimary(tx, userId);

			await tx
				.update(address)
				.set({ ...toRow(fields), ...(promote && { isPrimary: true }) })
				.where(mine(userId, id));
		});
	},

	async setPrimary(userId: string, id: string) {
		await db.transaction(async (tx) => {
			await lockOwner(tx, userId);

			const [target] = await tx
				.select({ isPrimary: address.isPrimary })
				.from(address)
				.where(mine(userId, id));
			ensure(target, "That address no longer exists.");
			if (target.isPrimary) return;

			await demotePrimary(tx, userId);
			await tx.update(address).set({ isPrimary: true }).where(mine(userId, id));
		});
	},

	async remove(userId: string, id: string) {
		await db.transaction(async (tx) => {
			await lockOwner(tx, userId);

			const [removed] = await tx
				.delete(address)
				.where(mine(userId, id))
				.returning({ isPrimary: address.isPrimary });
			if (!removed?.isPrimary) return;

			// Never leave an account without a primary while it still has addresses.
			const [next] = await tx
				.select({ id: address.id })
				.from(address)
				.where(eq(address.userId, userId))
				.orderBy(asc(address.createdAt), asc(address.id))
				.limit(1);
			if (next) {
				await tx
					.update(address)
					.set({ isPrimary: true })
					.where(eq(address.id, next.id));
			}
		});
	},
};


===== ./src/features/addresses/components/address-book.tsx =====

import { useAuth, useSession } from "@better-auth-ui/react";
import { Add01Icon, Location01Icon } from "@hugeicons/core-free-icons";
import { HugeiconsIcon } from "@hugeicons/react";
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { Fragment, useState } from "react";
import { Button } from "#/components/ui/button";
import { Card, CardContent } from "#/components/ui/card";
import {
	Empty,
	EmptyContent,
	EmptyDescription,
	EmptyHeader,
	EmptyMedia,
	EmptyTitle,
} from "#/components/ui/empty";
import {
	Item,
	ItemContent,
	ItemGroup,
	ItemMedia,
	ItemSeparator,
} from "#/components/ui/item";
import { Skeleton } from "#/components/ui/skeleton";
import { cn } from "#/lib/utils";
import { addressesQuery } from "../address.queries";
import { MAX_ADDRESSES } from "../address.schema";
import { AddressRow } from "./address-row";
import { AddressSheet } from "./address-sheet";

export type AddressBookProps = {
	className?: string;
};

/** Saved delivery addresses in account settings. */
export function AddressBook({ className }: AddressBookProps) {
	const { authClient } = useAuth();
	const { data: session } = useSession(authClient);
	const {
		data: addresses,
		isPending,
		isError,
		isFetching,
		refetch,
	} = useQuery(addressesQuery(session?.user.id));
	const [adding, setAdding] = useState(false);

	const count = addresses?.length ?? 0;
	const atLimit = count >= MAX_ADDRESSES;

	return (
		<div>
			<div className="mb-3 flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
				<h2 className="text-sm font-semibold">Addresses</h2>

				{count > 0 && (
					<Button
						variant="outline"
						size="sm"
						disabled={atLimit}
						onClick={() => setAdding(true)}
					>
						<HugeiconsIcon icon={Add01Icon} data-icon="inline-start" />
						Add address
					</Button>
				)}
			</div>

			<Card className={cn("gap-0 p-0", className)}>
				<CardContent className="p-0">
					{isPending ? (
						<AddressRowSkeleton />
					) : isError ? (
						<div className="flex flex-col items-start gap-3 p-4">
							<p role="alert" className="text-destructive text-sm">
								Couldn't load your addresses.
							</p>
							<Button
								variant="outline"
								size="sm"
								disabled={isFetching}
								onClick={() => refetch()}
							>
								{isFetching ? "Trying again…" : "Try again"}
							</Button>
						</div>
					) : addresses.length === 0 ? (
						<Empty>
							<EmptyHeader>
								<EmptyMedia variant="icon">
									<HugeiconsIcon icon={Location01Icon} />
								</EmptyMedia>
								<EmptyTitle>No saved addresses</EmptyTitle>
								<EmptyDescription>
									Save and manage your delivery addresses.
								</EmptyDescription>
							</EmptyHeader>
							<EmptyContent>
								<Button size="sm" onClick={() => setAdding(true)}>
									<HugeiconsIcon icon={Add01Icon} data-icon="inline-start" />
									Add address
								</Button>
							</EmptyContent>
						</Empty>
					) : (
						<ItemGroup className="gap-0!">
							{addresses.map((address, index) => (
								<Fragment key={address.id}>
									{index > 0 && <ItemSeparator className="my-0!" />}
									<AddressRow
										address={address}
										isOnly={addresses.length === 1}
									/>
								</Fragment>
							))}
						</ItemGroup>
					)}
				</CardContent>

				{count > 0 && (
					<p className="border-t p-4 text-muted-foreground text-xs">
						{atLimit
							? `You've saved the most addresses allowed (${MAX_ADDRESSES}). Delete one to add another.`
							: "Your primary address is your default delivery address."}
					</p>
				)}
			</Card>

			<AddressSheet
				open={adding}
				onOpenChange={setAdding}
				firstAddress={count === 0}
			/>
		</div>
	);
}

function AddressRowSkeleton() {
	return (
		<Item>
			<ItemMedia>
				<Skeleton className="size-4 rounded-md" />
			</ItemMedia>
			<ItemContent>
				<Skeleton className="h-4 w-32" />
				<Skeleton className="h-3 w-48" />
				<Skeleton className="h-3 w-24" />
			</ItemContent>
		</Item>
	);
}


===== ./src/features/addresses/components/address-row.tsx =====

import {
	Delete02Icon,
	Edit02Icon,
	Location01Icon,
	MoreHorizontalIcon,
	StarIcon,
} from "@hugeicons/core-free-icons";
import { HugeiconsIcon } from "@hugeicons/react";
import { useState } from "react";
import {
	AlertDialog,
	AlertDialogCancel,
	AlertDialogContent,
	AlertDialogDescription,
	AlertDialogFooter,
	AlertDialogHeader,
	AlertDialogMedia,
	AlertDialogTitle,
} from "#/components/ui/alert-dialog";
import { Badge } from "#/components/ui/badge";
import { Button } from "#/components/ui/button";
import {
	DropdownMenu,
	DropdownMenuContent,
	DropdownMenuItem,
	DropdownMenuSeparator,
	DropdownMenuTrigger,
} from "#/components/ui/dropdown-menu";
import {
	Item,
	ItemActions,
	ItemContent,
	ItemDescription,
	ItemMedia,
	ItemTitle,
} from "#/components/ui/item";
import { Spinner } from "#/components/ui/spinner";
import { formatAddress } from "../address.format";
import type { Address } from "../address.functions";
import { useMakePrimaryAddress, useRemoveAddress } from "../use-addresses";
import { AddressSheet } from "./address-sheet";

type AddressRowProps = {
	address: Address;
	/** The only saved address, so deleting it leaves nobody to inherit the primary. */
	isOnly: boolean;
};

/** One saved address with its actions. The row owns its edit sheet and delete prompt. */
export function AddressRow({ address, isOnly }: AddressRowProps) {
	const makePrimary = useMakePrimaryAddress();
	const remove = useRemoveAddress();
	const [editing, setEditing] = useState(false);
	const [confirmingDelete, setConfirmingDelete] = useState(false);

	const { street, locality } = formatAddress(address);

	return (
		<Item>
			<ItemMedia variant="icon">
				<HugeiconsIcon icon={Location01Icon} />
			</ItemMedia>

			<ItemContent>
				<ItemTitle>
					{address.fullName}
					{address.isPrimary && <Badge variant="secondary">Primary</Badge>}
				</ItemTitle>
				<ItemDescription>{street}</ItemDescription>
				<ItemDescription>{locality}</ItemDescription>
				<ItemDescription>{address.phone}</ItemDescription>
			</ItemContent>

			<ItemActions>
				<DropdownMenu>
					<DropdownMenuTrigger
						render={
							<Button
								variant="ghost"
								size="icon-sm"
								aria-label={`Actions for ${address.fullName}, ${street}`}
							/>
						}
					>
						<HugeiconsIcon icon={MoreHorizontalIcon} size={18} />
					</DropdownMenuTrigger>
					<DropdownMenuContent align="end">
						{!address.isPrimary && (
							<DropdownMenuItem
								disabled={makePrimary.isPending}
								onClick={() => makePrimary.mutate(address.id)}
							>
								<HugeiconsIcon icon={StarIcon} />
								Make primary
							</DropdownMenuItem>
						)}
						<DropdownMenuItem onClick={() => setEditing(true)}>
							<HugeiconsIcon icon={Edit02Icon} />
							Edit
						</DropdownMenuItem>
						<DropdownMenuSeparator />
						<DropdownMenuItem
							variant="destructive"
							onClick={() => setConfirmingDelete(true)}
						>
							<HugeiconsIcon icon={Delete02Icon} />
							Delete
						</DropdownMenuItem>
					</DropdownMenuContent>
				</DropdownMenu>
			</ItemActions>

			<AddressSheet
				open={editing}
				onOpenChange={setEditing}
				address={address}
			/>

			<AlertDialog
				open={confirmingDelete}
				onOpenChange={(open) => !remove.isPending && setConfirmingDelete(open)}
			>
				<AlertDialogContent>
					<AlertDialogHeader>
						<AlertDialogMedia className="bg-destructive/10 text-destructive dark:bg-destructive/20 dark:text-destructive">
							<HugeiconsIcon icon={Delete02Icon} />
						</AlertDialogMedia>
						<AlertDialogTitle>Delete this address?</AlertDialogTitle>
						<AlertDialogDescription>
							{street}, {locality} will be removed from your account.
							{address.isPrimary &&
								!isOnly &&
								" Your oldest remaining address becomes the primary one."}
						</AlertDialogDescription>
					</AlertDialogHeader>
					<AlertDialogFooter>
						<AlertDialogCancel disabled={remove.isPending}>
							Cancel
						</AlertDialogCancel>
						<Button
							type="button"
							variant="destructive"
							disabled={remove.isPending}
							onClick={() => remove.mutate(address.id)}
						>
							{remove.isPending && <Spinner />}
							Delete address
						</Button>
					</AlertDialogFooter>
				</AlertDialogContent>
			</AlertDialog>
		</Item>
	);
}


===== ./src/features/addresses/components/address-sheet.tsx =====

import { useId } from "react";
import { useAuthForm } from "#/components/auth/auth-form";
import { Button } from "#/components/ui/button";
import {
	Field,
	FieldContent,
	FieldDescription,
	FieldGroup,
	FieldLabel,
} from "#/components/ui/field";
import {
	Sheet,
	SheetContent,
	SheetDescription,
	SheetFooter,
	SheetHeader,
	SheetTitle,
} from "#/components/ui/sheet";
import { Switch } from "#/components/ui/switch";
import { site } from "#/data/site";
import type { Address } from "../address.functions";
import { addressSchema } from "../address.schema";
import { useSaveAddress } from "../use-addresses";

const textFields = [
	{
		name: "fullName",
		label: "Full name",
		autoComplete: "name",
		required: true,
	},
	{
		name: "phone",
		label: "Phone number",
		autoComplete: "tel",
		type: "tel",
		required: true,
	},
	{
		name: "line1",
		label: "Street address",
		autoComplete: "address-line1",
		required: true,
	},
	{
		name: "line2",
		label: "Apartment, floor, entrance (optional)",
		autoComplete: "address-line2",
	},
	{
		name: "city",
		label: "City",
		autoComplete: "address-level2",
		required: true,
	},
	{
		name: "postalCode",
		label: "Postal code (optional)",
		autoComplete: "postal-code",
	},
] as const;

type AddressSheetProps = {
	open: boolean;
	onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void;
	/** The address being edited; leave out to add a new one. */
	address?: Address;
	/** Adding the account's first address, which is primary automatically. */
	firstAddress?: boolean;
};

/**
 * The add/edit form in a side sheet. The sheet unmounts its content when it
 * closes, so every opening starts from a fresh form.
 */
export function AddressSheet({
	open,
	onOpenChange,
	address,
	firstAddress = false,
}: AddressSheetProps) {
	return (
		<Sheet open={open} onOpenChange={onOpenChange}>
			<SheetContent className="data-[side=right]:w-full data-[side=right]:sm:max-w-md">
				<SheetHeader>
					<SheetTitle>{address ? "Edit address" : "Add address"}</SheetTitle>
					<SheetDescription>
						We deliver within {site.delivery.region}.
					</SheetDescription>
				</SheetHeader>

				<AddressForm
					address={address}
					firstAddress={firstAddress}
					onDone={() => onOpenChange(false)}
				/>
			</SheetContent>
		</Sheet>
	);
}

function primaryHint(address: Address | undefined, firstAddress: boolean) {
	if (address?.isPrimary) {
		return "This is your primary address. Make another address primary to change it.";
	}
	if (firstAddress) return "Your first address is primary automatically.";
	return "Make this your default delivery address.";
}

type AddressFormProps = {
	address?: Address;
	firstAddress: boolean;
	onDone: () => void;
};

function AddressForm({ address, firstAddress, onDone }: AddressFormProps) {
	const save = useSaveAddress();
	const primaryId = useId();
	// An account always has a primary address, so these cases can't be unticked.
	const lockedPrimary = Boolean(address?.isPrimary) || firstAddress;

	const form = useAuthForm({
		defaultValues: {
			fullName: address?.fullName ?? "",
			phone: address?.phone ?? "",
			line1: address?.line1 ?? "",
			line2: address?.line2 ?? "",
			city: address?.city ?? "",
			postalCode: address?.postalCode ?? "",
			isPrimary: lockedPrimary,
		},
		validators: { onChange: addressSchema },
		onSubmit: async ({ value }) => {
			await save.mutateAsync({ id: address?.id, address: value });
			onDone();
		},
	});

	return (
		<form.AppForm>
			<form.AuthFormRoot className="flex min-h-0 flex-1 flex-col">
				<FieldGroup className="flex-1 gap-4 overflow-y-auto px-6 pb-6">
					<div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
						{textFields.map(({ name, ...props }) => (
							<div
								key={name}
								className={
									name === "city" || name === "postalCode"
										? undefined
										: "sm:col-span-2"
								}
							>
								<form.AppField name={name}>
									{(field) => (
										<field.AuthFormTextField
											{...props}
											disabled={save.isPending}
										/>
									)}
								</form.AppField>
							</div>
						))}
					</div>

					<form.AppField name="isPrimary">
						{(field) => (
							<Field orientation="horizontal">
								<FieldContent>
									<FieldLabel htmlFor={primaryId}>
										Use as primary address
									</FieldLabel>
									<FieldDescription>
										{primaryHint(address, firstAddress)}
									</FieldDescription>
								</FieldContent>
								<Switch
									id={primaryId}
									name={field.name}
									onBlur={field.handleBlur}
									checked={field.state.value}
									disabled={lockedPrimary || save.isPending}
									onCheckedChange={field.handleChange}
								/>
							</Field>
						)}
					</form.AppField>

					<form.AuthFormServerError />
				</FieldGroup>

				<SheetFooter className="flex-row justify-end border-t">
					<Button
						type="button"
						variant="outline"
						disabled={save.isPending}
						onClick={onDone}
					>
						Cancel
					</Button>
					<form.AuthFormSubmitButton isPending={save.isPending}>
						{address ? "Save changes" : "Save address"}
					</form.AuthFormSubmitButton>
				</SheetFooter>
			</form.AuthFormRoot>
		</form.AppForm>
	);
}


===== ./src/features/addresses/use-addresses.ts =====

import {
	type QueryClient,
	useMutation,
	useQueryClient,
} from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { toast } from "sonner";
import {
	createAddressFn,
	deleteAddressFn,
	setPrimaryAddressFn,
	updateAddressFn,
} from "./address.functions";
import { addressQueryKey } from "./address.queries";
import type { AddressInput } from "./address.schema";

const refresh = (queryClient: QueryClient) =>
	queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: addressQueryKey });

const showError = () =>
	toast.error("Couldn't update your addresses. Please try again.");

/**
 * Adds an address, or edits one when `id` is given. A failure is not toasted:
 * the form shows it in place, next to the fields that caused it.
 */
export function useSaveAddress() {
	const queryClient = useQueryClient();

	return useMutation({
		mutationFn: ({ id, address }: { id?: string; address: AddressInput }) =>
			id
				? updateAddressFn({ data: { id, address } })
				: createAddressFn({ data: address }),
		onSuccess: async (_data, { id }) => {
			await refresh(queryClient);
			toast.success(id ? "Address updated" : "Address added");
		},
	});
}

export function useMakePrimaryAddress() {
	const queryClient = useQueryClient();

	return useMutation({
		mutationFn: (id: string) => setPrimaryAddressFn({ data: { id } }),
		onSuccess: async () => {
			await refresh(queryClient);
			toast.success("Primary address updated");
		},
		onError: showError,
	});
}

export function useRemoveAddress() {
	const queryClient = useQueryClient();

	return useMutation({
		mutationFn: (id: string) => deleteAddressFn({ data: { id } }),
		onSuccess: async () => {
			await refresh(queryClient);
			toast.success("Address deleted");
		},
		onError: showError,
	});
}

===== ./src/features/auth/auth.server.test.ts =====

import { betterAuth } from "better-auth";
import { memoryAdapter } from "better-auth/adapters/memory";
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";

const { emails, send } = vi.hoisted(() => {
	const emails: { to: string; subject: string; html: string }[] = [];
	return {
		emails,
		send: vi.fn(async (email: (typeof emails)[number]) => {
			emails.push(email);
			return { error: null };
		}),
	};
});

vi.mock("resend", () => ({
	Resend: class {
		emails = { send };
	},
}));
vi.mock("#/db", () => ({ db: {} }));
vi.mock("@better-auth/drizzle-adapter/relations-v2", () => ({
	drizzleAdapter: () => undefined,
}));
vi.mock("#/server/redis.server", () => ({ getRedis: vi.fn() }));
vi.mock("#/server/env.server", () => ({
	env: {
		BETTER_AUTH_URL: "http://localhost:3000",
		BETTER_AUTH_SECRET: "test-secret-that-is-long-enough-for-auth",
		RESEND_API_KEY: "test-key",
		EMAIL_FROM: "2o <test@example.com>",
	},
}));

const { auth: appAuth } = await import("./auth.server");

function createTestAuth() {
	return betterAuth({
		...appAuth.options,
		database: memoryAdapter({
			user: [],
			session: [],
			account: [],
			verification: [],
		}),
		secondaryStorage: undefined,
		rateLimit: { enabled: false },
		plugins: appAuth.options.plugins.filter(
			(plugin) => plugin.id === "email-otp",
		),
	});
}

let auth = createTestAuth();
const email = "shopper@example.com";
const password = "original-password-123";
const newPassword = "replacement-password-456";

async function post(path: string, body: object) {
	return auth.handler(
		new Request(`http://localhost:3000/api/auth${path}`, {
			method: "POST",
			headers: {
				"Content-Type": "application/json",
				Origin: "http://localhost:3000",
			},
			body: JSON.stringify(body),
		}),
	);
}

function lastCode() {
	const message = emails.at(-1);
	expect(message?.to).toBe(email);
	const code = message?.html.match(/<strong>(\d{6})<\/strong>/)?.[1];
	expect(code).toBeDefined();
	return code;
}

beforeEach(() => {
	auth = createTestAuth();
	emails.length = 0;
});

describe("email codes through Resend", () => {
	it("requires email verification and rejects incorrect or reused codes", async () => {
		const signup = await post("/sign-up/email", {
			name: "Shopper",
			email,
			password,
		});
		expect(signup.status).toBe(200);
		expect((await signup.json()).token).toBeNull();
		expect(lastCode()).toMatch(/^\d{6}$/);

		expect((await post("/sign-in/email", { email, password })).status).toBe(
			403,
		);
		const otp = lastCode();
		const incorrect = otp === "000000" ? "111111" : "000000";
		expect(
			(await post("/email-otp/verify-email", { email, otp: incorrect })).ok,
		).toBe(false);
		expect((await post("/email-otp/verify-email", { email, otp })).ok).toBe(
			true,
		);
		expect((await post("/email-otp/verify-email", { email, otp })).ok).toBe(
			false,
		);
		expect((await post("/sign-in/email", { email, password })).ok).toBe(true);
	});

	it("resets passwords only with a valid code and revokes existing sessions", async () => {
		await post("/sign-up/email", { name: "Shopper", email, password });
		await post("/email-otp/verify-email", { email, otp: lastCode() });
		const signedIn = await post("/sign-in/email", { email, password });
		const cookie = signedIn.headers
			.getSetCookie()
			.map((value) => value.split(";")[0])
			.join("; ");
		expect(cookie).not.toBe("");
		expect(
			(await post("/email-otp/request-password-reset", { email })).ok,
		).toBe(true);
		const otp = lastCode();
		expect(emails.at(-1)?.subject).toBe("Your password reset code");
		const incorrect = otp === "000000" ? "111111" : "000000";
		expect(
			(
				await post("/email-otp/reset-password", {
					email,
					otp: incorrect,
					password: newPassword,
				})
			).ok,
		).toBe(false);
		expect(
			(
				await post("/email-otp/reset-password", {
					email,
					otp,
					password: newPassword,
				})
			).ok,
		).toBe(true);
		expect(
			(await post("/email-otp/reset-password", { email, otp, password })).ok,
		).toBe(false);
		expect((await post("/sign-in/email", { email, password })).ok).toBe(false);
		expect(
			(await post("/sign-in/email", { email, password: newPassword })).ok,
		).toBe(true);
		const session = await auth.handler(
			new Request("http://localhost:3000/api/auth/get-session", {
				headers: { cookie },
			}),
		);
		expect(await session.json()).toBeNull();
	});

	it("does not send a recovery email for an unknown account", async () => {
		expect(
			(await post("/email-otp/request-password-reset", { email })).ok,
		).toBe(true);
		expect(emails).toHaveLength(0);
	});
});


===== ./src/features/auth/auth.server.ts =====

import { drizzleAdapter } from "@better-auth/drizzle-adapter/relations-v2";
import { type BetterAuthOptions, betterAuth } from "better-auth";
import { emailOTP } from "better-auth/plugins";
import { tanstackStartCookies } from "better-auth/tanstack-start";
import { site } from "#/data/site";
import { db } from "#/db";
import * as schema from "#/db/auth-schema";
import { emailTemplates, sendEmail } from "#/features/auth/email.server";
import { socialProviders } from "#/features/auth/social-providers.server";
import { env } from "#/server/env.server";
import { getRedis } from "#/server/redis.server";

const secondaryStorage: NonNullable<BetterAuthOptions["secondaryStorage"]> = {
	get: async (key) => (await getRedis()).get(key),
	getAndDelete: async (key) => (await getRedis()).getDel(key),
	set: async (key, value, ttl) => {
		await (await getRedis()).set(key, value, ttl ? { EX: ttl } : undefined);
	},
	delete: async (key) => {
		await (await getRedis()).del([key]);
	},
	increment: async (key, ttl) => {
		const redis = await getRedis();
		const count = await redis.incr(key);
		if (count === 1) await redis.expire(key, ttl);
		return count;
	},
};

const perHour = (max: number) => ({ window: 3600, max });

export const auth = betterAuth({
	database: drizzleAdapter(db, { provider: "pg", schema }),
	secondaryStorage,

	appName: site.name,
	basePath: "/api/auth",
	baseURL: env.BETTER_AUTH_URL,
	secret: env.BETTER_AUTH_SECRET,

	emailAndPassword: {
		enabled: true,
		requireEmailVerification: true,
		revokeSessionsOnPasswordReset: true,
	},

	emailVerification: {
		sendOnSignIn: true,
	},

	user: {
		changeEmail: {
			enabled: true,
			sendChangeEmailConfirmation: async ({ user, newEmail, url }) => {
				await sendEmail({
					to: user.email,
					...emailTemplates.approveEmailChange(newEmail, url),
				});
			},
		},
	},

	rateLimit: {
		enabled: true,
		storage: "secondary-storage",
		window: 60,
		max: 100,
		customRules: {
			"/sign-in/email": { window: 60, max: 5 },
			"/sign-up/email": perHour(5),
			"/request-password-reset": perHour(3),
			"/forget-password": perHour(3),
			"/change-email": perHour(3),
		},
	},

	socialProviders,

	plugins: [
		emailOTP({
			overrideDefaultEmailVerification: true,
			disableSignUp: true,
			storeOTP: "hashed",
			async sendVerificationOTP({ email, otp, type }) {
				await sendEmail({ to: email, ...emailTemplates.code(otp, type) });
			},
		}),
		tanstackStartCookies(),
	],
});


===== ./src/features/auth/email.server.ts =====

import { escape as escapeHtml } from "es-toolkit";
import { Resend } from "resend";
import { env } from "#/server/env.server";

const resend = env.RESEND_API_KEY ? new Resend(env.RESEND_API_KEY) : null;

type Email = { subject: string; html: string };

export async function sendEmail({ to, subject, html }: Email & { to: string }) {
	if (!resend) {
		// Only reachable in development; production requires the key.
		console.info(`[email not sent] to=${to} subject=${subject}\n${html}`);
		return;
	}

	const { error } = await resend.emails.send({
		from: env.EMAIL_FROM,
		to,
		subject,
		html,
	});

	if (error) {
		console.error("Failed to send email:", error);
		throw new Error("Unable to send email. Please try again.");
	}
}

/** Transactional email bodies, kept out of the auth configuration. */
export const emailTemplates = {
	code: (otp: string, type: string): Email => ({
		subject:
			type === "forget-password"
				? "Your password reset code"
				: "Your email verification code",
		html: `<p>Your confirmation code is <strong>${escapeHtml(otp)}</strong>.</p><p>It expires in 5 minutes. If you didn't request this, ignore this email.</p>`,
	}),

	approveEmailChange: (newEmail: string, url: string): Email => ({
		subject: "Approve your email change",
		html: `<p>You asked to change your email to ${escapeHtml(newEmail)}. <a href="${escapeHtml(url)}">Approve the change</a>. If this wasn't you, ignore this email.</p>`,
	}),
};


===== ./src/features/auth/session.server.ts =====

import { getRequestHeaders } from "@tanstack/react-start/server";
import { auth } from "./auth.server";

/** The signed-in user's id. Throws for guests, so a handler can't run without one. */
export async function requireUserId() {
	const session = await auth.api.getSession({ headers: getRequestHeaders() });
	if (!session) throw new Error("Please sign in to continue.");
	return session.user.id;
}


===== ./src/features/auth/social-providers.server.ts =====

import { env } from "#/server/env.server";

type Credentials = { clientId: string; clientSecret: string };

const credentials = (
	clientId?: string,
	clientSecret?: string,
): Credentials | undefined =>
	clientId && clientSecret ? { clientId, clientSecret } : undefined;

const google = credentials(env.GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID, env.GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET);
const facebook = credentials(
	env.FACEBOOK_CLIENT_ID,
	env.FACEBOOK_CLIENT_SECRET,
);

/** Only providers that have credentials; the rest are neither enabled nor shown. */
export const socialProviders = {
	...(google && { google }),
	...(facebook && { facebook }),
};

export const enabledSocialProviders = Object.keys(socialProviders) as Array<
	keyof typeof socialProviders
>;


===== ./src/features/cart/cart-drawer.store.ts =====

import { createStore, useSelector } from "@tanstack/react-store";

/**
 * UI-only state: is the cart drawer open? Everything else about the cart is
 * server state and lives in TanStack Query (see `use-cart.ts`).
 *
 * Being a plain store, it can be opened from anywhere, including mutation
 * callbacks that run outside React.
 */
export const cartDrawer = createStore<
	boolean,
	{
		open: () => void;
		close: () => void;
		set: (open: boolean) => void;
	}
>(false, ({ setState }) => ({
	open: () => setState(() => true),
	close: () => setState(() => false),
	set: (open) => setState(() => open),
}));

export const useCartDrawerOpen = () => useSelector(cartDrawer);


===== ./src/features/cart/cart.functions.ts =====

import { createServerFn } from "@tanstack/react-start";
import { z } from "zod";
import { MAX_QUANTITY } from "./cart";
import { cart } from "./cart.server";

const skuInput = z.object({ sku: z.uuid() });
const quantityInput = skuInput.extend({
	quantity: z.int().min(0).max(MAX_QUANTITY),
});

export const getCartFn = createServerFn().handler(() => cart.get());

export const addItemFn = createServerFn({ method: "POST" })
	.validator(skuInput)
	.handler(({ data }) => cart.add(data.sku));

export const setQuantityFn = createServerFn({ method: "POST" })
	.validator(quantityInput)
	.handler(({ data }) => cart.setQuantity(data.sku, data.quantity));


===== ./src/features/cart/cart.queries.test.ts =====

import { QueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import type { CartItem } from "./cart";
import { getCartFn } from "./cart.functions";
import { cartQuery } from "./cart.queries";

vi.mock("./cart.functions", () => ({ getCartFn: vi.fn() }));

it("aborts the transport and preserves the cart when a pending read is cancelled", async () => {
	const client = new QueryClient();
	const response = Promise.withResolvers<CartItem[]>();
	vi.mocked(getCartFn).mockReturnValue(response.promise);
	client.setQueryData(cartQuery.queryKey, []);
	const request = client.query(cartQuery).catch((error: unknown) => error);
	const signal = vi.mocked(getCartFn).mock.calls[0]?.[0]?.signal;
	expect(signal).toBeInstanceOf(AbortSignal);

	await client.cancelQueries({ queryKey: cartQuery.queryKey });
	expect(signal?.aborted).toBe(true);
	expect(await request).toEqual([]);
	response.resolve([
		{
			id: "stale",
			name: "Stale response",
			quantity: 1,
			price: 100,
			currency: "GEL",
			image: null,
		},
	]);
	await response.promise;
	expect(client.getQueryData(cartQuery.queryKey)).toEqual([]);
	client.clear();
});

===== ./src/features/cart/cart.server.ts =====

import { getCookie, setCookie } from "@tanstack/react-start/server";
import { and, eq, inArray, sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import { compact, invariant, keyBy } from "es-toolkit";
import { z } from "zod";
import { db } from "#/db";
import { product, skus } from "#/db/schema";
import { getRedis } from "#/server/redis.server";
import {
	type CartItem,
	hasCurrencyConflict,
	MAX_CART_LINES,
	MAX_QUANTITY,
} from "./cart";

const CART_COOKIE = "cartId";
const CART_TTL_SECONDS = 60 * 60 * 24 * 30; // 30 days

const cookieId = () => z.uuid().safeParse(getCookie(CART_COOKIE)).data;
const keyFor = (id: string) => `cart:${id}`;

/** The visitor's cart key, creating the cart (and its cookie) on first use. */
const cartKey = () => {
	const id = cookieId() ?? crypto.randomUUID();
	setCookie(CART_COOKIE, id, {
		httpOnly: true,
		sameSite: "lax",
		secure: process.env.NODE_ENV === "production",
		maxAge: CART_TTL_SECONDS,
		path: "/",
	});
	return keyFor(id);
};

/** In-stock SKUs, already shaped like cart lines (minus the quantity). */
const findAvailableSkus = (ids: string[]) =>
	db
		.select({
			id: skus.id,
			name: sql<string>`${product.name} || ' (' || ${skus.color} || ', ' || ${skus.size} || ')'`,
			currency: product.currency,
			image: skus.imageUrl,
			price: skus.price, // minor units
		})
		.from(skus)
		.innerJoin(product, eq(skus.productId, product.id))
		.where(and(inArray(skus.id, ids), eq(skus.inStock, true)));

async function getCart(): Promise<CartItem[]> {
	const id = cookieId();
	const entries = id
		? Object.entries(await (await getRedis()).hGetAll(keyFor(id)))
		: [];
	if (entries.length === 0) return [];

	const available = keyBy(
		await findAvailableSkus(entries.map(([field]) => field)),
		(sku) => sku.id,
	);

	return compact(
		entries.map(([field, value]) => {
			const sku = available[String(field)];
			return sku && { ...sku, quantity: Number(value) };
		}),
	);
}

export const cart = {
	get: getCart,

	async add(skuId: string) {
		const [sku] = await findAvailableSkus([skuId]);
		invariant(sku, "Unavailable");
		invariant(
			!hasCurrencyConflict(await getCart(), sku.currency),
			"A cart can only hold one currency",
		);

		const redis = await getRedis();
		const key = cartKey();

		if (!(await redis.hExists(key, skuId))) {
			invariant((await redis.hLen(key)) < MAX_CART_LINES, "Cart is full");
		}

		const [quantity] = await redis
			.multi()
			.hIncrBy(key, skuId, 1)
			.expire(key, CART_TTL_SECONDS)
			.exec();

		if (Number(quantity) > MAX_QUANTITY) {
			await redis.hIncrBy(key, skuId, -1);
			throw new Error("Maximum quantity reached");
		}
	},

	/**
	 * Changes a line that is already in the cart; a quantity of 0 removes it.
	 * New lines only enter through `add`, which checks the SKU is real.
	 */
	async setQuantity(skuId: string, quantity: number) {
		const id = cookieId();
		if (!id) return;

		const redis = await getRedis();
		const key = keyFor(id);
		if (!(await redis.hExists(key, skuId))) return;

		const batch = redis.multi();
		if (quantity === 0) batch.hDel(key, skuId);
		else batch.hSet(key, skuId, String(quantity));

		await batch.expire(key, CART_TTL_SECONDS).exec();
	},

	/** Empties the cart (for checkout to call once an order is placed). */
	async clear() {
		const id = cookieId();
		if (id) await (await getRedis()).del([keyFor(id)]);
	},
};


===== ./src/features/cart/cart.test.ts =====

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import type { CartItem } from "./cart";
import {
	applyLineChange,
	EMPTY_CART,
	hasCurrencyConflict,
	summarizeCart,
} from "./cart";

const item = (overrides: Partial<CartItem>): CartItem => ({
	id: "sku",
	name: "Shoe",
	price: 1000,
	currency: "GEL",
	image: null,
	quantity: 1,
	...overrides,
});

describe("summarizeCart", () => {
	it("totals quantities and line prices", () => {
		const summary = summarizeCart([
			item({ price: 1000, quantity: 2 }),
			item({ id: "b", price: 250, quantity: 3 }),
		]);
		expect(summary.count).toBe(5);
		expect(summary.subtotal).toBe(2750);
		expect(summary.currency).toBe("GEL");
	});

	it("is empty (in the default currency) for a new cart", () => {
		expect(EMPTY_CART).toMatchObject({
			count: 0,
			subtotal: 0,
			currency: "GEL",
		});
	});
});

describe("hasCurrencyConflict", () => {
	it("accepts an empty cart and matching currencies", () => {
		expect(hasCurrencyConflict([], "USD")).toBe(false);
		expect(hasCurrencyConflict([item({})], "GEL")).toBe(false);
	});

	it("flags a line in another currency", () => {
		expect(hasCurrencyConflict([item({})], "USD")).toBe(true);
	});
});

describe("applyLineChange", () => {
	const items = [item({ id: "a", quantity: 2 }), item({ id: "b" })];

	it("sets the quantity of one line without touching the input", () => {
		const next = applyLineChange(items, { sku: "a", quantity: 5 });
		expect(next.map((i) => i.quantity)).toEqual([5, 1]);
		expect(items[0]?.quantity).toBe(2);
	});

	it("removes a line at quantity 0", () => {
		expect(
			applyLineChange(items, { sku: "a", quantity: 0 }).map((i) => i.id),
		).toEqual(["b"]);
	});

	it("ignores unknown SKUs", () => {
		expect(applyLineChange(items, { sku: "zzz", quantity: 3 })).toEqual(items);
	});
});


===== ./src/features/cart/cart.ts =====

import { sumBy } from "es-toolkit";
import { site } from "#/data/site";

export type CartItem = {
	/** SKU id (uuid). */
	id: string;
	name: string;
	/** Unit price in minor units. */
	price: number;
	currency: string;
	image: string | null;
	quantity: number;
};

export const MAX_QUANTITY = 10;

/** Distinct lines one cart may hold, so a cart can't grow without bound. */
export const MAX_CART_LINES = 50;

export const summarizeCart = (items: CartItem[]) => {
	return {
		items,
		count: sumBy(items, (item) => item.quantity),
		subtotal: sumBy(items, (item) => item.price * item.quantity),
		currency: items[0]?.currency ?? site.currency,
	};
};

type CartSummary = ReturnType<typeof summarizeCart>;

export const EMPTY_CART: CartSummary = summarizeCart([]);

/** The subtotal is one number, so a cart holds a single currency. */
export const hasCurrencyConflict = (items: CartItem[], currency: string) =>
	items.some((item) => item.currency !== currency);

export type LineChange = { sku: string; quantity: number };

/** A quantity of 0 (or less) removes the line; unknown SKUs are ignored. */
export const applyLineChange = (
	items: CartItem[],
	{ sku, quantity }: LineChange,
) =>
	quantity <= 0
		? items.filter((item) => item.id !== sku)
		: items.map((item) => (item.id === sku ? { ...item, quantity } : item));


===== ./src/features/cart/components/add-to-cart-button.tsx =====

import { Button } from "#/components/ui/button";
import { Spinner } from "#/components/ui/spinner";
import { useAddToCart } from "#/features/cart/use-cart";

/** Adds the chosen SKU to the cart; disabled until a size has been picked. */
export function AddToCartButton({ sku }: { sku?: string }) {
	const { addItem, isAdding } = useAddToCart();

	return (
		<Button
			size="lg"
			className="h-13 w-full rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold"
			disabled={!sku || isAdding}
			onClick={() => sku && addItem(sku)}
		>
			{isAdding && <Spinner />}
			{sku ? "Add to cart" : "Select a size"}
		</Button>
	);
}
