===== ./src/features/cart/components/cart-drawer.tsx =====

import { LinkButton } from "#/components/link-button";
import { Button } from "#/components/ui/button";
import {
	Sheet,
	SheetContent,
	SheetFooter,
	SheetHeader,
	SheetTitle,
} from "#/components/ui/sheet";
import { type CartItem, MAX_QUANTITY } from "#/features/cart/cart";
import {
	cartDrawer,
	useCartDrawerOpen,
} from "#/features/cart/cart-drawer.store";
import { useCart, useCartLineActions } from "#/features/cart/use-cart";
import { ProductImage } from "#/features/catalog/components/product-image";
import { formatPrice } from "#/lib/price";

export function CartDrawer() {
	const open = useCartDrawerOpen();
	const { items, subtotal, currency, isPending, isError, isFetching, refetch } =
		useCart();

	return (
		<Sheet open={open} onOpenChange={cartDrawer.actions.set}>
			<SheetContent
				side="right"
				className="flex w-full flex-col gap-0 sm:max-w-lg"
			>
				<SheetHeader className="px-6 py-7">
					<SheetTitle className="text-xl font-semibold tracking-tight">
						Your cart
					</SheetTitle>
				</SheetHeader>

				{isPending ? (
					<p role="status" className="p-4 text-muted-foreground">
						Loading your cart…
					</p>
				) : isError ? (
					<div className="flex flex-col items-start gap-3 p-4">
						<p role="alert">Couldn't load your cart.</p>
						<Button
							variant="outline"
							disabled={isFetching}
							onClick={() => refetch()}
						>
							{isFetching ? "Trying again…" : "Try again"}
						</Button>
					</div>
				) : items.length === 0 ? (
					<EmptyCart />
				) : (
					<>
						<CartLines items={items} />
						<SheetFooter className="gap-4 border-t p-6">
							<div className="flex items-center justify-between font-semibold text-lg">
								<span>Subtotal</span>
								<span>{formatPrice(subtotal, currency)}</span>
							</div>
							<p className="text-muted-foreground text-xs">
								Shipping and taxes are calculated at checkout.
							</p>
							<LinkButton
								to="/checkout"
								size="lg"
								className="h-12 w-full rounded-lg"
								onClick={cartDrawer.actions.close}
							>
								Checkout
							</LinkButton>
						</SheetFooter>
					</>
				)}
			</SheetContent>
		</Sheet>
	);
}

function EmptyCart() {
	return (
		<div className="flex flex-1 flex-col items-center justify-center gap-4 px-4 text-center">
			<p className="font-medium">Your cart is empty</p>
			<p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm">
				Add some deals and they will show up here.
			</p>
			<Button variant="outline" onClick={cartDrawer.actions.close}>
				Continue shopping
			</Button>
		</div>
	);
}

function CartLines({ items }: { items: CartItem[] }) {
	const { setQuantity, removeItem } = useCartLineActions();

	return (
		<ul className="flex-1 divide-y overflow-y-auto px-6">
			{items.map((item) => (
				<li key={item.id} className="flex gap-4 py-6">
					<ProductImage
						src={item.image}
						alt={item.name}
						className="size-24 shrink-0 rounded-lg"
					/>

					<div className="flex min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col">
						<div className="flex justify-between gap-2">
							<p className="line-clamp-2 text-sm font-medium leading-snug">
								{item.name}
							</p>
							<p className="shrink-0 text-sm font-semibold tabular-nums">
								{formatPrice(item.price * item.quantity, item.currency)}
							</p>
						</div>
						<p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm">
							{formatPrice(item.price, item.currency)} each
						</p>

						<div className="mt-auto flex items-center justify-between pt-2">
							<div className="flex items-center gap-2">
								<Button
									variant="outline"
									size="icon-sm"
									aria-label={`Decrease quantity of ${item.name}`}
									onClick={() => setQuantity(item.id, item.quantity - 1)}
								>
									−
								</Button>
								<span className="w-6 text-center text-sm">{item.quantity}</span>
								<Button
									variant="outline"
									size="icon-sm"
									aria-label={`Increase quantity of ${item.name}`}
									disabled={item.quantity >= MAX_QUANTITY}
									onClick={() => setQuantity(item.id, item.quantity + 1)}
								>
									+
								</Button>
							</div>
							<Button
								variant="link"
								size="sm"
								type="button"
								className="h-auto p-0 text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground"
								onClick={() => removeItem(item.id)}
							>
								Remove
							</Button>
						</div>
					</div>
				</li>
			))}
		</ul>
	);
}


===== ./src/features/cart/use-cart.ts =====

import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { toast } from "sonner";
import {
	applyLineChange,
	EMPTY_CART,
	type LineChange,
	summarizeCart,
} from "#/features/cart/cart";
import { addItemFn, setQuantityFn } from "#/features/cart/cart.functions";
import { cartQuery } from "#/features/cart/cart.queries";
import { cartDrawer } from "./cart-drawer.store";

/** The cart's line items plus derived count, subtotal and currency. */
export const useCart = () => {
	const { data, isPending, isError, isFetching, refetch } = useQuery({
		...cartQuery,
		select: summarizeCart,
	});

	return { ...(data ?? EMPTY_CART), isPending, isError, isFetching, refetch };
};

const showCartError = () =>
	toast.error("Couldn't update your cart. Please try again.");

const useRefetchCart = () => {
	const queryClient = useQueryClient();

	return () => queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: cartQuery.queryKey });
};

/** Adds one unit of a SKU and opens the drawer after a successful update. */
export function useAddToCart() {
	const refetch = useRefetchCart();

	const { mutate, isPending } = useMutation({
		mutationFn: (sku: string) => addItemFn({ data: { sku } }),
		onError: showCartError,
		onSettled: async (_data, error) => {
			await refetch();
			if (!error) cartDrawer.actions.open();
		},
	});

	return { addItem: mutate, isAdding: isPending };
}

/** Quantity changes show up immediately and roll back if the server refuses. */
export function useCartLineActions() {
	const queryClient = useQueryClient();
	const refetch = useRefetchCart();

	const { mutate } = useMutation({
		mutationFn: (change: LineChange) => setQuantityFn({ data: change }),
		onMutate: async (change) => {
			await queryClient.cancelQueries({ queryKey: cartQuery.queryKey });
			const previous = queryClient.getQueryData(cartQuery.queryKey);
			queryClient.setQueryData(
				cartQuery.queryKey,
				(items) => items && applyLineChange(items, change),
			);
			return { previous };
		},
		onError: (_error, _change, context) => {
			if (context?.previous) {
				queryClient.setQueryData(cartQuery.queryKey, context.previous);
			}
			showCartError();
		},
		onSettled: refetch,
	});

	return {
		setQuantity: (sku: string, quantity: number) => mutate({ sku, quantity }),
		removeItem: (sku: string) => mutate({ sku, quantity: 0 }),
	};
}


===== ./src/features/catalog/components/active-filters.tsx =====

import { Cancel01Icon } from "@hugeicons/core-free-icons";
import { HugeiconsIcon } from "@hugeicons/react";
import { Button } from "#/components/ui/button";
import {
	type CatalogFilters,
	DEFAULT_FILTERS,
} from "#/features/search/catalog-filters";
import { formatPriceCompact } from "#/lib/price";

type Chip = { key: string; label: string; clear: Partial<CatalogFilters> };

const capitalize = (text: string) =>
	text.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + text.slice(1);

function priceLabel({ minPrice, maxPrice }: CatalogFilters) {
	if (minPrice != null && maxPrice != null) {
		return `${formatPriceCompact(minPrice)} to ${formatPriceCompact(maxPrice)}`;
	}
	return minPrice != null
		? `From ${formatPriceCompact(minPrice)}`
		: `Up to ${formatPriceCompact(maxPrice ?? 0)}`;
}

function chipsFor(filters: CatalogFilters, showSale: boolean): Chip[] {
	const chips: Chip[] = [];

	if (filters.q) {
		chips.push({ key: "q", label: `“${filters.q}”`, clear: { q: undefined } });
	}
	for (const brand of filters.brands) {
		chips.push({
			key: `brand:${brand}`,
			label: brand,
			clear: { brands: filters.brands.filter((b) => b !== brand) },
		});
	}
	for (const size of filters.sizes) {
		chips.push({
			key: `size:${size}`,
			label: `Size ${size.toUpperCase()}`,
			clear: { sizes: filters.sizes.filter((s) => s !== size) },
		});
	}
	for (const color of filters.colors) {
		chips.push({
			key: `color:${color}`,
			label: capitalize(color),
			clear: { colors: filters.colors.filter((c) => c !== color) },
		});
	}
	if (filters.minPrice != null || filters.maxPrice != null) {
		chips.push({
			key: "price",
			label: priceLabel(filters),
			clear: { minPrice: undefined, maxPrice: undefined },
		});
	}
	if (showSale && filters.sale) {
		chips.push({ key: "sale", label: "On sale", clear: { sale: false } });
	}

	return chips;
}

type ActiveFiltersProps = {
	filters: CatalogFilters;
	/** The deals view is always on sale, so it has no sale chip. */
	showSale: boolean;
	onChange: (filters: CatalogFilters) => void;
};

/** What is narrowing the results right now, each removable with one click. */
export function ActiveFilters({
	filters,
	showSale,
	onChange,
}: ActiveFiltersProps) {
	const chips = chipsFor(filters, showSale);
	if (chips.length === 0) return null;

	return (
		<ul
			className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2"
			aria-label="Active filters"
		>
			{chips.map((chip) => (
				<li key={chip.key}>
					<Button
						variant="secondary"
						size="xs"
						aria-label={`Remove filter: ${chip.label}`}
						onClick={() => onChange({ ...filters, ...chip.clear })}
					>
						{chip.label}
						<HugeiconsIcon icon={Cancel01Icon} size={12} />
					</Button>
				</li>
			))}
			<li>
				<Button
					variant="ghost"
					size="xs"
					onClick={() => onChange({ ...DEFAULT_FILTERS, sort: filters.sort })}
				>
					Clear all
				</Button>
			</li>
		</ul>
	);
}


===== ./src/features/catalog/components/catalog-listing.tsx =====

import { useQuery, useSuspenseInfiniteQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import { InfiniteScrollFooter } from "#/components/infinite-scroll-footer";
import { Button } from "#/components/ui/button";
import {
	catalogFacetsQuery,
	catalogQuery,
} from "#/features/search/catalog.queries";
import {
	type CatalogFilters,
	type CatalogScope,
	DEFAULT_FILTERS,
	hasActiveFilters,
} from "#/features/search/catalog-filters";
import { isDeal } from "../product.types";
import { DealCard, dealImageLoading } from "./deal-card";
import { FilterBar } from "./filter-bar";
import { ProductGrid } from "./product-grid";

type CatalogListingProps = {
	scope: CatalogScope;
	filters: CatalogFilters;
	onFiltersChange: (filters: CatalogFilters) => void;
	/** Shown when the scope has no products at all (not when filters hide them). */
	empty?: ReactNode;
};

/**
 * One listing for departments, categories and deals. The URL holds the
 * filters, the server does the filtering, and this only renders the result.
 */
export function CatalogListing({
	scope,
	filters,
	onFiltersChange,
	empty,
}: CatalogListingProps) {
	const {
		data,
		hasNextPage,
		fetchNextPage,
		isFetchingNextPage,
		isFetchNextPageError,
	} = useSuspenseInfiniteQuery(catalogQuery(scope, filters));
	const { data: facets } = useQuery(catalogFacetsQuery(scope, filters));

	const items = data.pages.flatMap((page) => page.items);
	const total = data.pages[0]?.total ?? 0;
	const filtered = hasActiveFilters(filters);

	return (
		<>
			{facets && (
				<FilterBar
					scope={scope}
					filters={filters}
					facets={facets}
					onChange={onFiltersChange}
				/>
			)}

			{total > 0 && (
				<p
					className="mt-5 text-muted-foreground text-xs tracking-wide"
					aria-live="polite"
				>
					<span className="type-num text-foreground">
						{total.toLocaleString("en-US")}
					</span>{" "}
					{total === 1 ? "result" : "results"}
				</p>
			)}

			{items.length === 0 ? (
				filtered ? (
					<div className="flex flex-col items-start gap-3 py-10">
						<p className="font-medium">Nothing matches these filters.</p>
						<p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm">
							Try a shorter search or remove a filter.
						</p>
						<Button
							variant="outline"
							onClick={() =>
								onFiltersChange({ ...DEFAULT_FILTERS, sort: filters.sort })
							}
						>
							Clear filters
						</Button>
					</div>
				) : (
					(empty ?? (
						<p className="py-10 text-muted-foreground">No products yet.</p>
					))
				)
			) : scope.mode === "deals" ? (
				<ul className="mt-8 grid grid-cols-2 gap-x-4 gap-y-10 sm:gap-x-6 md:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-4">
					{items.filter(isDeal).map((deal, index) => (
						<li key={deal.id}>
							<DealCard deal={deal} loading={dealImageLoading(index)} />
						</li>
					))}
				</ul>
			) : (
				<ProductGrid products={items} />
			)}

			<InfiniteScrollFooter
				hasNextPage={hasNextPage}
				isFetchingNextPage={isFetchingNextPage}
				isError={isFetchNextPageError}
				fetchNextPage={fetchNextPage}
			/>
		</>
	);
}


===== ./src/features/catalog/components/deal-card.tsx =====

import { Link } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import type { Deal } from "#/features/catalog/product.types";
import { formatPriceCompact } from "#/lib/price";
import { cn } from "#/lib/utils";
import { ProductImage } from "./product-image";
import { SkewTag } from "./skew-tag";

/** Cards this far down a row or grid are usually below the fold. */
const EAGER_IMAGE_COUNT = 4;

/** Image loading strategy for the card at `index` in a list of deals. */
export const dealImageLoading = (index: number) =>
	index < EAGER_IMAGE_COUNT ? "eager" : "lazy";

type DealCardProps = {
	deal: Deal;
	loading?: "lazy" | "eager";
	className?: string;
};

/** One-link card (stretched link on the name) for a discounted product. */
export function DealCard({ deal, loading, className }: DealCardProps) {
	const {
		slug,
		name,
		brand,
		imageUrl,
		price,
		originalPrice,
		currency,
		discount,
	} = deal;

	return (
		<article className={cn("group relative", className)}>
			<div className="relative">
				<ProductImage
					src={imageUrl}
					alt=""
					loading={loading}
					className="aspect-4/5 rounded-xl [&_img]:transition-transform [&_img]:duration-500 motion-safe:group-hover:[&_img]:scale-[1.03]"
				/>
				<SkewTag className="type-num absolute top-3 left-3 text-sm">
					−{discount}%
				</SkewTag>
			</div>

			<div className="mt-5 space-y-2">
				{brand && <p className="type-eyebrow text-muted-foreground">{brand}</p>}
				<h3 className="min-h-11 text-sm font-medium leading-snug tracking-tight sm:text-base">
					<Link
						to="/product/$slug"
						params={{ slug }}
						className="after:absolute after:inset-0 after:rounded-xl focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:after:outline-2 focus-visible:after:outline-offset-4"
					>
						{name}
					</Link>
				</h3>
				<p className="flex flex-wrap items-baseline gap-x-2 gap-y-1 pt-1">
					<span className="text-muted-foreground text-xs">From</span>
					<span className="type-num text-lg sm:text-xl">
						{formatPriceCompact(price, currency)}
					</span>
					<span className="text-muted-foreground text-sm line-through">
						{formatPriceCompact(originalPrice, currency)}
					</span>
				</p>
				<p className="font-medium text-green-700 text-xs sm:text-sm">
					You save {formatPriceCompact(originalPrice - price, currency)}
				</p>
			</div>
		</article>
	);
}


===== ./src/features/catalog/components/department-breadcrumbs.tsx =====

import { Link } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import {
	Breadcrumb,
	BreadcrumbItem,
	BreadcrumbLink,
	BreadcrumbList,
	BreadcrumbPage,
	BreadcrumbSeparator,
} from "#/components/ui/breadcrumb";
import {
	type Category,
	categories,
	type Department,
	departments,
} from "#/data/catalog";

type DepartmentBreadcrumbsProps = {
	department: Department;
	category?: Category;
};

export function DepartmentBreadcrumbs({
	department,
	category,
}: DepartmentBreadcrumbsProps) {
	const departmentLabel = departments[department].label;

	return (
		<Breadcrumb>
			<BreadcrumbList>
				<BreadcrumbItem>
					<BreadcrumbLink render={<Link to="/">Home</Link>} />
				</BreadcrumbItem>
				<BreadcrumbSeparator />
				<BreadcrumbItem>
					{category ? (
						<BreadcrumbLink
							render={
								<Link
									to="/$department/{-$category}"
									params={{ department, category: undefined }}
								>
									{departmentLabel}
								</Link>
							}
						/>
					) : (
						<BreadcrumbPage>{departmentLabel}</BreadcrumbPage>
					)}
				</BreadcrumbItem>
				{category && (
					<>
						<BreadcrumbSeparator />
						<BreadcrumbItem>
							<BreadcrumbPage>{categories[category].label}</BreadcrumbPage>
						</BreadcrumbItem>
					</>
				)}
			</BreadcrumbList>
		</Breadcrumb>
	);
}


===== ./src/features/catalog/components/facet-filter.tsx =====

import { useId } from "react";
import { Badge } from "#/components/ui/badge";
import { Checkbox } from "#/components/ui/checkbox";
import { Label } from "#/components/ui/label";
import { ToggleGroup, ToggleGroupItem } from "#/components/ui/toggle-group";
import { cn } from "#/lib/utils";
import { FilterPopover } from "./filter-popover";

type FacetOption = { value: string; count: number };

type FacetFilterProps = {
	label: string;
	options: FacetOption[];
	selected: string[];
	onChange: (selected: string[]) => void;
	/** "list" for long lists with counts (brands); "chips" for short ones (sizes, colors). */
	layout?: "list" | "chips";
	/** Styles each option's text, e.g. "capitalize". */
	optionClassName?: string;
};

/** A filter button that opens a popover of choices; one component serves brand, size and color. */
export function FacetFilter({
	label,
	options,
	selected,
	onChange,
	layout = "list",
	optionClassName,
}: FacetFilterProps) {
	const id = useId();

	// A chosen value stays visible even when the other filters leave it with no matches.
	const shown = [
		...options,
		...selected
			.filter((value) => !options.some((option) => option.value === value))
			.map((value) => ({ value, count: 0 })),
	];
	if (shown.length === 0) return null;

	const toggle = (value: string, on: boolean) =>
		onChange(on ? [...selected, value] : selected.filter((v) => v !== value));

	return (
		<FilterPopover
			label={label}
			trigger={
				<>
					{label}
					{selected.length > 0 && <Badge>{selected.length}</Badge>}
				</>
			}
			active={selected.length > 0}
			onClear={() => onChange([])}
		>
			{layout === "chips" ? (
				<ToggleGroup
					multiple
					variant="outline"
					spacing={2}
					className="flex-wrap"
					aria-label={label}
					value={selected}
					onValueChange={onChange}
				>
					{shown.map((option) => (
						<ToggleGroupItem
							key={option.value}
							value={option.value}
							disabled={option.count === 0 && !selected.includes(option.value)}
							className={optionClassName}
						>
							{option.value}
						</ToggleGroupItem>
					))}
				</ToggleGroup>
			) : (
				<ul className="-mx-1 max-h-64 overflow-y-auto">
					{shown.map((option, index) => (
						<li
							key={option.value}
							className="flex items-center gap-2.5 rounded-lg px-2 py-1.5 hover:bg-muted"
						>
							<Checkbox
								id={`${id}-${index}`}
								checked={selected.includes(option.value)}
								onCheckedChange={(on) => toggle(option.value, on)}
							/>
							<Label
								htmlFor={`${id}-${index}`}
								className={cn(
									"flex-1 cursor-pointer font-normal",
									optionClassName,
								)}
							>
								{option.value}
							</Label>
							<span className="text-muted-foreground text-xs tabular-nums">
								{option.count}
							</span>
						</li>
					))}
				</ul>
			)}
		</FilterPopover>
	);
}


===== ./src/features/catalog/components/filter-bar.tsx =====

import {
	Select,
	SelectContent,
	SelectItem,
	SelectTrigger,
	SelectValue,
} from "#/components/ui/select";
import { Toggle } from "#/components/ui/toggle";
import type { CatalogFacets } from "#/features/search/catalog.functions";
import {
	type CatalogFilters,
	type CatalogScope,
	type CatalogSort,
	effectiveSort,
	sortLabels,
} from "#/features/search/catalog-filters";
import { ActiveFilters } from "./active-filters";
import { FacetFilter } from "./facet-filter";
import { PriceFilter } from "./price-filter";
import { SearchBox } from "./search-box";

type FilterBarProps = {
	scope: CatalogScope;
	filters: CatalogFilters;
	facets: CatalogFacets;
	onChange: (filters: CatalogFilters) => void;
};

/** Pure composition: every control reads `filters` and reports a change; the URL owns the state. */
export function FilterBar({
	scope,
	filters,
	facets,
	onChange,
}: FilterBarProps) {
	const set = (patch: Partial<CatalogFilters>) =>
		onChange({ ...filters, ...patch });

	const sort = effectiveSort(filters, scope);
	const naturalSort = effectiveSort({ ...filters, sort: undefined }, scope);
	const sortChoices = (Object.keys(sortLabels) as CatalogSort[])
		.filter((value) => value !== "relevance" || filters.q)
		.map((value) => ({ value, label: sortLabels[value] }));

	const isListing = scope.mode === "listing";

	return (
		<div className="mt-7 flex flex-col gap-4 border-y py-5">
			<div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
				<SearchBox
					className="w-full sm:w-72"
					value={filters.q ?? ""}
					onCommit={(q) => set({ q: q.trim() || undefined })}
				/>

				<FacetFilter
					label="Brand"
					options={facets.brands}
					selected={filters.brands}
					onChange={(brands) => set({ brands })}
				/>
				<FacetFilter
					label="Size"
					layout="chips"
					optionClassName="uppercase"
					options={facets.sizes}
					selected={filters.sizes}
					onChange={(sizes) => set({ sizes })}
				/>
				<FacetFilter
					label="Color"
					layout="chips"
					optionClassName="capitalize"
					options={facets.colors}
					selected={filters.colors}
					onChange={(colors) => set({ colors })}
				/>
				<PriceFilter
					range={facets.price}
					minPrice={filters.minPrice}
					maxPrice={filters.maxPrice}
					onChange={(price) => set(price)}
				/>

				{isListing && (
					<Toggle
						variant="outline"
						size="sm"
						className="h-10 rounded-lg px-4 aria-pressed:border-foreground/40"
						pressed={filters.sale}
						onPressedChange={(sale) => set({ sale })}
					>
						On sale
					</Toggle>
				)}

				<div className="w-full sm:ml-auto sm:w-auto">
					<Select
						items={sortChoices}
						value={sort}
						onValueChange={(next) =>
							set({
								sort: next === naturalSort ? undefined : (next as CatalogSort),
							})
						}
					>
						<SelectTrigger
							className="h-10 w-full rounded-lg sm:w-48"
							aria-label="Sort by"
						>
							<SelectValue />
						</SelectTrigger>
						<SelectContent>
							{sortChoices.map((choice) => (
								<SelectItem key={choice.value} value={choice.value}>
									{choice.label}
								</SelectItem>
							))}
						</SelectContent>
					</Select>
				</div>
			</div>

			<ActiveFilters
				filters={filters}
				showSale={isListing}
				onChange={onChange}
			/>
		</div>
	);
}


===== ./src/features/catalog/components/filter-popover.tsx =====

import { ArrowDown01Icon } from "@hugeicons/core-free-icons";
import { HugeiconsIcon } from "@hugeicons/react";
import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import { Button } from "#/components/ui/button";
import {
	Popover,
	PopoverContent,
	PopoverTitle,
	PopoverTrigger,
} from "#/components/ui/popover";
import { cn } from "#/lib/utils";

type FilterPopoverProps = {
	label: string;
	trigger: ReactNode;
	active: boolean;
	onClear: () => void;
	children: ReactNode;
};

/** Shared trigger, accessible title, and clear action for catalog filters. */
export function FilterPopover({
	label,
	trigger,
	active,
	onClear,
	children,
}: FilterPopoverProps) {
	return (
		<Popover>
			<PopoverTrigger
				render={<Button variant="outline" size="sm" />}
				className={cn("h-10 rounded-lg px-4", active && "border-foreground/40")}
			>
				{trigger}
				<HugeiconsIcon icon={ArrowDown01Icon} size={14} />
			</PopoverTrigger>
			<PopoverContent align="start">
				<PopoverTitle className="sr-only">{label}</PopoverTitle>
				{children}
				{active && (
					<Button
						variant="ghost"
						size="xs"
						className="self-start"
						onClick={onClear}
					>
						Clear {label.toLowerCase()}
					</Button>
				)}
			</PopoverContent>
		</Popover>
	);
}


===== ./src/features/catalog/components/option-group.tsx =====

import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import { FieldLegend, FieldSet } from "#/components/ui/field";
import { ToggleGroup, ToggleGroupItem } from "#/components/ui/toggle-group";
import { cn } from "#/lib/utils";

type Option = {
	value: string;
	label: string;
	soldOut?: boolean;
	ariaLabel?: string;
};

type OptionGroupProps = {
	legend: ReactNode;
	options: Option[];
	value?: string;
	/** Called with the new value, or `undefined` when the active option is toggled off. */
	onChange: (value?: string) => void;
	className?: string;
};

/** A single-choice set of toggle buttons (colors, sizes, ...). */
export function OptionGroup({
	legend,
	options,
	value,
	onChange,
	className,
}: OptionGroupProps) {
	return (
		<FieldSet>
			<FieldLegend variant="label">{legend}</FieldLegend>
			<ToggleGroup
				variant="outline"
				spacing={2}
				className={cn("flex-wrap", className)}
				value={value ? [value] : []}
				onValueChange={([next]) => onChange(next)}
			>
				{options.map((o) => (
					<ToggleGroupItem
						key={o.value}
						value={o.value}
						disabled={o.soldOut}
						aria-label={o.ariaLabel}
						className={cn(
							"min-h-11 min-w-12 rounded-lg px-4",
							o.soldOut && "line-through",
						)}
					>
						{o.label}
					</ToggleGroupItem>
				))}
			</ToggleGroup>
		</FieldSet>
	);
}


===== ./src/features/catalog/components/price-filter.tsx =====

import { useState } from "react";
import { Slider } from "#/components/ui/slider";
import { formatPriceCompact } from "#/lib/price";
import { FilterPopover } from "./filter-popover";

type PriceFilterProps = {
	/** Cheapest and dearest price for the current results, in minor units. */
	range: { min: number; max: number } | null;
	minPrice?: number;
	maxPrice?: number;
	onChange: (next: { minPrice?: number; maxPrice?: number }) => void;
};

type PriceFilterInnerProps = PriceFilterProps & {
	range: NonNullable<PriceFilterProps["range"]>;
};

const toRange = (value: number | readonly number[]): [number, number] =>
	typeof value === "number" ? [value, value] : [value[0] ?? 0, value[1] ?? 0];

/** Price in whole currency units; the URL and the index keep minor units. */
export function PriceFilter(props: PriceFilterProps) {
	if (!props.range) return null;

	// Remounting on committed-value change resets the drag draft.
	return (
		<PriceFilterInner
			key={`${props.minPrice ?? ""}-${props.maxPrice ?? ""}`}
			{...props}
			range={props.range}
		/>
	);
}

function PriceFilterInner({
	range,
	minPrice,
	maxPrice,
	onChange,
}: PriceFilterInnerProps) {
	// Where the thumbs sit while being dragged; the URL only hears about it on release.
	const [draft, setDraft] = useState<[number, number] | null>(null);

	const lo = Math.floor(range.min / 100);
	const hi = Math.max(lo + 1, Math.ceil(range.max / 100));
	const clamp = (value: number) => Math.min(hi, Math.max(lo, value));

	const [from, to] = draft ?? [
		clamp(minPrice != null ? minPrice / 100 : lo),
		clamp(maxPrice != null ? maxPrice / 100 : hi),
	];
	const active = minPrice != null || maxPrice != null;

	return (
		<FilterPopover
			label="Price"
			trigger={
				active
					? `${formatPriceCompact(from * 100)} to ${formatPriceCompact(to * 100)}`
					: "Price"
			}
			active={active}
			onClear={() => onChange({ minPrice: undefined, maxPrice: undefined })}
		>
			<p className="type-num text-lg">
				{formatPriceCompact(from * 100)} to {formatPriceCompact(to * 100)}
			</p>
			<Slider
				min={lo}
				max={hi}
				step={1}
				value={[from, to]}
				aria-label="Price range"
				onValueChange={(value) => setDraft(toRange(value))}
				onValueCommitted={(value) => {
					const [nextFrom, nextTo] = toRange(value);
					onChange({
						minPrice: nextFrom <= lo ? undefined : nextFrom * 100,
						maxPrice: nextTo >= hi ? undefined : nextTo * 100,
					});
				}}
			/>
		</FilterPopover>
	);
}


===== ./src/features/catalog/components/price.tsx =====

import { formatPrice, isOnSale } from "#/lib/price";

type PriceProps = {
	price: number;
	originalPrice: number | null;
	currency: string;
};

export function Price({ price, originalPrice, currency }: PriceProps) {
	return (
		<>
			{formatPrice(price, currency)}
			{isOnSale(price, originalPrice) && (
				<span className="ml-2 text-[0.8em] font-normal text-muted-foreground line-through">
					{formatPrice(originalPrice, currency)}
				</span>
			)}
		</>
	);
}


===== ./src/features/catalog/components/product-card.tsx =====

import { Link } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { Badge } from "#/components/ui/badge";
import { buttonVariants } from "#/components/ui/button";
import { Card, CardContent, CardFooter } from "#/components/ui/card";
import type { ProductListItem } from "#/features/catalog/product.types";
import { isOnSale } from "#/lib/price";
import { cn } from "#/lib/utils";
import { Price } from "./price";
import { ProductImage } from "./product-image";

/**
 * The whole card is one link (a "stretched link" on the name) rather than
 * three separate links to the same page, which keeps keyboard and screen-reader
 * navigation to a single stop per product.
 */
export function ProductCard({ product }: { product: ProductListItem }) {
	const { slug, name, brand, imageUrl, price, originalPrice, currency } =
		product;

	return (
		<Card className="group relative h-full min-w-0 gap-4 overflow-visible rounded-none py-0 ring-0">
			<div className="relative">
				<ProductImage
					src={imageUrl}
					alt=""
					className="aspect-4/5 rounded-xl [&_img]:transition-transform [&_img]:duration-500 motion-safe:group-hover:[&_img]:scale-[1.03]"
				/>
				{isOnSale(price, originalPrice) && (
					<Badge className="absolute top-3 left-3">Sale</Badge>
				)}
			</div>

			<CardContent className="flex-1 space-y-2 px-0">
				{brand && <p className="type-eyebrow text-muted-foreground">{brand}</p>}
				<h3 className="min-h-11 text-sm font-medium leading-snug tracking-tight sm:text-base">
					<Link
						to="/product/$slug"
						params={{ slug }}
						className="after:absolute after:inset-0 after:rounded-xl focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:after:outline-2 focus-visible:after:outline-offset-4"
					>
						{name}
					</Link>
				</h3>
				<p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm font-semibold tabular-nums sm:text-base">
					<Price
						price={price}
						originalPrice={originalPrice}
						currency={currency}
					/>
				</p>
			</CardContent>

			<CardFooter className="px-0">
				{/* Visual affordance only; the stretched link above does the work. */}
				<span
					aria-hidden
					className={cn(
						buttonVariants(),
						"h-11 w-full rounded-lg text-xs sm:text-sm",
					)}
				>
					Choose size
				</span>
			</CardFooter>
		</Card>
	);
}


===== ./src/features/catalog/components/product-details.tsx =====

import { Container } from "#/components/layout/container";
import { Badge } from "#/components/ui/badge";
import { AddToCartButton } from "#/features/cart/components/add-to-cart-button";
import type { ProductDetail } from "#/features/catalog/product.functions";
import type { SkuSelection } from "#/features/catalog/product-selection";
import { isOnSale } from "#/lib/price";
import { OptionGroup } from "./option-group";
import { Price } from "./price";
import { ProductImage } from "./product-image";

type ProductDetailsProps = {
	product: ProductDetail;
	selection: SkuSelection<ProductDetail["skus"][number]>;
	onColorChange: (color: string) => void;
	onSkuChange: (sku: string | undefined) => void;
};

/** Presentational product page; selection state lives in the URL (see the route). */
export function ProductDetails({
	product,
	selection,
	onColorChange,
	onSkuChange,
}: ProductDetailsProps) {
	const { colors, color, colorSkus, selected, shown, imageUrl } = selection;

	return (
		<Container className="grid items-start gap-8 py-8 md:grid-cols-2 md:gap-12 md:py-12 lg:gap-20 lg:py-16">
			<ProductImage
				src={imageUrl}
				alt={`${product.name}, ${color}`}
				className="aspect-4/5 rounded-xl"
			/>

			<div className="flex min-w-0 flex-col gap-7 md:py-4 lg:gap-8">
				<div>
					{product.brand && (
						<p className="type-eyebrow mb-3 text-muted-foreground">
							{product.brand}
						</p>
					)}
					<h1 className="type-title text-3xl lg:text-4xl">{product.name}</h1>
					{shown && (
						<p className="mt-5 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 text-xl tabular-nums">
							<span className="font-semibold">
								{!selected && "From "}
								<Price
									price={shown.price}
									originalPrice={shown.originalPrice}
									currency={product.currency}
								/>
							</span>
							{isOnSale(shown.price, shown.originalPrice) && (
								<Badge>Sale</Badge>
							)}
						</p>
					)}
				</div>

				{shown ? (
					<>
						{colors.length > 1 && (
							<OptionGroup
								legend={
									<>
										Color:{" "}
										<span className="font-normal capitalize">{color}</span>
									</>
								}
								className="capitalize"
								value={color}
								options={colors.map((c) => ({ value: c, label: c }))}
								onChange={(next) => next && onColorChange(next)}
							/>
						)}

						<OptionGroup
							legend="Size"
							value={selected?.id}
							options={colorSkus.map((s) => ({
								value: s.id,
								label: s.size,
								soldOut: !s.inStock,
								ariaLabel: `Size ${s.size}${s.inStock ? "" : ", sold out"}`,
							}))}
							onChange={onSkuChange}
						/>

						<AddToCartButton sku={selected?.id} />
					</>
				) : (
					<p className="text-muted-foreground">
						This product is currently unavailable.
					</p>
				)}

				{product.description && (
					<p className="max-w-prose border-t pt-7 text-sm leading-7 text-muted-foreground">
						{product.description}
					</p>
				)}
			</div>
		</Container>
	);
}


===== ./src/features/catalog/components/product-grid.tsx =====

import type { ProductListItem } from "#/features/catalog/product.types";
import { ProductCard } from "./product-card";

export function ProductGrid({ products }: { products: ProductListItem[] }) {
	if (products.length === 0) {
		return <p className="py-10 text-muted-foreground">No products yet.</p>;
	}

	return (
		<div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-x-4 gap-y-10 py-8 sm:gap-x-6 md:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-4">
			{products.map((p) => (
				<ProductCard key={p.id} product={p} />
			))}
		</div>
	);
}


===== ./src/features/catalog/components/product-image.tsx =====

import type { ComponentProps } from "react";
import { cn } from "#/lib/utils";

type ProductImageProps = {
	src: string | null;
	alt: string;
	/** Pass `"lazy"` for images that are usually below the fold. */
	loading?: "lazy" | "eager";
} & Omit<ComponentProps<"div">, "children">;

/** Image on a muted placeholder, so a missing photo still reserves its space. */
export function ProductImage({
	src,
	alt,
	loading,
	className,
	...props
}: ProductImageProps) {
	return (
		<div className={cn("overflow-hidden bg-muted", className)} {...props}>
			{src && (
				<img
					src={src}
					alt={alt}
					loading={loading}
					className="size-full object-cover"
				/>
			)}
		</div>
	);
}


===== ./src/features/catalog/components/search-box.tsx =====

import { Cancel01Icon, Search01Icon } from "@hugeicons/core-free-icons";
import { HugeiconsIcon } from "@hugeicons/react";
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import {
	InputGroup,
	InputGroupAddon,
	InputGroupButton,
	InputGroupInput,
} from "#/components/ui/input-group";
import { useDebouncedCallback } from "#/hooks/use-debounced-callback";

type SearchBoxProps = {
	/** The search text currently in the URL. */
	value: string;
	/** Called shortly after typing stops, and at once on Enter or clear. */
	onCommit: (value: string) => void;
	className?: string;
};

export function SearchBox({ value, onCommit, className }: SearchBoxProps) {
	const [draft, setDraft] = useState(value);
	const committed = useRef(value);

	const commit = useDebouncedCallback((next: string) => {
		committed.current = next;
		onCommit(next);
	}, 250);

	// Follow changes made elsewhere (a removed chip, "Clear all") without ever fighting the typing.
	useEffect(() => {
		if (value !== committed.current) {
			commit.cancel();
			committed.current = value;
			setDraft(value);
		}
	}, [value, commit]);

	const clear = () => {
		commit.cancel();
		committed.current = "";
		setDraft("");
		onCommit("");
	};

	return (
		<form
			className={className}
			onSubmit={(event) => {
				event.preventDefault();
				commit.flush();
			}}
		>
			<InputGroup className="h-10">
				<InputGroupAddon align="inline-start">
					<HugeiconsIcon icon={Search01Icon} size={16} />
				</InputGroupAddon>
				<InputGroupInput
					type="search"
					autoComplete="off"
					placeholder="Search shoes and clothing"
					aria-label="Search products"
					className="[&::-webkit-search-cancel-button]:hidden"
					value={draft}
					onChange={(event) => {
						setDraft(event.target.value);
						commit(event.target.value);
					}}
				/>
				{draft && (
					<InputGroupAddon align="inline-end">
						<InputGroupButton
							size="icon-xs"
							aria-label="Clear search"
							onClick={clear}
						>
							<HugeiconsIcon icon={Cancel01Icon} size={14} />
						</InputGroupButton>
					</InputGroupAddon>
				)}
			</InputGroup>
		</form>
	);
}


===== ./src/features/catalog/components/skew-tag.tsx =====

import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import { cn } from "#/lib/utils";

/** The slanted yellow label used for discounts and the hero's "up to" claim. */
export function SkewTag({
	children,
	className,
}: {
	children: ReactNode;
	className?: string;
}) {
	return (
		<span
			className={cn(
				"inline-block -skew-x-12 bg-brand px-3 py-1 font-bold text-brand-foreground",
				className,
			)}
		>
			{/* Un-skew the text so only the shape is slanted. */}
			<span className="inline-block skew-x-12">{children}</span>
		</span>
	);
}


===== ./src/features/catalog/department-route.test.ts =====

import { QueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import {
	createMemoryHistory,
	createRootRouteWithContext,
	createRoute,
	createRouter,
} from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { loadCatalog } from "#/features/search/catalog.queries";
import { DEFAULT_FILTERS } from "#/features/search/catalog-filters";
import type { Route as RootRoute } from "#/routes/__root";
import { Route } from "#/routes/$department.{-$category}";

vi.mock("#/features/search/catalog.queries", () => ({
	loadCatalog: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(undefined),
}));
vi.mock("#/features/catalog/components/catalog-listing", () => ({
	CatalogListing: () => null,
}));

function routerFor(path: string) {
	const loader: NonNullable<typeof RootRoute.options.loader> = async () => ({
		chatEmbedUrl: null,
		socialProviders: [],
	});
	const root = createRootRouteWithContext<{ queryClient: QueryClient }>()({
		loader,
	});
	const department = createRoute({
		getParentRoute: () => root,
		path: "/$department/{-$category}",
		beforeLoad: Route.options.beforeLoad,
		validateSearch: Route.options.validateSearch,
		search: Route.options.search,
		loaderDeps: Route.options.loaderDeps,
		loader: Route.options.loader,
	});
	const deals = createRoute({ getParentRoute: () => root, path: "/deals" });
	return createRouter({
		routeTree: root.addChildren([department, deals]),
		context: { queryClient: new QueryClient() },
		history: createMemoryHistory({ initialEntries: [path] }),
	});
}

beforeEach(() => vi.clearAllMocks());

describe("department route", () => {
	it.each([
		["/men", "men", undefined, "Men"],
		["/women", "women", undefined, "Women"],
		["/kids/shoes", "kids", "shoes", "Kids shoes"],
		["/women/clothing", "women", "clothing", "Women clothing"],
	])(
		"loads %s with its validated scope",
		async (path, department, category, title) => {
			const router = routerFor(path);
			await router.load();
			expect(router.state.matches.at(-1)?.status).toBe("success");
			expect(router.state.matches.at(-1)?.context).toMatchObject({ title });
			expect(loadCatalog).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
				router.options.context?.queryClient,
				{ department, category, mode: "listing" },
				DEFAULT_FILTERS,
			);
		},
	);

	it.each(["/unknown", "/men/unknown"])(
		"returns a 404 for %s",
		async (path) => {
			const router = routerFor(path);
			await router.load();
			expect(router.state.matches.at(-1)?.status).toBe("notFound");
			expect(loadCatalog).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
		},
	);

	it("clears the category when navigating to a department", async () => {
		const router = routerFor("/men/shoes");
		await router.load();
		const location = router.buildLocation({
			to: "/$department/{-$category}",
			params: { department: "women", category: undefined },
		});
		expect(location.pathname).toBe("/women");
	});

	it("keeps static routes ahead of the optional department route", async () => {
		const router = routerFor("/deals");
		await router.load();
		expect(router.state.matches.at(-1)?.routeId).toBe("/deals");
		expect(loadCatalog).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
	});

	it("validates URL filters before loading", async () => {
		const router = routerFor("/men/shoes?q=boots&minPrice=5000");
		await router.load();
		expect(loadCatalog).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
			expect.any(QueryClient),
			{ department: "men", category: "shoes", mode: "listing" },
			{ ...DEFAULT_FILTERS, q: "boots", minPrice: 5000 },
		);
	});

	it("matches existing department URLs with a trailing slash", () => {
		const router = routerFor("/women/");
		expect(router.matchRoutes("/women/").at(-1)).toMatchObject({
			routeId: "/$department/{-$category}",
			params: { department: "women" },
		});
	});
});

===== ./src/features/catalog/product-route.test.ts =====

import { QueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import {
	createMemoryHistory,
	createRootRouteWithContext,
	createRoute,
	createRouter,
} from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import type { Route as RootRoute } from "#/routes/__root";

function routerFor(slug: string) {
	const loader: NonNullable<typeof RootRoute.options.loader> = async () => ({
		chatEmbedUrl: null,
		socialProviders: [],
	});
	const root = createRootRouteWithContext<{ queryClient: QueryClient }>()({
		loader,
	});

		getParentRoute: () => root,

			data: "missing",
			signal: expect.any(AbortSignal),
		});
	});
});

===== ./src/features/catalog/product-selection.test.ts =====

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { resolveSelection, type SelectableSku } from "./product-selection";

const sku = (overrides: Partial<SelectableSku>): SelectableSku => ({
	id: "id",
	color: "black",
	size: "40",
	imageUrl: null,
	price: 100,
	originalPrice: null,
	inStock: true,
	...overrides,
});

const skus = [
	sku({ id: "b40", color: "black", size: "40", price: 120 }),
	sku({ id: "b41", color: "black", size: "41", price: 100 }),
	sku({ id: "b42", color: "black", size: "42", inStock: false }),
	sku({ id: "w40", color: "white", size: "40", imageUrl: "/white.webp" }),
];

describe("resolveSelection", () => {
	it("defaults to the first color with stock and its cheapest SKU", () => {
		const s = resolveSelection(skus, {});
		expect(s.color).toBe("black");
		expect(s.selected).toBeUndefined();
		expect(s.shown?.id).toBe("b41");
	});

	it("honours a valid color and size", () => {
		const s = resolveSelection(skus, { color: "white", sku: "w40" });
		expect(s.color).toBe("white");
		expect(s.selected?.id).toBe("w40");
		expect(s.imageUrl).toBe("/white.webp");
	});

	it("falls back when the URL names an unknown color", () => {
		expect(resolveSelection(skus, { color: "pink" }).color).toBe("black");
	});

	it("ignores sold-out and cross-color SKUs", () => {
		expect(resolveSelection(skus, { sku: "b42" }).selected).toBeUndefined();
		expect(
			resolveSelection(skus, { color: "white", sku: "b40" }).selected,
		).toBeUndefined();
	});

	it("handles a product with no SKUs", () => {
		const s = resolveSelection([], {});
		expect(s.shown).toBeUndefined();
		expect(s.colors).toEqual([]);
	});
});


===== ./src/features/catalog/product-selection.ts =====

import { minBy, uniq } from "es-toolkit";

export type SelectableSku = {
	id: string;
	color: string;
	size: string;
	imageUrl: string | null;
	price: number;
	originalPrice: number | null;
	inStock: boolean;
};

type SkuChoice = { color?: string; sku?: string };

/**
 * Derives everything the product page renders from the SKU list and the
 * (URL-backed) choice. Invalid or stale choices silently fall back to a sane
 * default, so a hand-edited link can never produce a broken page.
 */
export function resolveSelection<T extends SelectableSku>(
	skus: readonly T[],
	choice: SkuChoice,
) {
	const colors = uniq(skus.map((s) => s.color));
	const color = colors.includes(choice.color ?? "")
		? choice.color
		: (skus.find((s) => s.inStock)?.color ?? colors[0]);

	const colorSkus = skus.filter((s) => s.color === color);
	const selected = colorSkus.find((s) => s.id === choice.sku && s.inStock);

	// Before a size is picked, advertise the cheapest available option.
	const shown =
		selected ??
		minBy(
			colorSkus.filter((s) => s.inStock),
			(s) => s.price,
		) ??
		colorSkus[0];

	return {
		colors,
		color,
		colorSkus,
		selected,
		shown,
		imageUrl: colorSkus.find((s) => s.imageUrl)?.imageUrl ?? null,
	};
}

export type SkuSelection<T extends SelectableSku> = ReturnType<
	typeof resolveSelection<T>
>;


===== ./src/features/catalog/product.server.ts =====

import { db } from "#/db";
import { sortSkus } from "./sizes";

export const findProductBySlug = async (slug: string) => {
	const found = await db.query.product.findFirst({
		where: { slug },
		columns: { url: false },
		with: { skus: { columns: { productId: false } } },
	});

	return found && { ...found, skus: sortSkus(found.skus, found.category) };
};


===== ./src/features/catalog/product.types.ts =====

/** One product in a listing, priced at its cheapest in-stock SKU. */
export type ProductListItem = {
	id: string;
	slug: string;
	name: string;
	brand: string | null;
	currency: string;
	price: number;
	originalPrice: number | null;
	imageUrl: string | null;
};

/** A listing item that is on sale, shown at its deepest-discounted SKU. */
export type Deal = ProductListItem & {
	originalPrice: number;
	/** Percent off, rounded. */
	discount: number;
};

/** A search hit: a listing item that also carries its product's best discount. */
export type CatalogItem = ProductListItem & { discount: number };

/** Narrows a hit to a real deal (the deals scope only ever returns these). */
export const isDeal = (item: CatalogItem): item is Deal =>
	item.originalPrice !== null && item.discount > 0;


===== ./src/features/catalog/sizes.test.ts =====

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { compareSizes, sortSkus } from "./sizes";

describe("compareSizes", () => {
	it("orders shoe sizes numerically, not lexically", () => {
		const sizes = ["9", "10", "38.5", "38"].toSorted((a, b) =>
			compareSizes("shoes", a, b),
		);
		expect(sizes).toEqual(["9", "10", "38", "38.5"]);
	});

	it("orders clothing sizes along the size scale", () => {
		const sizes = ["xl", "S", "xxs", "2XL", "m"].toSorted((a, b) =>
			compareSizes("clothing", a, b),
		);
		expect(sizes).toEqual(["xxs", "S", "m", "xl", "2XL"]);
	});

	it("puts unknown clothing sizes last", () => {
		const sizes = ["One Size", "m", "xs"].toSorted((a, b) =>
			compareSizes("clothing", a, b),
		);
		expect(sizes).toEqual(["xs", "m", "One Size"]);
	});
});

describe("sortSkus", () => {
	it("groups by color and then sorts sizes, without mutating the input", () => {
		const skus = [
			{ color: "red", size: "10" },
			{ color: "black", size: "9" },
			{ color: "red", size: "9" },
		];
		expect(sortSkus(skus, "shoes")).toEqual([
			{ color: "black", size: "9" },
			{ color: "red", size: "9" },
			{ color: "red", size: "10" },
		]);
		expect(skus[0]).toEqual({ color: "red", size: "10" });
	});
});


===== ./src/features/catalog/sizes.ts =====

import type { Category } from "#/data/catalog";

const CLOTHING_SIZES = ["xxs", "xs", "s", "m", "l", "xl", "2xl", "xxl", "3xl"];

/** Numeric rank used to order sizes within a category; unknown sizes sort last. */
const sizeRank: Record<Category, (size: string) => number> = {
	shoes: Number,
	clothing: (size) => {
		const index = CLOTHING_SIZES.indexOf(size.toLowerCase());
		return index === -1 ? Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY : index;
	},
};

export const compareSizes = (category: Category, a: string, b: string) =>
	sizeRank[category](a) - sizeRank[category](b) ||
	a.localeCompare(b, undefined, { numeric: true });

/** Orders SKUs by color, then by size using the category's size scale. */
export const sortSkus = <T extends { color: string; size: string }>(
	skus: readonly T[],
	category: Category,
) =>
	skus.toSorted(
		(a, b) =>
			a.color.localeCompare(b.color) || compareSizes(category, a.size, b.size),
	);
