===== ./src/features/search/catalog-doc.test.ts =====

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { buildCatalogDocs, type SourceProduct } from "./catalog-doc";

const product = (overrides: Partial<SourceProduct>): SourceProduct => ({
	id: "p",
	slug: "p",
	name: "Product",
	brand: "Nike",
	department: "men",
	category: "shoes",
	currency: "GEL",
	skus: [],
	...overrides,
});

const sku = (
	overrides: Partial<SourceProduct["skus"][number]> = {},
): SourceProduct["skus"][number] => ({
	color: "black",
	size: "40",
	price: 100,
	originalPrice: null,
	discount: 0,
	imageUrl: null,
	...overrides,
});

describe("buildCatalogDocs", () => {
	it("leaves out products with nothing in stock", () => {
		const docs = buildCatalogDocs([
			product({ id: "a", skus: [sku()] }),
			product({ id: "b", skus: [] }),
		]);
		expect(docs.map((d) => d.id)).toEqual(["a"]);
	});

	it("prices a listing at the cheapest SKU and a deal at the deepest discount", () => {
		const [doc] = buildCatalogDocs([
			product({
				skus: [
					sku({ size: "40", price: 5000 }),
					sku({ size: "41", price: 8000, originalPrice: 16000, discount: 50 }),
				],
			}),
		]);
		expect(doc?.price).toBe(5000);
		expect(doc?.discount).toBe(50);
		expect(doc?.deal).toEqual({
			price: 8000,
			originalPrice: 16000,
			imageUrl: null,
		});
	});

	it("has no deal when nothing is discounted", () => {
		const [doc] = buildCatalogDocs([product({ skus: [sku()] })]);
		expect(doc?.deal).toBeNull();
		expect(doc?.discount).toBe(0);
	});

	it("collects distinct colors and sizes as comma-separated tags, and searches brand and name together", () => {
		const [doc] = buildCatalogDocs([
			product({
				name: "Air Runner",
				skus: [
					sku({ color: "black", size: "40" }),
					sku({ color: "black", size: "41" }),
					sku({ color: "white", size: "40" }),
				],
			}),
		]);
		expect(doc?.colors).toBe("black,white");
		expect(doc?.sizes).toBe("40,41");
		expect(doc?.searchText).toBe("Nike Air Runner");
	});

	it("keeps commas inside a value from splitting it into two tags", () => {
		const [doc] = buildCatalogDocs([
			product({ skus: [sku({ color: "red, blue" })] }),
		]);
		expect(doc?.colors).toBe("red  blue");
	});

	it("omits a missing brand instead of writing null", () => {
		const [doc] = buildCatalogDocs([product({ brand: null, skus: [sku()] })]);
		expect(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(doc))).not.toHaveProperty("brand");
		expect(doc?.searchText).toBe("Product");
	});

	it("gives every product a unique sort key, even when prices tie", () => {
		const docs = buildCatalogDocs(
			["a", "b", "c"].map((id) =>
				product({ id, name: "Same", skus: [sku({ price: 100 })] }),
			),
		);
		for (const key of ["name", "price", "dealPrice", "discount"] as const) {
			expect(new Set(docs.map((d) => d.sort[key])).size).toBe(3);
		}
	});

	it("orders deals independently of the cheapest variant", () => {
		const docs = buildCatalogDocs([
			product({
				id: "a",
				skus: [
					sku({ price: 1000 }),
					sku({ price: 8000, originalPrice: 16000, discount: 50 }),
				],
			}),
			product({
				id: "b",
				skus: [sku({ price: 5000, originalPrice: 10000, discount: 50 })],
			}),
		]);
		expect(
			docs.toSorted((a, b) => a.sort.price - b.sort.price).map((doc) => doc.id),
		).toEqual(["a", "b"]);
		expect(
			docs
				.toSorted((a, b) => a.sort.dealPrice - b.sort.dealPrice)
				.map((doc) => doc.id),
		).toEqual(["b", "a"]);
	});

	it("ranks names alphabetically", () => {
		const docs = buildCatalogDocs([
			product({ id: "1", name: "Zed", skus: [sku()] }),
			product({ id: "2", name: "Alpha", skus: [sku()] }),
		]);
		const byName = docs.toSorted((a, b) => a.sort.name - b.sort.name);
		expect(byName.map((d) => d.name)).toEqual(["Alpha", "Zed"]);
	});
});


===== ./src/features/search/catalog-doc.ts =====

import type { Category, Department } from "#/data/catalog";

/** Bigger than any product count, so `value * SEQ + seq` stays unique and below 2^53. */
const SEQ = 1_000_000;

/**
 * One indexed product. Only in-stock SKUs are summarised, so a product with
 * nothing in stock never enters the index (matching the old listings).
 */
export type CatalogDoc = {
	id: string;
	slug: string;
	name: string;
	/** Omitted rather than null, so the TAG field simply isn't indexed. */
	brand?: string;
	/** Brand and name together, so "nike air" matches across both. */
	searchText: string;
	department: Department;
	category: Category;
	currency: string;
	/** Distinct colors, comma-separated, indexed as a multi-value TAG. */
	colors: string;
	/** Distinct sizes, comma-separated, indexed as a multi-value TAG. */
	sizes: string;
	/** Cheapest in-stock SKU: what listings show. */
	price: number;
	originalPrice: number | null;
	imageUrl: string | null;
	/** Deepest discount among in-stock SKUs; 0 means not on sale. */
	discount: number;
	/** Deepest-discounted SKU: what the deals views show. */
	deal: {
		price: number;
		originalPrice: number;
		imageUrl: string | null;
	} | null;
	/**
	 * Sort keys with a per-product sequence mixed in. A plain SORTBY with ties
	 * would let offset pages overlap or skip; these are unique, so they can't.
	 */
	sort: { name: number; price: number; dealPrice: number; discount: number };
};

type SourceSku = {
	color: string;
	size: string;
	price: number;
	originalPrice: number | null;
	discount: number;
	imageUrl: string | null;
};

export type SourceProduct = {
	id: string;
	slug: string;
	name: string;
	brand: string | null;
	department: Department;
	category: Category;
	currency: string;
	/** In-stock SKUs only. */
	skus: SourceSku[];
};

/** Distinct values as one TAG string; the index splits it on ",". */
const tagList = (values: string[]) =>
	[...new Set(values.map((value) => value.replaceAll(",", " ").trim()))]
		.filter(Boolean)
		.join(",");

/** Pure on purpose: it is the whole read model, and it is unit-tested. */
export function buildCatalogDocs(products: SourceProduct[]): CatalogDoc[] {
	const stocked = products.filter((p) => p.skus.length > 0);

	const nameRank = new Map(
		stocked
			.toSorted(
				(a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name) || a.id.localeCompare(b.id),
			)
			.map((p, rank) => [p.id, rank]),
	);

	return stocked
		.toSorted((a, b) => a.id.localeCompare(b.id))
		.map((p, seq): CatalogDoc => {
			const cheapest = p.skus.reduce((a, b) => (b.price < a.price ? b : a));
			const deepest = p.skus.reduce((a, b) =>
				b.discount > a.discount ? b : a,
			);
			const deal =
				deepest.discount > 0 && deepest.originalPrice !== null
					? {
							price: deepest.price,
							originalPrice: deepest.originalPrice,
							imageUrl: deepest.imageUrl,
						}
					: null;

			return {
				id: p.id,
				slug: p.slug,
				name: p.name,
				brand: p.brand ?? undefined,
				searchText: [p.brand, p.name].filter(Boolean).join(" "),
				department: p.department,
				category: p.category,
				currency: p.currency,
				colors: tagList(p.skus.map((s) => s.color)),
				sizes: tagList(p.skus.map((s) => s.size)),
				price: cheapest.price,
				originalPrice: cheapest.originalPrice,
				imageUrl: cheapest.imageUrl,
				discount: deepest.discount,
				deal,
				sort: {
					name: nameRank.get(p.id) ?? 0,
					price: cheapest.price * SEQ + seq,
					dealPrice: (deal?.price ?? cheapest.price) * SEQ + seq,
					discount: deepest.discount * SEQ + seq,
				},
			};
		});
}


===== ./src/features/search/catalog-fields.ts =====

/**
 * The search index, declared once. The FT.CREATE schema, the query builder and
 * `pnpm search:check` all read from here, so a field can't drift between them.
 *
 * `colors` and `sizes` are comma-separated strings indexed as TAG fields
 * with an explicit separator, shared by the query and facet builders.
 */
const FIELDS = {
	q: { path: "$.searchText", type: "TEXT" },
	brand: { path: "$.brand", type: "TAG" },
	department: { path: "$.department", type: "TAG" },
	category: { path: "$.category", type: "TAG" },
	colors: { path: "$.colors", type: "TAG", separator: "," },
	sizes: { path: "$.sizes", type: "TAG", separator: "," },
	price: { path: "$.price", type: "NUMERIC" },
	dealPrice: { path: "$.deal.price", type: "NUMERIC" },
	discount: { path: "$.discount", type: "NUMERIC" },
	sortName: { path: "$.sort.name", type: "NUMERIC" },
	sortPrice: { path: "$.sort.price", type: "NUMERIC" },
	sortDealPrice: { path: "$.sort.dealPrice", type: "NUMERIC" },
	sortDiscount: { path: "$.sort.discount", type: "NUMERIC" },
} as const satisfies Record<
	string,
	{ path: string; type: "TEXT" | "TAG" | "NUMERIC"; separator?: string }
>;

type FieldName = keyof typeof FIELDS;

export type TagField = {
	[K in FieldName]: (typeof FIELDS)[K]["type"] extends "TAG" ? K : never;
}[FieldName];

/** The tail of `FT.CREATE ... SCHEMA`: path, alias, type (and separator) for every field. */
export const schemaArgs = () =>
	Object.entries(FIELDS).flatMap(([name, field]) => [
		field.path,
		"AS",
		name,
		field.type,
		...("separator" in field ? ["SEPARATOR", field.separator] : []),
	]);


===== ./src/features/search/catalog-filters.test.ts =====

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import {
	type CatalogScope,
	catalogFiltersSchema,
	DEFAULT_FILTERS,
	effectiveSort,
	hasActiveFilters,
} from "./catalog-filters";

const listing: CatalogScope = { mode: "listing" };
const deals: CatalogScope = { mode: "deals" };

describe("catalogFiltersSchema", () => {
	it("defaults to no filters", () => {
		expect(DEFAULT_FILTERS).toEqual({
			brands: [],
			sizes: [],
			colors: [],
			sale: false,
		});
		expect(hasActiveFilters(DEFAULT_FILTERS)).toBe(false);
	});

	it("drops malformed values instead of failing", () => {
		const parsed = catalogFiltersSchema.parse({
			brands: "Nike",
			minPrice: "cheap",
			maxPrice: -5,
			sale: "yes",
			sort: "random",
			q: "air",
		});
		expect(parsed).toEqual({
			brands: [],
			sizes: [],
			colors: [],
			sale: false,
			q: "air",
		});
	});

	it("keeps well-formed values", () => {
		const parsed = catalogFiltersSchema.parse({
			brands: ["Nike"],
			minPrice: 5000,
			sort: "price-asc",
		});
		expect(parsed.brands).toEqual(["Nike"]);
		expect(parsed.minPrice).toBe(5000);
		expect(parsed.sort).toBe("price-asc");
		expect(hasActiveFilters(parsed)).toBe(true);
	});
});

describe("effectiveSort", () => {
	it("uses each page's natural order", () => {
		expect(effectiveSort(DEFAULT_FILTERS, listing)).toBe("name");
		expect(effectiveSort(DEFAULT_FILTERS, deals)).toBe("discount");
	});

	it("ranks a search by relevance unless told otherwise", () => {
		const searching = { ...DEFAULT_FILTERS, q: "air" };
		expect(effectiveSort(searching, listing)).toBe("relevance");
		expect(effectiveSort({ ...searching, sort: "price-asc" }, listing)).toBe(
			"price-asc",
		);
	});

	it("ignores relevance once the search is cleared", () => {
		expect(
			effectiveSort({ ...DEFAULT_FILTERS, sort: "relevance" }, listing),
		).toBe("name");
	});
});


===== ./src/features/search/catalog-filters.ts =====

import { z } from "zod";
import {
	categorySchema,
	departmentSchema,
} from "#/features/catalog/product.schema";

/** Products per page of a listing. */
export const CATALOG_PAGE_SIZE = 48;

/** Products in the home page's drops row. */
export const HOME_DEALS_COUNT = 12;

const sorts = [
	"relevance",
	"name",
	"price-asc",
	"price-desc",
	"discount",
] as const;
export type CatalogSort = (typeof sorts)[number];

export const sortLabels = {
	relevance: "Best match",
	name: "Name",
	"price-asc": "Price: low to high",
	"price-desc": "Price: high to low",
	discount: "Biggest discount",
} satisfies Record<CatalogSort, string>;

const list = (max: number) =>
	z.array(z.string().min(1).max(60)).max(max).default([]).catch([]);

const money = z.int().min(0).max(100_000_000);

/**
 * Everything a shopper can narrow a listing by. It is also the URL's search
 * schema: malformed values fall back to "no filter" instead of erroring, so a
 * hand-edited link can never produce a broken page. Prices are minor units.
 */
export const catalogFiltersSchema = z.object({
	q: z.string().trim().max(80).optional().catch(undefined),
	brands: list(20),
	sizes: list(30),
	colors: list(20),
	minPrice: money.optional().catch(undefined),
	maxPrice: money.optional().catch(undefined),
	sale: z.boolean().default(false).catch(false),
	sort: z.enum(sorts).optional().catch(undefined),
});
export type CatalogFilters = z.infer<typeof catalogFiltersSchema>;

export const DEFAULT_FILTERS: CatalogFilters = catalogFiltersSchema.parse({});

/** Where a listing lives: a department or category, or the deals view. */
export const scopeSchema = z.object({
	department: departmentSchema.optional(),
	category: categorySchema.optional(),
	/** "deals" shows each product at its deepest-discounted SKU. */
	mode: z.enum(["listing", "deals"]).default("listing"),
});
export type CatalogScope = z.infer<typeof scopeSchema>;

/** The sort in force: the shopper's pick, else relevance for a search, else the page's natural order. */
export const effectiveSort = (
	filters: CatalogFilters,
	scope: CatalogScope,
): CatalogSort => {
	if (filters.sort && (filters.sort !== "relevance" || filters.q)) {
		return filters.sort;
	}
	if (filters.q) return "relevance";
	return scope.mode === "deals" ? "discount" : "name";
};

export const hasActiveFilters = (f: CatalogFilters) =>
	Boolean(f.q) ||
	f.brands.length > 0 ||
	f.sizes.length > 0 ||
	f.colors.length > 0 ||
	f.minPrice != null ||
	f.maxPrice != null ||
	f.sale;


===== ./src/features/search/catalog.functions.ts =====

import { createServerFn } from "@tanstack/react-start";
import { z } from "zod";
import {
	CATALOG_PAGE_SIZE,
	catalogFiltersSchema,
	scopeSchema,
} from "./catalog-filters";
import { catalogFacets, searchCatalog } from "./catalog-search.server";

const searchInput = z.object({
	scope: scopeSchema,
	filters: catalogFiltersSchema,
	page: z.int().min(1).max(10_000).default(1),
	pageSize: z.int().min(1).max(CATALOG_PAGE_SIZE).default(CATALOG_PAGE_SIZE),
});

/** One page of a listing (a department, a category or the deals), already filtered and sorted. */
export const searchCatalogFn = createServerFn({ method: "GET" })
	.validator(searchInput)
	.handler(({ data }) =>
		searchCatalog(data.scope, data.filters, data.page, data.pageSize),
	);

/** What the filter bar offers: brands, sizes and colors with counts, and the price span. */
export const catalogFacetsFn = createServerFn({ method: "GET" })
	.validator(searchInput.pick({ scope: true, filters: true }))
	.handler(({ data }) => catalogFacets(data.scope, data.filters));

export type CatalogFacets = Awaited<ReturnType<typeof catalogFacetsFn>>;


===== ./src/features/search/catalog-index.server.test.ts =====

import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";

const { ft, findMany, redis } = vi.hoisted(() => ({
	ft: { list: vi.fn(), create: vi.fn(), dropIndex: vi.fn(), search: vi.fn() },
	findMany: vi.fn(),
	redis: { scan: vi.fn() },
}));
vi.mock("./redis-ft.server", () => ({ ft }));
vi.mock("#/db", () => ({ db: { query: { product: { findMany } } } }));
vi.mock("#/server/redis.server", () => ({ getRedis: async () => redis }));

beforeEach(() => {
	vi.resetModules();
	vi.resetAllMocks();
	findMany.mockResolvedValue([]);
	redis.scan.mockResolvedValue({ cursor: "0", keys: [] });
});

describe("catalog initialization", () => {
	it("rebuilds derived fields when a new schema indexes existing documents", async () => {
		ft.list.mockResolvedValue([]);
		ft.search.mockResolvedValue({ total: 10 });
		const { ensureCatalogReady } = await import("./catalog-index.server");
		await Promise.all([ensureCatalogReady(), ensureCatalogReady()]);
		expect(ft.create).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
		expect(findMany).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
	});

	it.each([0, 10])(
		"only refills an existing index when empty (count %s)",
		async (total) => {
			const { INDEX, ensureCatalogReady } = await import(
				"./catalog-index.server"
			);
			ft.list.mockResolvedValue([INDEX]);
			ft.search.mockResolvedValue({ total });
			await ensureCatalogReady();
			expect(ft.create).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
			expect(findMany).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(total === 0 ? 1 : 0);
		},
	);

	it("retries an interrupted rebuild on the next request", async () => {
		ft.list.mockResolvedValue([]);
		ft.search.mockResolvedValue({ total: 10 });
		findMany.mockRejectedValueOnce(new Error("database unavailable"));
		const { ensureCatalogReady } = await import("./catalog-index.server");
		await expect(ensureCatalogReady()).rejects.toThrow("database unavailable");
		await expect(ensureCatalogReady()).resolves.toBeUndefined();
		expect(findMany).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
	});
});


===== ./src/features/search/catalog-index.server.ts =====

import { createHash } from "node:crypto";
import { db } from "#/db";
import { getRedis } from "#/server/redis.server";
import { buildCatalogDocs } from "./catalog-doc";
import { schemaArgs } from "./catalog-fields";
import { ft } from "./redis-ft.server";

const FAMILY = "catalog_";
const KEY_PREFIX = "product:";

/** Named after its schema, so changing `FIELDS` simply creates a new index. */
export const INDEX = `${FAMILY}${createHash("sha1")
	.update(schemaArgs().join("|"))
	.digest("hex")
	.slice(0, 8)}`;

let indexReady: Promise<boolean> | undefined;

async function createIndex() {
	const existing = await ft.list();
	const created = !existing.includes(INDEX);
	if (created) {
		await ft.create(INDEX, KEY_PREFIX, schemaArgs());
	}
	// Indexes from an older schema only cost memory.
	await Promise.all(
		existing
			.filter((name) => name.startsWith(FAMILY) && name !== INDEX)
			.map((name) => ft.dropIndex(name)),
	);
	return created;
}

const ensureIndex = () => {
	indexReady ??= createIndex().catch((error: unknown) => {
		indexReady = undefined;
		throw error;
	});
	return indexReady;
};

/** Deletes indexed products that are no longer in the catalogue. */
async function removeStaleKeys(live: ReadonlySet<string>) {
	const redis = await getRedis();
	let cursor = "0";
	do {
		const { cursor: next, keys } = await redis.scan(cursor, {
			MATCH: `${KEY_PREFIX}*`,
			COUNT: 1_000,
		});
		cursor = String(next);
		const stale = keys.map(String).filter((key) => !live.has(key));
		if (stale.length > 0) await redis.del(stale);
	} while (cursor !== "0");
}

/**
 * Rebuilds the search index from Postgres and returns how many products it
 * holds. Safe to run any time and as often as you like: it overwrites in place.
 */
export async function syncCatalogIndex() {
	await ensureIndex();

	const rows = await db.query.product.findMany({
		columns: {
			id: true,
			slug: true,
			name: true,
			brand: true,
			department: true,
			category: true,
			currency: true,
		},
		with: {
			skus: {
				where: { inStock: true },
				columns: {
					color: true,
					size: true,
					price: true,
					originalPrice: true,
					discount: true,
					imageUrl: true,
				},
			},
		},
	});

	const docs = buildCatalogDocs(rows);
	const redis = await getRedis();

	for (let i = 0; i < docs.length; i += 500) {
		const batch = redis.multi();
		for (const doc of docs.slice(i, i + 500)) {
			batch.json.set(KEY_PREFIX + doc.id, "$", doc);
		}
		await batch.execAsPipeline();
	}

	await removeStaleKeys(new Set(docs.map((doc) => KEY_PREFIX + doc.id)));
	return docs.length;
}

let catalogReady: Promise<void> | undefined;

/**
 * Every search goes through here. It creates the index, and if Redis came up
 * empty (a fresh volume, a lost disk) it refills it from Postgres, so the
 * storefront heals itself instead of showing an empty catalogue.
 */
export const ensureCatalogReady = () => {
	catalogReady ??= (async () => {
		const created = await ensureIndex();
		const { total } = await ft.search(INDEX, "*", {
			offset: 0,
			limit: 0,
		});
		// Existing JSON documents also need new derived fields after a schema change.
		if (created || total === 0) await syncCatalogIndex();
	})().catch((error: unknown) => {
		catalogReady = undefined;
		throw error;
	});
	return catalogReady;
};


===== ./src/features/search/catalog.queries.ts =====

import {
	infiniteQueryOptions,
	keepPreviousData,
	type QueryClient,
	queryOptions,
} from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { isDeal } from "#/features/catalog/product.types";
import { nextPageParam } from "#/lib/pagination";
import { catalogFacetsFn, searchCatalogFn } from "./catalog.functions";
import {
	type CatalogFilters,
	type CatalogScope,
	DEFAULT_FILTERS,
	HOME_DEALS_COUNT,
} from "./catalog-filters";

export const catalogQuery = (scope: CatalogScope, filters: CatalogFilters) =>
	infiniteQueryOptions({
		queryKey: ["catalog", scope, filters],
		queryFn: ({ pageParam, signal }) =>
			searchCatalogFn({ data: { scope, filters, page: pageParam }, signal }),
		initialPageParam: 1,
		getNextPageParam: nextPageParam,
		staleTime: 60_000,
	});

export const catalogFacetsQuery = (
	scope: CatalogScope,
	filters: CatalogFilters,
) =>
	queryOptions({
		queryKey: ["catalog-facets", scope, filters],
		queryFn: ({ signal }) =>
			catalogFacetsFn({ data: { scope, filters }, signal }),
		staleTime: 60_000,
		// Keep the bar on screen while the next counts arrive.
		placeholderData: keepPreviousData,
	});

/** The home page's drops row: the biggest discounts, no filters. */
export const homeDealsQuery = queryOptions({
	queryKey: ["catalog", "home-deals"],
	queryFn: ({ signal }) =>
		searchCatalogFn({
			signal,
			data: {
				scope: { mode: "deals" },
				filters: DEFAULT_FILTERS,
				page: 1,
				pageSize: HOME_DEALS_COUNT,
			},
		}),
	select: (result) => result.items.filter(isDeal),
	staleTime: 60_000,
});

/** For route loaders: have both the first page and the facets cached before the page renders. */
export const loadCatalog = async (
	queryClient: QueryClient,
	scope: CatalogScope,
	filters: CatalogFilters,
) => {
	await Promise.all([
		queryClient.infiniteQuery(catalogQuery(scope, filters)),
		queryClient.query(catalogFacetsQuery(scope, filters)),
	]);
};


===== ./src/features/search/catalog-query.test.ts =====

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { schemaArgs } from "./catalog-fields";
import { DEFAULT_FILTERS } from "./catalog-filters";
import {
	buildQuery,
	escapeTag,
	expandFacet,
	facetOptions,
	sortOptions,
	textClause,
} from "./catalog-query";

const listing = { mode: "listing" } as const;

describe("escapeTag", () => {
	it("escapes spaces and punctuation but leaves letters and digits", () => {
		expect(escapeTag("New Balance")).toBe("New\\ Balance");
		expect(escapeTag("38.5")).toBe("38\\.5");
		expect(escapeTag("2xl")).toBe("2xl");
	});
});

describe("textClause", () => {
	it("turns words into one prefix clause each", () => {
		expect(textClause("Nike air")).toBe("@q:nike* @q:air*");
	});

	it("cannot carry query syntax", () => {
		expect(textClause('air) | @brand:{x} "')).toBe("@q:air* @q:brand*");
		expect(textClause("-(*)")).toBe("");
	});

	it("is empty for nothing searchable", () => {
		expect(textClause(undefined)).toBe("");
		expect(textClause("  a  ")).toBe("");
	});

	it("caps the number of terms", () => {
		expect(textClause("aa bb cc dd ee ff gg hh ii jj").split(" ")).toHaveLength(
			8,
		);
	});
});

describe("buildQuery", () => {
	it("matches everything when nothing narrows it", () => {
		expect(buildQuery(listing, DEFAULT_FILTERS)).toBe("*");
	});

	it("scopes to a department and category", () => {
		expect(
			buildQuery(
				{ ...listing, department: "men", category: "shoes" },
				DEFAULT_FILTERS,
			),
		).toBe("@department:{men} @category:{shoes}");
	});

	it("ORs values within a facet and ANDs facets together", () => {
		expect(
			buildQuery(listing, {
				...DEFAULT_FILTERS,
				brands: ["Nike", "New Balance"],
				sizes: ["41"],
				colors: ["black"],
			}),
		).toBe("@brand:{Nike | New\\ Balance} @sizes:{41} @colors:{black}");
	});

	it("renders open and closed price ranges", () => {
		expect(buildQuery(listing, { ...DEFAULT_FILTERS, minPrice: 5000 })).toBe(
			"@price:[5000 +inf]",
		);
		expect(buildQuery(listing, { ...DEFAULT_FILTERS, maxPrice: 9000 })).toBe(
			"@price:[-inf 9000]",
		);
		expect(
			buildQuery(listing, { ...DEFAULT_FILTERS, minPrice: 1, maxPrice: 2 }),
		).toBe("@price:[1 2]");
	});

	it("restricts to discounted products for the sale toggle and the deals scope", () => {
		expect(buildQuery(listing, { ...DEFAULT_FILTERS, sale: true })).toBe(
			"@discount:[1 +inf]",
		);
		expect(buildQuery({ mode: "deals" }, DEFAULT_FILTERS)).toBe(
			"@discount:[1 +inf]",
		);
	});

	it("filters deals by the displayed variant's price", () => {
		expect(
			buildQuery({ mode: "deals" }, { ...DEFAULT_FILTERS, maxPrice: 6000 }),
		).toBe("@dealPrice:[-inf 6000] @discount:[1 +inf]");
	});
});

describe("sortOptions / facetOptions / schemaArgs", () => {
	it("sorts on the unique keys", () => {
		expect(sortOptions("price-desc", listing)).toEqual({
			BY: "sortPrice",
			DIRECTION: "DESC",
		});
		expect(sortOptions("relevance", listing)).toBeUndefined();
	});

	it.each(["price-asc", "price-desc"] as const)(
		"sorts deals by their displayed price (%s)",
		(sort) => {
			expect(sortOptions(sort, { mode: "deals" })).toEqual({
				BY: "sortDealPrice",
				DIRECTION: sort === "price-asc" ? "ASC" : "DESC",
			});
		},
	);

	it("groups a facet by its own field", () => {
		expect(facetOptions("sizes").STEPS[0]).toMatchObject({
			type: "GROUPBY",
			properties: "@sizes",
			REDUCE: { type: "COUNT", AS: "count" },
		});
	});

	it("declares path, alias and type for each field", () => {
		const args = schemaArgs();
		expect(args.slice(0, 4)).toEqual(["$.searchText", "AS", "q", "TEXT"]);
	});

	it("gives the multi-value tags a comma separator", () => {
		const args = schemaArgs().join(" ");
		expect(args).toContain("$.sizes AS sizes TAG SEPARATOR ,");
		expect(args).toContain("$.colors AS colors TAG SEPARATOR ,");
	});
});

describe("expandFacet", () => {
	it("splits joined values and sums their counts", () => {
		expect(
			expandFacet(
				[
					{ sizes: "40,41", count: "2" },
					{ sizes: "41", count: "3" },
					{ sizes: "m", count: "1" },
				],
				"sizes",
				true,
			),
		).toEqual([
			{ value: "41", count: 5 },
			{ value: "40", count: 2 },
			{ value: "m", count: 1 },
		]);
	});

	it("keeps single-value fields whole and skips unreadable rows", () => {
		expect(
			expandFacet(
				[
					{ brand: "New Balance", count: "1" },
					{ count: "4" },
					{ brand: "Nike", count: "x" },
				],
				"brand",
				false,
			),
		).toEqual([{ value: "New Balance", count: 1 }]);
	});
});


===== ./src/features/search/catalog-query.ts =====

import type { FtSearchOptions } from "redis";
import type { TagField } from "./catalog-fields";
import type {
	CatalogFilters,
	CatalogScope,
	CatalogSort,
} from "./catalog-filters";

/** TAG values need punctuation and spaces escaped: "New Balance" becomes New\ Balance. */
export const escapeTag = (value: string) =>
	value.replace(/[^\p{L}\p{N}_]/gu, (char) => `\\${char}`);

const tag = (field: TagField, values: readonly string[]) =>
	values.length > 0 ? `@${field}:{${values.map(escapeTag).join(" | ")}}` : "";

/**
 * Shopper text becomes prefix terms ("nik air" matches Nike Air). Input is
 * reduced to letters and digits first, so it can never inject query syntax.
 * One-letter terms are dropped: prefix queries need at least two characters.
 * Each term is its own `@q:term*` clause; adjacent clauses are ANDed.
 */
export function textClause(q: string | undefined) {
	const terms = (q ?? "")
		.toLowerCase()
		.split(/[^\p{L}\p{N}]+/u)
		.filter((term) => term.length >= 2)
		.slice(0, 8);

	return terms.map((term) => `@q:${term}*`).join(" ");
}

/** The FT.SEARCH query for a scope and its filters. Clauses are ANDed by juxtaposition. */
export function buildQuery(scope: CatalogScope, filters: CatalogFilters) {
	const clauses = [
		scope.department && tag("department", [scope.department]),
		scope.category && tag("category", [scope.category]),
		textClause(filters.q),
		tag("brand", filters.brands),
		tag("sizes", filters.sizes),
		tag("colors", filters.colors),
		filters.minPrice != null || filters.maxPrice != null
			? `@${scope.mode === "deals" ? "dealPrice" : "price"}:[${filters.minPrice ?? "-inf"} ${filters.maxPrice ?? "+inf"}]`
			: "",
		filters.sale || scope.mode === "deals" ? "@discount:[1 +inf]" : "",
	].filter(Boolean);

	return clauses.length > 0 ? clauses.join(" ") : "*";
}

const SORT_OPTIONS = {
	relevance: undefined,
	name: { BY: "sortName", DIRECTION: "ASC" },
	"price-asc": { BY: "sortPrice", DIRECTION: "ASC" },
	"price-desc": { BY: "sortPrice", DIRECTION: "DESC" },
	discount: { BY: "sortDiscount", DIRECTION: "DESC" },
} satisfies Record<CatalogSort, FtSearchOptions["SORTBY"]>;

export function sortOptions(sort: CatalogSort, scope: CatalogScope) {
	const options = SORT_OPTIONS[sort];
	return scope.mode === "deals" && options?.BY === "sortPrice"
		? { ...options, BY: "sortDealPrice" }
		: options;
}

/** Count products per tag value using the Redis client's aggregation options. */
export const facetOptions = (field: TagField) => ({
	LOAD: `@${field}` as const,
	STEPS: [
		{
			type: "GROUPBY" as const,
			properties: `@${field}` as const,
			REDUCE: { type: "COUNT" as const, AS: "count" },
		},
		{
			type: "SORTBY" as const,
			BY: { BY: "@count" as const, DIRECTION: "DESC" as const },
		},
		{ type: "LIMIT" as const, from: 0, size: 1000 },
	],
});

/**
 * Turns facet rows into `{ value, count }` options. Multi-value TAG fields may
 * come back joined ("40,41") or one value per row; either way each value's
 * counts are summed, most common first.
 */
export function expandFacet(
	rows: Record<string, string>[],
	field: string,
	multiValue: boolean,
) {
	const totals = new Map<string, number>();
	for (const row of rows) {
		const raw = row[field];
		const count = Number(row.count);
		if (!raw || !Number.isFinite(count)) continue;
		for (const value of multiValue ? raw.split(",") : [raw]) {
			if (value) totals.set(value, (totals.get(value) ?? 0) + count);
		}
	}
	return [...totals]
		.map(([value, count]) => ({ value, count }))
		.toSorted((a, b) => b.count - a.count || a.value.localeCompare(b.value));
}


===== ./src/features/search/catalog-search.server.test.ts =====

import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { buildCatalogDocs } from "./catalog-doc";
import { DEFAULT_FILTERS } from "./catalog-filters";
import { catalogFacets, searchCatalog } from "./catalog-search.server";

const ft = vi.hoisted(() => ({ search: vi.fn(), aggregate: vi.fn() }));
vi.mock("./redis-ft.server", () => ({ ft }));
vi.mock("./catalog-index.server", () => ({
	INDEX: "test",
	ensureCatalogReady: vi.fn(),
}));

const docs = buildCatalogDocs([
	{
		id: "product",
		slug: "product",
		name: "Product",
		brand: null,
		department: "men",
		category: "shoes",
		currency: "GEL",
		skus: [
			{
				color: "black",
				size: "40",
				price: 5000,
				originalPrice: null,
				discount: 0,
				imageUrl: null,
			},
			{
				color: "white",
				size: "41",
				price: 8000,
				originalPrice: 16000,
				discount: 50,
				imageUrl: null,
			},
		],
	},
]);

	vi.clearAllMocks();
	ft.search.mockResolvedValue({ total: 1, docs });
	ft.aggregate.mockResolvedValue([]);
});

	it.each([
		["listing", 5000, "sortPrice"],
		["deals", 8000, "sortDealPrice"],
	] as const)(
		"keeps %s cards, sorting, and price bounds consistent",
		async (mode, price, sortField) => {

				scope,
				{ ...DEFAULT_FILTERS, sort: "price-asc" },
				1,
				48,
			);

				"test",

				expect.objectContaining({ sort: { BY: sortField, DIRECTION: "ASC" } }),
			);

			const facets = await catalogFacets(scope, DEFAULT_FILTERS);
			expect(facets.price).toEqual({ min: price, max: price });
			expect(ft.search).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith(
				"test",
				expect.any(String),
				expect.objectContaining({ sort: { BY: sortField, DIRECTION: "DESC" } }),
			);
		},
	);
});


===== ./src/features/search/catalog-search.server.ts =====

import type { Category } from "#/data/catalog";
import type { CatalogItem } from "#/features/catalog/product.types";
import { compareSizes } from "#/features/catalog/sizes";
import { pageOffset } from "#/lib/pagination";
import type { CatalogDoc } from "./catalog-doc";
import type { TagField } from "./catalog-fields";
import {
	type CatalogFilters,
	type CatalogScope,
	effectiveSort,
} from "./catalog-filters";
import { ensureCatalogReady, INDEX } from "./catalog-index.server";
import {
	buildQuery,
	expandFacet,
	facetOptions,
	sortOptions,
} from "./catalog-query";
import { ft } from "./redis-ft.server";

/** Listings show the cheapest SKU; the deals scope shows the deepest-discounted one. */
const toItem =
	(mode: CatalogScope["mode"]) =>
	(doc: CatalogDoc): CatalogItem => {
		const shown = mode === "deals" && doc.deal ? doc.deal : doc;
		return {
			id: doc.id,
			slug: doc.slug,
			name: doc.name,
			brand: doc.brand ?? null,
			currency: doc.currency,
			price: shown.price,
			originalPrice: shown.originalPrice,
			imageUrl: shown.imageUrl,
			discount: doc.discount,
		};
	};

/** One page of products for a scope and its filters, with the total match count. */
export async function searchCatalog(
	scope: CatalogScope,
	filters: CatalogFilters,
	page: number,
	pageSize: number,
) {
	await ensureCatalogReady();

	const offset = pageOffset(page, pageSize);
	const { total, docs } = await ft.search<CatalogDoc>(
		INDEX,
		buildQuery(scope, filters),
		{
			sort: sortOptions(effectiveSort(filters, scope), scope),
			offset,
			limit: pageSize,
		},
	);

	return {
		items: docs.map(toItem(scope.mode)),
		total,
		hasMore: offset + docs.length < total,
	};
}

type FacetOption = { value: string; count: number };

/** The filter each facet is driven by, which its own counts must ignore. */
const FACET_FILTER = {
	brand: "brands",
	sizes: "sizes",
	colors: "colors",
} as const satisfies Partial<Record<TagField, keyof CatalogFilters>>;

async function tagFacet(
	scope: CatalogScope,
	filters: CatalogFilters,
	field: keyof typeof FACET_FILTER,
): Promise<FacetOption[]> {
	// Counts ignore their own filter, so picking one value doesn't hide the rest.
	const query = buildQuery(scope, { ...filters, [FACET_FILTER[field]]: [] });
	const rows = await ft.aggregate(INDEX, query, facetOptions(field));

	return expandFacet(rows, field, field !== "brand");
}

/**
 * Cheapest and dearest price in the results, read from both ends of the
 * unique price sort key.
 */
async function priceBounds(scope: CatalogScope, filters: CatalogFilters) {
	const query = buildQuery(scope, {
		...filters,
		minPrice: undefined,
		maxPrice: undefined,
	});
	const edge = async (sort: "price-asc" | "price-desc") => {
		const { docs } = await ft.search<CatalogDoc>(INDEX, query, {
			sort: sortOptions(sort, scope),
			offset: 0,
			limit: 1,
		});
		const doc = docs[0];
		return doc && toItem(scope.mode)(doc).price;
	};

	const [min, max] = await Promise.all([edge("price-asc"), edge("price-desc")]);
	return min != null && max != null ? { min, max } : null;
}

/** Shoe sizes are numbers and clothing sizes are letters: numbers first, each on its own scale. */
const sizeKind = (size: string): Category =>
	Number.isNaN(Number(size)) ? "clothing" : "shoes";

const compareFacetSizes = (a: FacetOption, b: FacetOption) => {
	const [kindA, kindB] = [sizeKind(a.value), sizeKind(b.value)];
	if (kindA !== kindB) return kindA === "shoes" ? -1 : 1;
	return compareSizes(kindA, a.value, b.value);
};

/** What the filter bar offers for the current scope and filters. */
export async function catalogFacets(
	scope: CatalogScope,
	filters: CatalogFilters,
) {
	await ensureCatalogReady();

	const [brands, sizes, colors, price] = await Promise.all([
		tagFacet(scope, filters, "brand"),
		tagFacet(scope, filters, "sizes"),
		tagFacet(scope, filters, "colors"),
		priceBounds(scope, filters),
	]);

	return { brands, sizes: sizes.toSorted(compareFacetSizes), colors, price };
}


===== ./src/features/search/redis-ft.server.ts =====

import type { createClient, FtSearchOptions } from "redis";
import { getRedis } from "#/server/redis.server";

type SearchOptions = {
	sort?: FtSearchOptions["SORTBY"];
	offset: number;
	limit: number;
};

type AggregateOptions = NonNullable<
	Parameters<ReturnType<typeof createClient>["ft"]["aggregate"]>[2]
>;

/** Catalog Search operations; the Redis client owns command and reply encoding. */
export const ft = {
	list: async () => (await getRedis()).ft._list(),

	// The same schema arguments also determine the index's version hash.
	create: async (index: string, prefix: string, schema: string[]) =>
		(await getRedis()).sendCommand([
			"FT.CREATE",
			index,
			"ON",
			"JSON",
			"PREFIX",
			"1",
			prefix,
			"SCHEMA",
			...schema,
		]),

	dropIndex: async (index: string) => (await getRedis()).ft.dropIndex(index),

	async search<T>(index: string, query: string, options: SearchOptions) {
		const { total, documents } = await (await getRedis()).ft.search(
			index,
			query,
			{
				RETURN: "$",
				SORTBY: options.sort,
				LIMIT: { from: options.offset, size: options.limit },
				DIALECT: 2,
			},
		);
		return { total, docs: documents.map(({ value }) => value as T) };
	},

	async aggregate(index: string, query: string, options: AggregateOptions) {
		const { results } = await (await getRedis()).ft.aggregate(index, query, {
			...options,
			DIALECT: 2,
		});
		return results;
	},
};


===== ./src/features/stats/format-checked-at.test.ts =====

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { formatCheckedAt, priceCheckNote } from "./format-checked-at";

// Asia/Tbilisi is UTC+4 all year.
describe("formatCheckedAt", () => {
	it("renders the time in the store's zone", () => {
		expect(formatCheckedAt("2026-10-23T04:00:00Z")).toBe("08:00");
		expect(formatCheckedAt("2026-10-22T20:30:00Z")).toBe("00:30");
	});
});

describe("priceCheckNote", () => {
	const now = new Date("2026-10-23T09:00:00Z");

	it("says today when the check ran on the store's current day", () => {
		expect(priceCheckNote("2026-10-23T04:05:00Z", now)).toBe(
			"Prices last checked today at 08:05.",
		);
	});

	it("names the date when the last check is older", () => {
		expect(priceCheckNote("2026-10-22T04:05:00Z", now)).toBe(
			"Prices last checked on 22 Oct at 08:05.",
		);
	});

	it("compares days in the store's zone, not UTC", () => {
		// 21:00 UTC on the 22nd is 01:00 on the 23rd in Tbilisi.
		expect(priceCheckNote("2026-10-22T21:00:00Z", now)).toBe(
			"Prices last checked today at 01:00.",
		);
	});

	it("falls back to the schedule before the first run", () => {
		expect(priceCheckNote(null, now)).toBe(
			"New prices land every morning at 08:00.",
		);
	});
});


===== ./src/features/stats/format-checked-at.ts =====

import { site } from "#/data/site";

// Pinned zone and locale, so the server and the browser render the same text.
const timeFormat = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-GB", {
	hour: "2-digit",
	minute: "2-digit",
	timeZone: site.timezone,
});

const dayKeyFormat = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-CA", {
	year: "numeric",
	month: "2-digit",
	day: "2-digit",
	timeZone: site.timezone,
});

const shortDateFormat = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-GB", {
	day: "numeric",
	month: "short",
	timeZone: site.timezone,
});

/** `"08:00"`, in the store's own time zone, from an ISO timestamp. */
export const formatCheckedAt = (iso: string) =>
	timeFormat.format(new Date(iso));

/**
 * One sentence about the last price check, true even if the importer missed
 * today: "Prices last checked today at 08:00." / "...on 22 Oct at 08:00."
 */
export function priceCheckNote(checkedAt: string | null, now = new Date()) {
	if (!checkedAt) {
		return `New prices land every morning at ${site.priceCheckTime}.`;
	}

	const at = new Date(checkedAt);
	const when =
		dayKeyFormat.format(at) === dayKeyFormat.format(now)
			? "today"
			: `on ${shortDateFormat.format(at)}`;

	return `Prices last checked ${when} at ${formatCheckedAt(checkedAt)}.`;
}

===== ./src/features/stats/stats.schema.ts =====

import { z } from "zod";

/** Numbers for the home hero and /deals, from the latest price check. */
export const statsSchema = z.object({
	deals: z.number().int().nonnegative(),
	warehouses: z.number().int().nonnegative(),
	/** ISO timestamp of when prices were last checked. */
	checkedAt: z.string().nullable(),
});

export type Stats = z.infer<typeof statsSchema>;


===== ./src/features/stats/stats.server.ts =====

import { countDistinct, max } from "drizzle-orm";
import { db } from "#/db";
import { onSale } from "#/db/predicates";
import { product, skus } from "#/db/schema";
import { createJsonCache } from "#/server/json-cache.server";
import { type Stats, statsSchema } from "./stats.schema";

async function computeStats(): Promise<Stats> {
	const [[dealRow], [statRow]] = await Promise.all([
		db
			.select({ count: countDistinct(skus.productId) })
			.from(skus)
			.where(onSale),
		db
			.select({
				warehouses: countDistinct(product.warehouse),
				checkedAt: max(product.lastSeenAt),
			})
			.from(product),
	]);

	return {
		deals: dealRow?.count ?? 0,
		warehouses: statRow?.warehouses ?? 0,
		checkedAt: statRow?.checkedAt?.toISOString() ?? null,
	};
}

const statsCache = createJsonCache({
	key: "stats",
	ttlSeconds: 60 * 60 * 24,
	schema: statsSchema,
	compute: computeStats,
});

/** Cached stats, queried from Postgres if Redis is empty or down. */
export const getStats = (): Promise<Stats> => statsCache.get();

/** @public Recomputes and overwrites the cache. For the importer to call after a run. */
export const refreshStats = (): Promise<Stats> => statsCache.refresh();


===== ./src/hooks/use-debounced-callback.ts =====

import { debounce } from "es-toolkit";
import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef } from "react";

/**
 * A stable debounced function that always calls the latest `fn`, and drops a
 * pending call on unmount. `.flush()` runs a pending call now (on Enter).
 */
export function useDebouncedCallback<A extends unknown[]>(
	fn: (...args: A) => void,
	wait: number,
) {
	const latest = useRef(fn);
	useEffect(() => {
		latest.current = fn;
	});

	const debounced = useMemo(
		() => debounce((...args: A) => latest.current(...args), wait),
		[wait],
	);
	useEffect(() => () => debounced.cancel(), [debounced]);

	return debounced;
}


===== ./src/hooks/use-scroll-edges.ts =====

import { type RefObject, useEffect, useState } from "react";

/** Whether the scroller can still move left / right, kept in sync with its scroll. */
export function useScrollEdges(ref: RefObject<HTMLElement | null>) {
	const [edges, setEdges] = useState({ atStart: true, atEnd: false });

	useEffect(() => {
		const el = ref.current;
		if (!el) return;

		const update = () =>
			setEdges({
				atStart: el.scrollLeft <= 1,
				atEnd: el.scrollLeft + el.clientWidth >= el.scrollWidth - 1,
			});

		update();
		el.addEventListener("scroll", update, { passive: true });
		const observer = new ResizeObserver(update);
		observer.observe(el);

		return () => {
			el.removeEventListener("scroll", update);
			observer.disconnect();
		};
	}, [ref]);

	return edges;
}

import type {

declare module "@better-auth-ui/core" {

/** Props the shadcn `<Auth>` router spreads onto plugin-contributed auth views. */
export type AuthViewProps = {
	className?: string;
	socialLayout?: "auto" | "horizontal" | "vertical" | "grid";
	socialPosition?: "top" | "bottom";
};

};

	AuthPluginComponents,

/** Inline `<script>` descriptor for the Tawk.to live-chat loader. */
export const chatWidgetScript = (embedUrl: string) => ({
	children: `
var Tawk_API=Tawk_API||{}, Tawk_LoadStart=new Date();
(function(){
	var s1=document.createElement("script"),s0=document.getElementsByTagName("script")[0];
	s1.async=true;
	s1.src=${JSON.stringify(embedUrl)};
	s1.charset="UTF-8";
	s1.setAttribute("crossorigin","*");
	s0.parentNode.insertBefore(s1,s0);
})();`,
});

declare global {
	interface Window {
		Tawk_API?: { maximize?: () => void };
	}
}

/** Opens the Tawk.to chat window (a no-op until the widget has loaded). */
export const openChat = () => window.Tawk_API?.maximize?.();


===== ./src/lib/pagination.test.ts =====

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { nextPageParam, pageOffset } from "./pagination";

describe("pageOffset", () => {
	it("starts the first page at zero", () => {
		expect(pageOffset(1, 48)).toBe(0);
		expect(pageOffset(3, 48)).toBe(96);
	});
});

describe("nextPageParam", () => {
	it("advances only while more pages exist", () => {
		expect(nextPageParam({ hasMore: true }, [], 2)).toBe(3);
		expect(nextPageParam({ hasMore: false }, [], 2)).toBeUndefined();
	});
});


===== ./src/lib/pagination.ts =====

/** Rows to skip so a 1-based `page` starts in the right place. */
export const pageOffset = (page: number, pageSize: number) =>
	(page - 1) * pageSize;

/** `getNextPageParam` for 1-based page infinite queries. */
export const nextPageParam = (
	last: { hasMore: boolean },
	_pages: unknown,
	lastPageParam: number,
) => (last.hasMore ? lastPageParam + 1 : undefined);


===== ./src/lib/price.test.ts =====

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { formatPrice, formatPriceCompact, isOnSale } from "./price";

describe("formatPrice", () => {
	it("formats minor units with the narrow currency symbol", () => {
		expect(formatPrice(5990, "GEL")).toBe("₾59.90");
		expect(formatPrice(5900)).toBe("₾59.00");
	});
});

describe("formatPriceCompact", () => {
	it("drops decimals only for whole amounts", () => {
		expect(formatPriceCompact(5900, "GEL")).toBe("₾59");
		expect(formatPriceCompact(5990, "GEL")).toBe("₾59.90");
	});

	it("uses the given currency", () => {
		expect(formatPriceCompact(1000, "USD")).toBe("$10");
	});
});

describe("isOnSale", () => {
	it("needs an original price above the current one", () => {
		expect(isOnSale(100, 150)).toBe(true);
		expect(isOnSale(100, 100)).toBe(false);
		expect(isOnSale(100, null)).toBe(false);
	});
});


===== ./src/lib/price.ts =====

import { site } from "#/data/site";

const formatters = new Map<string, Intl.NumberFormat>();

function getFormatter(currency: string, whole: boolean) {
	const key = `${currency}:${whole}`;
	let formatter = formatters.get(key);
	if (!formatter) {
		formatter = new Intl.NumberFormat("en-US", {
			style: "currency",
			currency,
			currencyDisplay: "narrowSymbol",
			...(whole && { minimumFractionDigits: 0, maximumFractionDigits: 0 }),
		});
		formatters.set(key, formatter);
	}
	return formatter;
}

/** Formats an amount in minor units (e.g. tetri) as a localized currency string. */
export function formatPrice(
	minorUnits: number,
	currency: string = site.currency,
) {
	return getFormatter(currency, false).format(minorUnits / 100);
}

/** Like `formatPrice`, but whole amounts lose their decimals: ₾59 and ₾59.90. */
export function formatPriceCompact(
	minorUnits: number,
	currency: string = site.currency,
) {
	return getFormatter(currency, minorUnits % 100 === 0).format(
		minorUnits / 100,
	);
}

export const isOnSale = (
	price: number,
	originalPrice: number | null,
): originalPrice is number => originalPrice !== null && originalPrice > price;


===== ./src/lib/route-guards.test.ts =====

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { z } from "zod";
import { parseParam } from "./route-guards";

const schema = z.enum(["men", "women"]);

describe("parseParam", () => {
	it("returns the parsed value", () => {
		expect(parseParam(schema, "men")).toBe("men");
	});

	it("turns a bad value into a 404", () => {
		let thrown: unknown;
		try {
			parseParam(schema, "dogs");
		} catch (error) {
			thrown = error;
		}
		expect(thrown).toMatchObject({ isNotFound: true });
	});
});


===== ./src/lib/route-guards.ts =====

import { notFound } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import type { z } from "zod";

/** Parses a route param (or any untrusted value) and turns failures into a 404. */
export function parseParam<S extends z.ZodType>(
	schema: S,
	value: unknown,
): z.output<S> {
	const { success, data } = schema.safeParse(value);
	if (!success) throw notFound();
	return data;
}


===== ./src/lib/utils.ts =====

import { type ClassValue, clsx } from "clsx";
import { twMerge } from "tailwind-merge";

export function cn(...inputs: ClassValue[]) {
	return twMerge(clsx(inputs));
}


===== ./src/router.tsx =====

import { QueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { createRouter as createTanStackRouter } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { setupRouterSsrQueryIntegration } from "@tanstack/react-router-ssr-query";
import {
	PageError,
	PageNotFound,
	PagePending,
} from "./components/route-feedback";
import { routeTree } from "./routeTree.gen";

export function getRouter() {
	const queryClient = new QueryClient();

	const router = createTanStackRouter({
		routeTree,
		context: { queryClient },
		scrollRestoration: true,
		defaultPreload: "intent",
		defaultPreloadStaleTime: 0,
		defaultErrorComponent: PageError,
		defaultNotFoundComponent: PageNotFound,
		defaultPendingComponent: PagePending,
	});

	setupRouterSsrQueryIntegration({ router, queryClient });

	return router;
}

declare module "@tanstack/react-router" {
	interface Register {
		router: ReturnType<typeof getRouter>;
	}
}


===== ./src/routes/$department.{-$category}.tsx =====

import { createFileRoute, stripSearchParams } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { Container } from "#/components/layout/container";
import { categories, departments } from "#/data/catalog";
import { site } from "#/data/site";
import { CatalogListing } from "#/features/catalog/components/catalog-listing";
import { DepartmentBreadcrumbs } from "#/features/catalog/components/department-breadcrumbs";
import {
	categorySchema,
	departmentSchema,
} from "#/features/catalog/product.schema";
import { loadCatalog } from "#/features/search/catalog.queries";
import {
	catalogFiltersSchema,
	DEFAULT_FILTERS,
} from "#/features/search/catalog-filters";
import { parseParam } from "#/lib/route-guards";

export const Route = createFileRoute("/$department/{-$category}")({
	beforeLoad: ({ params }) => {
		const department = parseParam(departmentSchema, params.department);
		const category = parseParam(categorySchema.optional(), params.category);
		return {
			scope: { department, category, mode: "listing" as const },
			title: category
				? `${departments[department].label} ${categories[category].label.toLowerCase()}`
				: departments[department].label,
		};
	},
	validateSearch: catalogFiltersSchema,
	search: { middlewares: [stripSearchParams(DEFAULT_FILTERS)] },
	loaderDeps: ({ search }) => ({ search }),
	loader: ({ context, deps }) =>
		loadCatalog(context.queryClient, context.scope, deps.search),
	head: ({ match }) => ({
		meta: [{ title: `${match.context.title} | ${site.name}` }],
	}),
	component: DepartmentPage,
});

function DepartmentPage() {
	const { scope, title } = Route.useRouteContext();
	const filters = Route.useSearch();
	const navigate = Route.useNavigate();

	return (
		<Container className="py-8 md:py-12">
			<DepartmentBreadcrumbs
				department={scope.department}
				category={scope.category}
			/>
			<h1 className="type-title mt-7 text-3xl md:text-5xl">{title}</h1>
			<CatalogListing
				key={`${scope.department}/${scope.category ?? ""}`}
				scope={scope}
				filters={filters}
				onFiltersChange={(search) =>
					navigate({ search, replace: true, resetScroll: false })
				}
			/>
		</Container>
	);
}

===== ./src/routes/auth/$path.tsx =====

import { viewPaths } from "@better-auth-ui/core";
import { createFileRoute, redirect } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { Auth } from "#/components/auth/auth";
import { site } from "#/data/site";

const validPaths = new Set<string>(Object.values(viewPaths.auth));

export const Route = createFileRoute("/auth/$path")({
	beforeLoad: ({ params }) => {
		if (params.path === viewPaths.auth.resetLinkSent) {
			throw redirect({
				to: "/auth/$path",
				params: { path: viewPaths.auth.resetPassword },
			});
		}
		if (!validPaths.has(params.path)) throw redirect({ to: "/" });
	},
	head: () => ({
		meta: [
			{ title: `Sign in | ${site.name}` },
			{ name: "robots", content: "noindex" },
		],
	}),
	component: () => (
		<div className="flex justify-center px-5 py-12 sm:px-8 md:py-20">
			<Auth path={Route.useParams().path} />
		</div>
	),
});

===== ./src/routes/deals.tsx =====

import { useSuspenseQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { createFileRoute, stripSearchParams } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { Container } from "#/components/layout/container";
import { site } from "#/data/site";
import { CatalogListing } from "#/features/catalog/components/catalog-listing";
import { loadCatalog } from "#/features/search/catalog.queries";
import {
	type CatalogScope,
	catalogFiltersSchema,
	DEFAULT_FILTERS,
} from "#/features/search/catalog-filters";
import { priceCheckNote } from "#/features/stats/format-checked-at";
import { statsQuery } from "#/features/stats/stats.queries";

const scope: CatalogScope = { mode: "deals" };

export const Route = createFileRoute("/deals")({
	validateSearch: catalogFiltersSchema,
	search: { middlewares: [stripSearchParams(DEFAULT_FILTERS)] },
	loaderDeps: ({ search }) => ({ search }),
	loader: async ({ context, deps }) => {
		await Promise.all([
			loadCatalog(context.queryClient, scope, deps.search),
			context.queryClient.query(statsQuery),
		]);
	},
	head: () => ({ meta: [{ title: `Deals | ${site.name}` }] }),
	component: DealsPage,
});

function DealsPage() {
	const filters = Route.useSearch();
	const navigate = Route.useNavigate();
	const { data: stats } = useSuspenseQuery(statsQuery);

	return (
		<Container className="py-10 md:py-14">
			<h1 className="type-title text-3xl md:text-5xl">All deals</h1>
			<p className="mt-4 max-w-2xl text-sm leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground sm:text-base">
				Everything on sale right now. {priceCheckNote(stats.checkedAt)}
			</p>

			<CatalogListing
				scope={scope}
				filters={filters}
				onFiltersChange={(search) =>
					navigate({ search, replace: true, resetScroll: false })
				}
				empty={
					<p className="py-10 text-muted-foreground">
						No deals right now. New prices land every morning at{" "}
						{site.priceCheckTime}.
					</p>
				}
			/>
		</Container>
	);
}

import { useSuspenseQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";

===== ./src/routes/product.$slug.tsx =====

import { createFileRoute, notFound } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { z } from "zod";
import { site } from "#/data/site";
import { ProductDetails } from "#/features/catalog/components/product-details";
import { getProductFn } from "#/features/catalog/product.functions";
import { productSlugSchema } from "#/features/catalog/product.schema";
import { resolveSelection } from "#/features/catalog/product-selection";
import { parseParam } from "#/lib/route-guards";

/**
 * The chosen color and size live in the URL: links are shareable, the server
 * renders the right variant, and there is no local state to reset between
 * products. Malformed values are dropped rather than rejected.
 */
const searchSchema = z.object({
	color: z.string().optional().catch(undefined),
	sku: z.uuid().optional().catch(undefined),
});

export const Route = createFileRoute("/product/$slug")({
	validateSearch: searchSchema,
	loader: async ({ params, abortController }) => {
		const product = await getProductFn({
			data: parseParam(productSlugSchema, params.slug),
			signal: abortController.signal,
		});
		if (!product) throw notFound();
		return product;
	},
	// Picking a variant only changes the search string; don't refetch for it.
	staleTime: 30_000,
	head: ({ loaderData }) => ({
		meta: [
			{
				title: loaderData?.name
					? `${loaderData.name} | ${site.name}`
					: site.name,
			},
			...(loaderData?.description
				? [{ name: "description", content: loaderData.description }]
				: []),
		],
	}),
	component: ProductPage,
});

function ProductPage() {
	const product = Route.useLoaderData();
	const search = Route.useSearch();
	const navigate = Route.useNavigate();

	return (
		<ProductDetails
			product={product}
			selection={resolveSelection(product.skus, search)}
			onColorChange={(color) =>
				navigate({ search: { color }, replace: true, resetScroll: false })
			}
			onSkuChange={(sku) =>
				navigate({
					search: (prev) => ({ ...prev, sku }),
					replace: true,
					resetScroll: false,
				})
			}
		/>
	);
}


===== ./src/routes/__root.tsx =====

import { TanStackDevtools } from "@tanstack/react-devtools";
import type { QueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import {
	createRootRouteWithContext,
	HeadContent,
	Outlet,
	Scripts,
} from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { TanStackRouterDevtoolsPanel } from "@tanstack/react-router-devtools";
import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import { SiteLayout } from "#/components/layout/site-layout";
import { Providers } from "#/components/providers";
import { site } from "#/data/site";
import { publicConfigQuery } from "#/features/config/config.queries";
import { chatWidgetScript } from "#/lib/chat-widget";
import appCss from "../styles.css?url";

export const Route = createRootRouteWithContext<{
	queryClient: QueryClient;
}>()({
	loader: ({ context }) => context.queryClient.query(publicConfigQuery),
	head: ({ loaderData }) => ({
		meta: [
			{ charSet: "utf-8" },
			{ name: "viewport", content: "width=device-width, initial-scale=1" },
			{ title: `${site.name} | ${site.tagline}` },
			{ name: "description", content: site.description },
		],
		links: [
			{ rel: "icon", href: "/favicon.ico", sizes: "32x32" },
			{ rel: "apple-touch-icon", href: "/apple-icon-180x180.png" },
			{ rel: "stylesheet", href: appCss },
		],
		scripts: loaderData?.chatEmbedUrl
			? [chatWidgetScript(loaderData.chatEmbedUrl)]
			: [],
	}),
	component: RootLayout,
	shellComponent: RootDocument,
});

function RootLayout() {
	return (
		<Providers>
			<SiteLayout>
				<Outlet />
			</SiteLayout>
		</Providers>
	);
}

// Keep the document independent of loader data so root errors can render.
function RootDocument({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
	return (
		<html lang="en" className="scrollbar-gutter-stable">
			<head>
				<HeadContent />
			</head>
			<body>
				{children}
				<TanStackDevtools
					config={{ position: "bottom-left" }}
					plugins={[
						{
							name: "Tanstack Router",
							render: <TanStackRouterDevtoolsPanel />,
						},
					]}
				/>
				<Scripts />
			</body>
		</html>
	);
}


===== ./src/routes/settings/$path.tsx =====

import { viewPaths } from "@better-auth-ui/core";
import { useAuthenticate } from "@better-auth-ui/react";
import { createFileRoute, notFound } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { Settings } from "#/components/auth/settings/settings";
import { site } from "#/data/site";
import { authClient } from "#/features/auth/auth-client";

const validPaths = new Set<string>(Object.values(viewPaths.settings));

export const Route = createFileRoute("/settings/$path")({
	beforeLoad: ({ params }) => {
		if (!validPaths.has(params.path)) throw notFound();
	},
	head: () => ({
		meta: [
			{ title: `Settings | ${site.name}` },
			{ name: "robots", content: "noindex" },
		],
	}),
	component: () => {

			return (

			);
		}

		return (
			<div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-3xl px-5 py-10 sm:px-8 md:py-16">
				<Settings path={path} />
			</div>
		);
	},
});


===== ./src/routes/sitemap[.]xml.ts =====

import { createFileRoute } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { desc } from "drizzle-orm";
import { escape as escapeXml } from "es-toolkit";
import { categorySlugs, departmentSlugs } from "#/data/catalog";
import { db } from "#/db";
import { product } from "#/db/schema";
import { env } from "#/server/env.server";

/** Per the sitemap protocol, one file lists at most 50,000 URLs. */
const MAX_SITEMAP_URLS = 50_000;

const staticPaths = [
	"/",
	"/deals",
	...departmentSlugs.flatMap((department) => [
		`/${department}`,
		...categorySlugs.map((category) => `/${department}/${category}`),
	]),
];

const urlEntry = (path: string, lastModified?: Date) =>
	`  <url><loc>${escapeXml(env.SITE_URL + path)}</loc>${
		lastModified ? `<lastmod>${lastModified.toISOString()}</lastmod>` : ""
	}</url>`;

export const Route = createFileRoute("/sitemap.xml")({
	server: {
		handlers: {
			GET: async () => {
				const products = await db
					.select({ slug: product.slug, updatedAt: product.updatedAt })
					.from(product)
					.orderBy(desc(product.updatedAt))
					.limit(MAX_SITEMAP_URLS - staticPaths.length);

				const entries = [
					...staticPaths.map((path) => urlEntry(path)),
					...products.map((p) =>
						urlEntry(`/product/${encodeURIComponent(p.slug)}`, p.updatedAt),
					),
				];

				const xml = `<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<urlset xmlns="http://www.sitemaps.org/schemas/sitemap/0.9">
${entries.join("\n")}
</urlset>`;

				return new Response(xml, {
					headers: {
						"Content-Type": "application/xml",
						"Cache-Control": "public, max-age=3600",
					},
				});
			},
		},
	},
});


===== ./src/server/env.server.test.ts =====

import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";

// env.server.ts validates process.env when imported, so give it a valid one.
vi.stubEnv("BETTER_AUTH_SECRET", "x".repeat(32));

const { parseEnv } = await import("./env.server");

const base = { BETTER_AUTH_SECRET: "x".repeat(32) };

describe("parseEnv", () => {
	it("defaults Redis locally and accepts an explicit host and port", () => {
		expect(parseEnv(base)).toMatchObject({
			REDIS_HOST: "localhost",
			REDIS_PORT: 6379,
		});
		expect(
			parseEnv({ ...base, REDIS_HOST: "redis", REDIS_PORT: "6380" }),
		).toMatchObject({ REDIS_HOST: "redis", REDIS_PORT: 6380 });
		expect(() => parseEnv({ ...base, REDIS_PORT: "invalid" })).toThrow();
		for (const port of ["0", "-1", "65536", "6379.5"]) {
			expect(() => parseEnv({ ...base, REDIS_PORT: port })).toThrow();
		}
	});

	it("needs only the auth secret in development", () => {
		const env = parseEnv(base);
		expect(env.GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID).toBeUndefined();
		expect(env.RESEND_API_KEY).toBeUndefined();
	});

	it("treats blank values as unset", () => {
		const env = parseEnv({
			...base,
			GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID: "  ",
			CHAT_EMBED_URL: "",
		});
		expect(env.GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID).toBeUndefined();
		expect(env.CHAT_EMBED_URL).toBeUndefined();
	});

	it("falls back to BETTER_AUTH_URL for SITE_URL", () => {
		const env = parseEnv({ ...base, BETTER_AUTH_URL: "https://2o.ge" });
		expect(env.SITE_URL).toBe("https://2o.ge");
		expect(
			parseEnv({
				...base,
				BETTER_AUTH_URL: "https://2o.ge",
				SITE_URL: "https://www.2o.ge",
			}).SITE_URL,
		).toBe("https://www.2o.ge");
	});

	it("rejects half-configured social providers", () => {
		expect(() => parseEnv({ ...base, GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID: "id" })).toThrow(
			/GOOGLE/,
		);
		expect(() => parseEnv({ ...base, FACEBOOK_CLIENT_SECRET: "s" })).toThrow(
			/FACEBOOK/,
		);
		expect(() =>
			parseEnv({ ...base, GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID: "id", GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET: "s" }),
		).not.toThrow();
	});

	it("requires an email key in production only", () => {
		expect(() => parseEnv({ ...base, NODE_ENV: "production" })).toThrow(
			/RESEND_API_KEY/,
		);
		expect(() =>
			parseEnv({ ...base, NODE_ENV: "production", RESEND_API_KEY: "re_1" }),
		).not.toThrow();
	});

	it("rejects a short auth secret and a malformed URL", () => {
		expect(() => parseEnv({ BETTER_AUTH_SECRET: "short" })).toThrow();
		expect(() => parseEnv({ ...base, CHAT_EMBED_URL: "not a url" })).toThrow();
	});
});


===== ./src/server/env.server.ts =====

import { z } from "zod";

/** Blank values in `.env` (`KEY=`) count as unset. */
const optionalString = z
	.string()
	.optional()
	.transform((value) => value?.trim() || undefined);

const optionalUrl = optionalString.pipe(z.url().optional());

const envSchema = z
	.object({
		NODE_ENV: z.string().optional(),

		REDIS_HOST: z.string().min(1).default("localhost"),
		REDIS_PORT: z.coerce.number().int().min(1).max(65535).default(6379),

		BETTER_AUTH_URL: z.url().default("http://localhost:3000"),
		BETTER_AUTH_SECRET: z.string().min(32),

		// Social sign-in is optional: a provider is enabled when both values are set.
		GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID: optionalString,
		GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET: optionalString,
		FACEBOOK_CLIENT_ID: optionalString,
		FACEBOOK_CLIENT_SECRET: optionalString,

		// Without a key, emails are logged instead of sent (required in production).
		RESEND_API_KEY: optionalString,
		EMAIL_FROM: z.string().min(1).default("2o <onboarding@resend.dev>"),

		/** Public origin used in the sitemap. Falls back to `BETTER_AUTH_URL`. */
		SITE_URL: optionalUrl,

		/** Tawk.to embed URL; the chat widget is hidden when unset. */
		CHAT_EMBED_URL: optionalUrl,
	})
	.refine(
		(e) => Boolean(e.GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID) === Boolean(e.GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET),
		{
			message: "Set both GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID and GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET, or neither",
			path: ["GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID"],
		},
	)
	.refine(
		(e) => Boolean(e.FACEBOOK_CLIENT_ID) === Boolean(e.FACEBOOK_CLIENT_SECRET),
		{
			message:
				"Set both FACEBOOK_CLIENT_ID and FACEBOOK_CLIENT_SECRET, or neither",
			path: ["FACEBOOK_CLIENT_ID"],
		},
	)
	.refine((e) => e.NODE_ENV !== "production" || e.RESEND_API_KEY, {
		message: "RESEND_API_KEY is required in production",
		path: ["RESEND_API_KEY"],
	})
	.transform((e) => ({ ...e, SITE_URL: e.SITE_URL ?? e.BETTER_AUTH_URL }));

/** Parses and validates an environment; exported so the rules can be tested. */
export const parseEnv = (source: Record<string, string | undefined>) =>
	envSchema.parse(source);

/** Validated server environment. Database settings live in `#/db/connection`. */
export const env = parseEnv(process.env);


===== ./src/server/json-cache.server.test.ts =====

import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { z } from "zod";
import { createJsonCache } from "./json-cache.server";

const redis = vi.hoisted(() => ({ get: vi.fn(), set: vi.fn() }));
vi.mock("./redis.server", () => ({ getRedis: async () => redis }));

beforeEach(() => vi.resetAllMocks());

describe("JSON cache", () => {
	it.each([0, false, ""])(
		"serves the cached value %j without recomputing",
		async (value) => {
			redis.get.mockResolvedValue(JSON.stringify(value));
			const compute = vi.fn();
			const cache = createJsonCache({
				key: "test",
				ttlSeconds: 60,
				schema: z.union([z.number(), z.boolean(), z.string()]),
				compute,
			});

			await expect(cache.get()).resolves.toBe(value);
			expect(compute).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
			expect(redis.set).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
		},
	);

	it.each([null, '"old shape"'])(
		"recomputes missing or incompatible cached data (%s)",
		async (value) => {
			redis.get.mockResolvedValue(value);
			const compute = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(42);
			const cache = createJsonCache({
				key: "test",
				ttlSeconds: 60,
				schema: z.number(),
				compute,
			});

			await expect(cache.get()).resolves.toBe(42);
			expect(compute).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
			expect(redis.set).toHaveBeenCalledWith("test", "42", { EX: 60 });
		},
	);
});


===== ./src/server/json-cache.server.ts =====

import type { z } from "zod";
import { getRedis } from "./redis.server";

type JsonCacheOptions<S extends z.ZodType> = {
	key: string;
	ttlSeconds: number;
	/** Validates cached payloads, so a changed shape reads as a miss. */
	schema: S;
	/** Source of truth, used on a miss or when Redis is down. */
	compute: () => Promise<z.output<S>>;
};

/**
 * A Redis-backed JSON cache with a Postgres (or any other) fallback. Reads
 * never throw: an empty, stale-shaped or unreachable cache falls through to
 * `compute`. Returns `get` (cache-aside) and `refresh` (recompute, overwrite).
 */
export function createJsonCache<S extends z.ZodType>({
	key,
	ttlSeconds,
	schema,
	compute,
}: JsonCacheOptions<S>) {
	type Value = z.output<S>;

	async function read(): Promise<Value | null> {
		try {
			const hit = await (await getRedis()).get(key);
			if (!hit) return null;

			const { success, data } = schema.safeParse(JSON.parse(String(hit)));
			return success ? data : null;
		} catch (error) {
			console.warn(`Cache read failed (${key}):`, error);
			return null;
		}
	}

	async function write(value: Value) {
		await (await getRedis()).set(key, JSON.stringify(value), {
			EX: ttlSeconds,
		});
	}

	return {
		async get(): Promise<Value> {
			const cached = await read();
			if (cached !== null) return cached;

			const value = await compute();
			write(value).catch((error) =>
				console.warn(`Cache write failed (${key}):`, error),
			);
			return value;
		},
		async refresh(): Promise<Value> {
			const value = await compute();
			await write(value);
			return value;
		},
	};
}


===== ./src/server/redis.server.test.ts =====

import { EventEmitter } from "node:events";
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";

const { createClient } = vi.hoisted(() => ({ createClient: vi.fn() }));
vi.mock("redis", () => ({ createClient }));
vi.mock("./env.server", () => ({
	env: { REDIS_HOST: "localhost", REDIS_PORT: 6379 },
}));

function connection() {
	return Object.assign(new EventEmitter(), {
		connect: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(undefined),
		isOpen: false,
		destroy: vi.fn(),
	});
}

beforeEach(() => {
	vi.resetModules();
	createClient.mockReset();
});

afterEach(() => vi.restoreAllMocks());

describe("getRedis", () => {
	it("shares a single connection between concurrent callers", async () => {
		const redis = connection();
		createClient.mockReturnValue(redis);
		const { getRedis } = await import("./redis.server");
		expect(createClient).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
		const clients = await Promise.all([getRedis(), getRedis()]);
		expect(clients).toEqual([redis, redis]);
		expect(redis.connect).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
	});

	it("allows another attempt after initialization fails", async () => {
		const failed = connection();
		failed.connect.mockRejectedValue(new Error("unavailable"));
		const recovered = connection();
		createClient.mockReturnValueOnce(failed).mockReturnValueOnce(recovered);
		const { getRedis } = await import("./redis.server");
		await expect(getRedis()).rejects.toThrow("unavailable");
		await expect(getRedis()).resolves.toBe(recovered);
	});

	it.each(["end", "terminated"])(
		"replaces a connection after %s",
		async (event) => {
			const closed = connection();
			const recovered = connection();
			createClient.mockReturnValueOnce(closed).mockReturnValueOnce(recovered);
			const { getRedis } = await import("./redis.server");
			await getRedis();
			closed.emit(event);
			await expect(getRedis()).resolves.toBe(recovered);
		},
	);

	it("does not let a late event from an old client discard its replacement", async () => {
		const closed = connection();
		const recovered = connection();
		createClient.mockReturnValueOnce(closed).mockReturnValueOnce(recovered);
		const { getRedis } = await import("./redis.server");
		await getRedis();
		closed.emit("terminated");
		await expect(getRedis()).resolves.toBe(recovered);
		closed.emit("end");
		await expect(getRedis()).resolves.toBe(recovered);
		expect(createClient).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
	});

	it("keeps a replacement when an older initialization subsequently rejects", async () => {
		const failed = connection();
		const connecting = Promise.withResolvers<void>();
		failed.connect.mockReturnValue(connecting.promise);
		const recovered = connection();
		createClient.mockReturnValueOnce(failed).mockReturnValueOnce(recovered);
		const { getRedis } = await import("./redis.server");
		const pending = getRedis();
		failed.emit("terminated");
		await expect(getRedis()).resolves.toBe(recovered);
		connecting.reject(new Error("unavailable"));
		await expect(pending).rejects.toThrow("unavailable");
		await expect(getRedis()).resolves.toBe(recovered);
		expect(createClient).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
	});

	it("destroys a partially open connection when initialization fails", async () => {
		const failed = connection();
		failed.isOpen = true;
		failed.connect.mockRejectedValue(new Error("handshake failed"));
		createClient.mockReturnValue(failed);
		const { getRedis } = await import("./redis.server");
		await expect(getRedis()).rejects.toThrow("handshake failed");
		expect(failed.destroy).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
	});

	it("uses bounded jittered retries and heartbeats below the socket idle timeout", async () => {
		vi.spyOn(Math, "random").mockReturnValue(0.5);
		createClient.mockReturnValue(connection());
		const { getRedis } = await import("./redis.server");
		await getRedis();
		const options = createClient.mock.calls[0]?.[0];
		expect(options.socket.reconnectStrategy(0)).toBe(200);
		expect(options.socket.reconnectStrategy(4)).toBe(1700);
		expect(options.socket.reconnectStrategy(5)).toBe(false);
		expect(options.pingInterval).toBeLessThan(options.socket.socketTimeout);
		expect(options.disableOfflineQueue).toBe(true);
	});
});


===== ./src/server/redis.server.ts =====

import { createClient } from "redis";
import { env } from "./env.server";

const createRedisClient = () =>
	createClient({
		RESP: 2,
		name: "2o",
		socket: {
			host: env.REDIS_HOST,
			port: env.REDIS_PORT,
			connectTimeout: 1_000,
			// Detect silent sockets, including commands already written to them.
			socketTimeout: 10_000,
			reconnectStrategy: (retries) =>
				retries >= 5
					? false
					: Math.min(100 * 2 ** retries, 2_000) +
						Math.floor(Math.random() * 200),
		},
		// Heartbeats keep healthy idle sockets below socketTimeout.
		pingInterval: 5_000,
		// This deadline applies only while a command is waiting to be sent.
		commandOptions: { timeout: 1_000 },
		disableOfflineQueue: true,
		commandsQueueMaxLength: 1_000,
	});

let client: Promise<ReturnType<typeof createRedisClient>> | undefined;

/** Shared lazy Redis connection; failed initialization is retried on the next call. */
export const getRedis = () => {
	if (client) return client;

	const pending: Promise<ReturnType<typeof createRedisClient>> = (async () => {
		const connection = createRedisClient();
		connection.on("error", (error) =>
			console.warn("Redis client error:", error),
		);
		// A terminal disconnect must not leave a closed client cached forever.
		const reset = () => {
			if (client === pending) client = undefined;
		};
		connection.on("end", reset);
		connection.on("terminated", reset);
		try {
			await connection.connect();
			return connection;
		} catch (error) {
			if (connection.isOpen) connection.destroy();
			throw error;
		}
	})().catch((error: unknown) => {
		if (client === pending) client = undefined;
		throw error;
	});
	client = pending;
	return pending;
};
